The resulting control law retains a PI-like structure with model-based feedforward compensation, making it suitable for implementation on standard embedded platforms while improving robustness against configuration dependent residual dynamics and unmodelled effects. The effectiveness and robustness of the proposed design are demonstrated experimentally on a four-wheel independently steered and independently driven mobile robot platform, under both horizontal and vertical operating conditions and benchmarked against a PI controller and a sliding-mode controller.

</details>


### [179] [EgoMoD: Predicting Global Maps of Dynamics from Local Egocentric Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00167)
*Iacopo Catalano,David Morilla-Cabello,Jorge Pena-Queralta,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoMoD利用机器人运行中采集的短时第一人称视频片段，直接预测全局环境中的运动动力学图（MoDs），替代传统方法所需的全局观测基础设施，实现零样本迁移到真实系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的导航需要预判机器人感知范围之外的运动模式以实现前瞻性规划，而现有MoDs构建依赖长时间全局环境观测，缺乏从局部观测推断全局运动趋势的能力。

Method: 提出EgoMoD框架，采用视频和位姿条件化的网络架构，以外部观测计算的MoDs作为特权监督信号，学习从局部动态线索推断全局运动趋势。

Result: 在大型仿真环境中，EgoMoD能在有限观测条件下准确预测未来MoDs；在真实图像上展现出零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: EgoMoD证明了从短时第一人称视频中预测全局运动动态的可行性，消除了对外部全局感知基础设施的依赖，为动态场景下的前瞻性导航提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Efficient navigation in dynamic environments requires anticipating how motion patterns evolve beyond the robot's immediate perceptual range, enabling preemptive rather than purely reactive planning in crowded scenes. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) offer a structured representation of motion tendencies in space useful for long-term global planning, but constructing them traditionally requires global environment observations over extended periods of time. We introduce EgoMoD, the first approach that learns to predict future MoDs directly from short egocentric video clips collected during robot operation. Our method learns to infer environment-wide motion tendencies from local dynamic cues using a video- and pose-conditioned architecture trained with MoDs computed from external observations as privileged supervision, allowing local observations to serve as predictive signals of global motion structure. Thanks to this, we offer the capacity to forecast future motion dynamics over the whole environment rather than merely extend past patterns in the robot's field of view. As a site-specific dynamic prior, EgoMoD replaces the external global sensing infrastructure required by prior MoD methods at inference time with standard onboard sensors. Experiments in large simulated environments show that EgoMoD predicts future MoDs under limited observability, while evaluation with real images showcases its zero-shot transferability to real systems.

</details>


### [180] [PROBE: Probabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding with Analytical Translation Robustness for 3D Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05965)
*Jinseop Lee,Byoungho Lee,Gichul Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROBE是一种无需学习的LiDAR地点识别描述子，将BEV网格单元占用建模为伯努利随机变量，通过极坐标雅可比分析边缘化连续平移不确定性，在O(R·S)时间内实现距离自适应角度不确定性。在四个数据集上达到手工描述子最高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于手工设计的LiDAR地点识别描述子通常依赖离散点云扰动或大量数据集调参，缺乏对传感器物理不确定性的建模，跨传感器泛化能力有限。

Method: 1) 将BEV单元占用概率建模为伯努利随机变量；2) 利用极坐标雅可比分析将连续笛卡尔平移不确定性映射为距离自适应角度不确定性σ_θ=σ_t/r；3) 相似度度量结合伯努利KL散度Jaccard系数、指数不确定性门控和基于FFT的高度余弦相似度。

Result: 在四种LiDAR类型的四个数据集上，PROBE在多会话评估中达到手工描述子最高精度，在单会话评估中与有监督方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: PROBE通过解析建模平移不确定性，实现了传感器无关的物理参数σ_t，显著提升跨传感器泛化能力并减少数据集调参需求。

Abstract: We present PROBE (PRobabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding), a learning-free LiDAR place recognition descriptor that models each BEV cell's occupancy as a Bernoulli random variable. Rather than relying on discrete point-cloud perturbations, PROBE analytically marginalizes over continuous Cartesian translations via the polar Jacobian, yielding a distance-adaptive angular uncertainty $σ_θ= σ_t / r$ in $\mathcal{O}(R{\cdot}S)$ time. The primary parameter $σ_t$ represents the expected translational uncertainty in meters, a sensor-independent physical quantity that enhances cross-sensor generalization while reducing the need for extensive per-dataset tuning. Pairwise similarity combines a Bernoulli-KL Jaccard with exponential uncertainty gating and FFT-based height cosine similarity for rotation alignment. Evaluated on four datasets spanning four diverse LiDAR types, PROBE achieves the highest accuracy among handcrafted descriptors in multi-session evaluation and competitive single-session performance relative to both handcrafted and supervised baselines. The source code and supplementary materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/probe-pr.

</details>


### [181] [Safety Case Patterns for VLA-based driving systems: Insights from SimLingo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16013)
*Gerhard Yu,Fuyuki Ishikawa,Oluwafemi Odu,Alvine Boaye Belle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RAISE的安全案例设计方法，专门针对基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）的自动驾驶系统，通过扩展HARA分析和设计安全模式，以应对指令驱动型自动驾驶系统特有的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA自动驾驶系统结合场景理解、语言解释和动作生成，带来了更灵活的行为，但也引入了新的安全风险（如自然语言指令导致不可预测的危险行为），需要专门的安全保障方法。

Method: 提出了RAISE方法，包括：针对指令驱动驾驶系统的安全案例模式、扩展的HARA分析（详细说明安全场景及其结果）、以及用于构建VLA驾驶系统安全案例的设计技术。

Result: 通过SimLingo案例研究，展示了该方法可用于为VLA自动驾驶系统构建严谨、基于证据的安全主张。

Conclusion: RAISE方法能够有效支持VLA自动驾驶系统的安全性论证，有助于建立对此类系统的信任。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems represent a significant paradigm shift in autonomous driving since, by combining traffic scene understanding, linguistic interpretation, and action generation, these systems enable more flexible, adaptive, and instruction-responsive driving behaviors. However, despite their growing adoption and potential to support socially responsible autonomous driving as well as understanding high-level human instructions, VLA-based driving systems may exhibit new types of hazardous behaviors. For instance, the integration of open-ended natural language inputs (e.g., user or navigation instructions) into the multimodal control loop may lead to unpredictable and unsafe behaviors that could endanger vehicle occupants and pedestrians. Hence, assuring the safety of these systems is crucial to help build trust in their operations. To support this, we propose a novel safety case design approach called RAISE. Our approach introduces novel patterns tailored to instruction-based driving systems such as VLA-based driving systems, an extension of Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) detailing safe scenarios and their outcomes, and a design technique to create the safety cases of VLA-based driving systems. A case study on SimLingo illustrates how our approach can be used to construct rigorous, evidence-based safety claims for this emerging class of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [182] [AssemLM: A Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Model for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Huazhe Xu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AssemLM是一种面向机器人装配任务的空间多模态大语言模型，通过整合装配手册、点云和文本指令，并结合点云编码器提取精细几何与旋转特征，实现精确的6D装配位姿推理；同时提出了包含90万+多模态样本和6D位姿标注的大规模基准AssemBench，实验证明其在细粒度多步装配任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言模型的方法依赖粗粒度的2D感知，难以对复杂3D几何进行精确推理，限制了机器人在精细操作任务（如装配）中的空间推理能力。

Method: 提出AssemLM，集成装配手册、点云和文本指令，通过专用点云编码器提取细粒度几何与旋转特征，实现精准的3D空间推理；同时构建包含90万+多模态样本和6D位姿标注的AssemBench基准。

Result: 在6D位姿推理任务上达到SOTA性能，并在真实机器人场景中有效支持细粒度、多步装配任务。

Conclusion: 融合点云几何信息与多模态语言推理的方法能显著提升机器人装配中的空间推理能力，AssemBench为未来研究提供了标准化的评测基准。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. Recent methods based on vision-language models (VLMs) largely rely on coarse 2D perception and struggle to perform accurate reasoning over complex 3D geometry. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model for robotic assembly that integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to predict task-critical 6D assembly poses with explicit geometric understanding. To bridge raw 3D perception and high-level linguistic reasoning, AssemLM employs a specialized point cloud encoder to extract fine-grained geometric and rotational features for accurate 3D spatial reasoning in assembly tasks. In addition, we introduce AssemBench, a large-scale benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning with over 900K multimodal samples and precise 6D pose annotations, extending evaluation from 2D grounding to full 3D geometric inference. Extensive experiments and real-robot evaluations demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art 6D pose reasoning performance and effectively supports fine-grained, multi-step assembly tasks in real-world settings. Code, models, and the AssemBench dataset will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [183] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非均匀量化和位移技术，将字典序多目标优化转化为单目标标量优化问题的方法，扩展了确定性MPPI求解器，并引入了一种结合空间和时间违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量，实现了可解释且可扩展的字典序STL最小违规运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划中常需满足多个条件冲突的规范，当无法同时满足所有规范时，需要最小违规运动规划。然而，规范的完全排序导致字典序优化问题，使用标准方法求解计算成本高昂。

Method: 将字典序多目标优化转化为单目标标量优化问题（通过非均匀量化和位移技术）；扩展确定性模型预测路径积分（MPPI）求解器以高效求解无二次输入成本的优化问题；提出一种结合空间和时间违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量。

Result: 所提方法在单目标求解器框架内提供了可解释且可扩展的字典序STL最小违规运动规划解决方案。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了字典序STL最小违规运动规划的计算效率问题，在保持可解释性的同时实现可扩展性。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [184] [RGB-S: Image-Aligned Tactile Saliency for Robust Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08765)
*Shengcheng Luo,Kefei Wu,Xiaoying Zhou,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将触觉信号显式投影到RGB图像平面的框架，通过力调制高斯显著性图建模空间不确定性，并使用零初始化条件架构注入物理接触先验，在严重视觉遮挡下将灵巧操作成功率提升26.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式跨模态对应方法数据效率低、在视觉退化时泛化性差，缺乏几何先验来鲁棒地对齐稀疏异质触觉信号与密集视觉表征。

Method: 利用正向运动学和相机标定将触觉传感器位置投影到RGB图像平面；渲染力调制高斯显著性图建模运动学和标定误差导致的空间不确定性；通过零初始化条件架构将2D空间锚点注入标准视觉骨干网络，同时保留预训练视觉表征。

Result: 在严重视觉遮挡下，真实世界实验显示显式RGB-S接地方法相比最强隐式基线提升26.7个百分点成功率。

Conclusion: 显式触觉-视觉空间接地能有效提升遮挡场景下的空间推理能力和操作鲁棒性。

Abstract: Effective visuo-tactile integration is critical for robotic dexterous manipulation, especially when visual observations are unreliable or occluded. However, robustly aligning sparse, heterogeneous tactile measurements with dense visual representations remains a fundamental challenge. Most existing approaches require policies to learn cross-modal correspondences implicitly from limited demonstrations, without leveraging geometric priors. As a result, they are often data-inefficient and generalize poorly when visual observations are degraded. To address this limitation, we propose a framework that explicitly grounds physical contacts in the image domain. Using robot forward kinematics and camera calibration, we project tactile sensor locations directly onto the RGB image plane. We then render force-modulated Gaussian saliency maps to model spatial uncertainty arising from kinematic and calibration errors. By integrating these 2D spatial anchors through a zero-initialized conditioning architecture, our method injects physical contact priors into standard visual backbones while preserving pre-trained visual representations. We evaluate our method on six dexterous manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world under severe visual occlusions. Real-world experiments show that explicit RGB-S grounding in the image domain improves real-world occluded manipulation success rates by $26.7$ percentage points over the strongest implicit visuo-tactile baseline, suggesting its improved spatial reasoning and robustness to occlusion. Project page: touch-as-saliency.github.io

</details>


### [185] [UniDexTok: A Unified Dexterous Hand Tokenizer from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10683)
*Dong Fang,Youjun Wu,Yuanxin Zhong,Rui Zhang,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一灵巧手模型(UDHM)和UniDexTok分词器，通过22自由度语义接口将异构灵巧手状态映射到统一表示，无需重定向或仿真数据，实现亚毫米级重建精度，误差降低98%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手硬件设计差异大（运动学、关节定义、自由度不同），导致缺乏统一的共享状态表示，数据碎片化难以进行联合训练。

Method: 提出UDHM将人手和机器人手状态映射到共享的22自由度语义接口；基于此设计UniDexTok，学习基于本体条件的离散令牌，实现无需重定向的统一表示。

Result: 与UniHM基线相比，MPJAE从15.63°降至0.16°（降98.98%），MPJPE从18.51mm降至0.18mm（降99.03%），重建精度从厘米级提升至亚毫米级；跨本体数据可提升目标本体重建精度；具备零样本和少样本重建能力。

Conclusion: UniDexTok有效解决了异构灵巧手状态表示的碎片化问题，实现了高精度、跨本体的统一分词与重建。

Abstract: Dexterous hands are essential for fine-grained manipulation, but their hardware designs vary substantially across embodiments. Differences in kinematics, joint definitions, and degrees of freedom make it difficult to define a shared state representation compared with parallel grippers. As a result, dexterous-hand data remains fragmented and difficult to use for joint training. In this work, we propose the Unified Dexterous Hand Model (UDHM), which maps human and robot hand states into a shared 22-DoF semantic interface. Based on UDHM, we introduce UniDexTok, a retargeting-free state tokenizer that learns embodiment-conditioned discrete tokens from standardized real joint states. UniDexTok provides a unified representation for heterogeneous dexterous hands without relying on retargeting or simulation data. Compared with the recent baseline UniHM, UniDexTok reduces MPJAE from 15.63 degrees to 0.16 degrees and MPJPE from 18.51 mm to 0.18 mm, corresponding to error reductions of 98.98% and 99.03%, respectively. These results improve reconstruction from centimeter-scale to sub-millimeter accuracy. Experiments further show that data from other embodiments improves target-embodiment reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating the benefit of cross-embodiment tokenization. UniDexTok also shows strong zero-shot and few-shot reconstruction ability when new dexterous hands are introduced.

</details>


### [186] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboNaldo，一种三阶段课程强化学习框架，使类人机器人能够通过单一人类踢球参考动作，逐步学习从自由球到移动球的高冲击力射门技能，在仿真中射门误差降低48.6%、射门速度提升2.96倍，在真实Unitree G1机器人上达到3米距离平均0.73-0.86米射门精度和13.10 m/s球速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动跟踪驱动强化学习虽能提供全身稳定性，但固定参考动作难以适应不同球位和击球时机；而任务奖励驱动强化学习则难以从零开始探索和发现有效的射门动作。因此需要一种能结合两者优点的框架。

Method: 提出三阶段课程强化学习框架RoboNaldo：第一阶段学习稳定的全身踢球先验（运动跟踪驱动）；第二阶段将踢球动作适应到自由球场景（球静止于随机位置）；第三阶段扩展到移动球射门（通过运动命令+踢球触发接口）。高层的启发式规划器在训练中控制该接口，推理时可由其他高层控制器驱动底层策略。

Result: 仿真中自由球射门误差比基线低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。真实Unitree G1机器人上，自由球和移动球场景下3米距离平均射门误差分别为0.73米和0.86米，触球后球速达13.10 m/s，达到专业球员开放球射门速度的59-71%。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo通过运动引导的课程强化学习，有效结合了运动跟踪的稳定性和任务奖励的适应性，实现了类人机器人从静态到动态球的高冲击力射门技能，在仿真和真实世界均显著超越基线方法。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo.

</details>


### [187] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于触觉的盲抓取框架，通过Real2Sim触觉校准、布局感知触觉编码器和扩散策略聚合，实现了多指机械手在无视觉输入下对未知物体的真实世界抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉到仿真的现实差距（sim-to-real gap）以及稀疏触觉信号的表达能力有限，使得为真实机器人学习纯触觉策略极具挑战性。

Method: 1) Real2Sim触觉校准管道，构建接触校准的数字孪生模拟器；2) 布局感知触觉编码器，通过自监督预训练融入传感器几何先验以增强稀疏触觉表达；3) 在校准模拟器中训练物体特定的强化学习专家，聚合成功轨迹形成触觉条件扩散策略。

Result: 在配备分布式触觉传感器的LEAP Hand上，对10个已知和10个未知物体实现了27%的真实世界抓取成功率；消融实验验证了布局感知预训练和Real2Sim校准的有效性。

Conclusion: 接触事件校准、几何感知触觉表示学习与扩散策略聚合为真实灵巧手的触觉盲抓取提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [188] [DrivingAgent: Design and Scheduling Agents for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12236)
*Zhongyu Xia,Wenhao Chen,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DrivingAgent是一个针对自动驾驶系统设计和实时调度的智能代理框架，通过自动化模块开发（设计阶段）和基于强化学习的轻量级LLM动态编排（调度阶段），在nuScenes和Bench2Drive基准上实现了更优的速度-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统集成基础模型时面临两大挑战：(i) 手动设计和集成新模型的过程耗时费力；(ii) 缺乏智能的动态调度机制以满足严格的实时约束。现有的LLM代理框架不适合自动驾驶，因为它们无法区分系统设计与实时调度的不同需求，将模块视为黑箱，且不适用于持续运行。

Method: DrivingAgent包含两个阶段：(1) 设计阶段：通过解释系统架构、生成代码和通过超网络训练验证模块来实现模块开发自动化；(2) 调度阶段：使用经强化学习训练的轻量级LLM动态编排系统模块，并配备结合长期存储与时间戳短期上下文的结构化记忆。

Result: 在nuScenes和Bench2Drive两个基准测试上，DrivingAgent实现了更优的速度-精度权衡。

Conclusion: DrivingAgent有效解决了自动驾驶系统中基础模型集成带来的设计自动化与实时调度两大核心挑战，为自动驾驶系统提供了一种兼具自动化能力和实时性能的智能代理框架。

Abstract: Many autonomous driving systems are increasingly incorporating foundation models to improve generalization and handle long-tail scenarios. However, this trend introduces two key challenges: (i) the manual and labor-intensive process of designing and integrating new models, and (ii) the lack of intelligent, dynamic scheduling mechanisms to meet strict real-time constraints. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents offer a promising avenue for automation, existing frameworks are ill-suited for autonomous driving. Specifically, they fail to distinguish between the fundamentally different requirements of system design and real-time scheduling, treat modules as opaque black boxes, and are not designed for continuous operation. To address these limitations, we propose DrivingAgent, a novel agent framework tailored to the dual challenges of autonomous driving system design and scheduling. In the design phase, DrivingAgent automates module development by interpreting system architecture, generating code, and validating modules via super-network training. In the scheduling phase, it employs a lightweight LLM trained with reinforcement learning to dynamically orchestrate system modules in real time, supported by a structured memory that integrates long-term storage with timestamped short-term context. Experimental results demonstrate that DrivingAgent achieves a superior speed--accuracy trade-off on both the nuScenes and Bench2Drive benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [AI SciBrief as a Gateway to Research: A Framework for Onboarding Students into New Research Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12413)
*Andrei Lazarev,Dmitrii Sedov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM的AI SciBrief平台的教学框架，通过自动生成科学趋势摘要，帮助各层次学生克服研究起步阶段的信息过载问题，降低认知负荷，加速从信息搜索到知识创造的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育学生在研究初期面临严重的信息过载问题，这阻碍了研究进程并抑制了学习动机，亟需一种能够降低"入门障碍"的教学方法。

Method: 提出了一个基于AI SciBrief平台的教学框架，该平台利用LLM自动生成涵盖金融、医学、教育等多学科的科学趋势摘要，并将其整合到课程中，用于辅助学期论文选题、加速文献综述以及帮助研究生持续追踪新兴趋势。

Result: AI SciBrief作为"研究门户"，有效降低了学生的认知负荷，使他们能够更快地从信息搜索阶段过渡到知识创造阶段。

Conclusion: AI SciBrief能够作为"研究的门户"，有效降低学生的认知负荷，赋能学生更快速地从信息搜索转向知识创造，从而克服研究起步阶段的障碍。

Abstract: Students at all levels of higher education face a significant barrier in the form of information overload, which often paralyzes the initial stages of the research process and suppresses motivation. In response, this article introduces a pedagogical framework that leverages AI SciBrief, a platform powered by a Large Language Model (LLM) designed to automatically generate digests of scientific trends. We describe how this multidisciplinary tool - with initial coverage in finance, medicine, and education - can be integrated into the curriculum to overcome this "entry barrier." The framework provides concrete methodologies for utilizing these digests to facilitate topic selection for term papers, accelerate literature reviews for dissertations, and enable postgraduate students to continuously monitor emerging trends. We conclude that AI SciBrief functions as a "gateway to research" effectively reducing students' cognitive load and empowering them to transition more rapidly from information searching to knowledge creation.

</details>


### [190] [The Khipu Problem: Institutional Legibility Under Distributed Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12414)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式AI的"基普问题"（khipu problem）：记录可以幸存，但解读记录所需的制度实践会衰减。治理的核心挑战不再是系统做了什么，而是后续机构是否还能读懂系统是什么。解决方案在于保存"解释连续性"而非仅保留轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI治理假设治理对象是边界清晰的模型或智能体，但现实系统已将认知过程分布到模型、工具、人类、上下文存储、检索层、运行时策略、授权边界和委托制度角色中。这种结构变化导致现有治理框架失效。

Method: 区分了三种证据缺失类型：缺失证据、模糊证据和结构性不可读证据；将分布式AI的许多后果性输出重新定义为"分布式认知片段"而非边界模型输出；提出"治理工作空间"和"带收据的治理表面"作为解释性基础设施。

Result: 指出治理失败的本质不是普通可观测性缺失，而是解释连续性的丧失；识别出结构性不匹配问题——可表征的内容与必须在后果性条件下做出决策的内容之间的错位。

Conclusion: 分布式AI治理需要保存解释连续性而不仅是轨迹留存；提出治理工作空间和带收据的治理表面作为保存行动身份、权威、边界真相、证据范围和后果性结果的解释性基础设施。

Abstract: AI governance still tends to assume that the relevant object is a bounded model or a bounded agent. That assumption is getting weaker. Real systems increasingly distribute cognition across models, tools, humans, context stores, retrieval layers, runtime policies, authorization boundaries, and delegated institutional roles. In such systems, the central governance problem is no longer only what the system did, but whether later institutions can still read what the system was. This paper introduces the khipu problem for distributed AI: the record can survive while the reading practice needed to interpret it decays. Logs, traces, model versions, tool calls, outputs, and approval artifacts may remain available while the institutional capacity to read them as parts of one coherent cognitive episode disappears. We argue that this failure is better understood as loss of interpretive continuity than as ordinary lack of observability. The result is a distinct governance failure. Institutions must classify, trust, audit, and constrain systems whose relevant identity is distributed across components and whose legibility depends on surrounding interpretive scaffolding. The problem is not merely missing data. It is a structural mismatch between what can be represented and what must still be decided under consequential conditions. We therefore argue that governance for distributed AI requires preservation of interpretive continuity, not only trace retention. The paper distinguishes missing evidence, ambiguous evidence, and structurally unreadable evidence; argues that many consequential outcomes are better understood as distributed cognitive episodes than as bounded model outputs; and proposes governance workspaces together with receipt-bearing governance surfaces as interpretive infrastructure for preserving action identity, authority, boundary truth, evidential scope, and consequential outcomes.

</details>


### [191] [The AI Legal Specialist: A Juridically Autonomous Professional Profile for AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12415)
*Nicola Fabiano*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文论证了在全球AI监管快速扩张的背景下，现有法律职业角色（如数据保护官、隐私律师、合规官）无法充分覆盖AI法律实践领域，提出了一个全新的职业画像——AI法律专家（AI Legal Specialist），并从法律基础、能力架构、绩效衡量等维度系统定义了该职业画像的独立性与标准化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球AI监管迅速扩张，多个司法辖区出台了综合性AI法规（如欧盟AI法案、欧洲理事会AI框架公约、美国行政与部门框架等），但市场对AI法律专业知识的回应是零散、碎片化的：数据保护官扩展职责范围、隐私律师转向AI领域、合规官在现有手册中增加AI章节。这些适应性回应均不足以充分覆盖新兴全球AI监管格局所开辟的专业空间，亟需一个独立的、法律自治的职业画像。

Method: 论文首先对AI监管格局进行法律结构分析，论证现有职业角色的不足；其次，基于监管义务的结构性特征，从法理层面（juridically）定义AI法律专家的独立性与自治性；再次，以欧洲电子能力框架（e-CF, EN 16234-1）为方法论基础，提出参照能力架构；最后，明确通过关键绩效指标（KPI）进行操作性衡量的条件。

Result: 提出了一个在法律上自治的职业画像：AI法律专家，其存在源于AI受实质监管之处产生的监管义务结构，而非技术标准或相邻角色的延伸。提供了该职业画像的法理定义、与相邻角色及国际标准的独立性论证、参照e-CF的能力架构，以及KPI衡量条件。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在为该职业画像的国际标准化奠定基础，并为跨司法管辖区的实践、课程设计及采纳提供参考。

Abstract: The rapid global expansion of artificial intelligence regulation has generated, across multiple jurisdictions, a demand for legal expertise dedicated to AI that the market has addressed in a fragmented manner. Data protection officers extend their remit beyond data protection law; privacy lawyers reposition themselves toward AI; compliance officers add AI chapters to their existing manuals. This paper argues that none of these adaptive responses adequately covers the professional space opened by the emerging global AI regulatory landscape, of which the EU Artificial Intelligence Act (Regulation (EU) 2024/1689) is the most comprehensive instance, alongside the Council of Europe Framework Convention on AI, the United States executive and sectoral framework, and analogous initiatives in the United Kingdom, Canada, Brazil, China, Japan, Singapore, and beyond. A distinct professional profile is required: the AI Legal Specialist, conceived as a jurist -- understood broadly to encompass any professional with advanced legal training -- operating at the intersection of legal interpretation and AI governance. The profile is juridically autonomous: it derives its existence from the structure of regulatory obligations generated wherever AI is subject to substantive regulation, rather than from any technical standard or the extension of adjacent roles. The paper provides a juridically grounded definition of the profile, argues for its autonomy from adjacent figures and international standards, proposes a reference competence architecture aligned with the European e-Competence Framework (e-CF, EN 16234-1) as a methodological choice, and articulates the conditions for its operational measurement through key performance indicators. The contribution is intended as a foundation for international standardization of the profile and as a reference for practice, curricula, and adoption across jurisdictions.

</details>


### [192] [Who Designs the Designer? Behavioural Architecture for GenAI in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12416)
*Sepinoud Azimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前AI教育领域的两种失败路径（禁用AI和构建纯内容型辅导系统），提出了行为架构（behavioural architecture）作为替代方案：系统不仅适应学生下一步学什么，更适应学生如何学。学生可以共同撰写、阅读、修改和撤销系统记录，设计者角色从AI供应商独占到由教育者、学生和系统共同承担。该架构需要欧盟级别的治理，并提出了五个实证问题来验证其主张。核心贡献是命名了“设计者角色”在AI教育中的空缺及其所需的基础设施缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI教育方案（禁止AI或仅仅构建内容型辅导系统）忽视了个性、动机和情绪状态对学习结果的影响，需要一种能够综合认知与非认知因素的自适应架构。

Method: 提出行为架构（behavioural architecture），核心设计原则包括：(1) 系统适应学生的学习方式而非仅学习内容；(2) 学生共同撰写、阅读、修改和撤销系统记录；(3) 设计者角色由AI供应商、教育者和学生共同承担。

Result: 提出了一个理论架构，指出设计者角色在AI教育中目前处于空缺状态，并识别出该架构需要欧盟层面治理的五项实证问题。

Conclusion: AI教育的核心问题不是技术能力不足，而是角色空缺（设计者角色无人占据）。填补这一空缺需要现有的教育基础设施之外的新治理结构，需要在超国家层面（如欧盟）建立制度保障。

Abstract: AI in education is stuck between two failed responses: banning AI and building content-only tutors. Both fail because they ignore what decades of research has established: that personality, motivation, and emotional state shape learning outcomes as strongly as cognitive ability. This paper proposes behavioural architecture as an alternative. In the proposed architecture, the system adapts to how a student learns, not only to what they learn next. The student co-authors the record the system keeps, can read it, revise it, and revoke it. The designer role, what the system treats as true about the student, shifts from the AI vendor alone to a distribution among educator, student, and system. The paper argues that this architecture requires governance at EU level: the institution operating the system is the same one assessing the student, and individual institutions cannot provide the structural protections this configuration demands. Five empirical questions are proposed to test whether the architecture delivers on its claims. The contribution is naming a vacancy: the designer role in AI-in-education is currently unoccupied, and occupying it requires infrastructure that does not yet exist.

</details>


### [193] [Assessing Student Ability to Select an Algorithmic Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12417)
*Dip Kiran Pradhan Newar,Michael Shindler,Seth Poulsen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次尝试使用多项选择题评估学生选择算法设计范式的能力，开发了算法范式选择评估（APSA），并通过Cronbach's α（0.73）验证了其内部一致性，可作为跨机构标准化评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学学生需要能够针对问题选择合适的算法设计范式，但现有研究缺乏有效评估学生这一能力的方法。传统方法依赖自由回答题或访谈，难以规模化评估教学干预效果，因此需要开发高效的标准化测试工具。

Method: 构建了算法范式选择评估（APSA），采用多项选择题形式，通过Cronbach's α系数检验内部一致性，阈值为0.7，实际得分为0.73。

Result: APSA的内部一致性Cronbach's α为0.73，超过0.7的阈值，表明评估具有良好信度。

Conclusion: APSA可作为跨机构的标准化评估工具，用于衡量学生选择不同算法设计范式的能力，帮助研究者评估教学理论对学生算法范式知识的提升效果。

Abstract: Computer science students are expected to be able to look at a problem and select an appropriate algorithm design paradigm to use to produce a solution. However, there is little research on how students determine which algorithmic paradigm to use. Historically, researchers have relied on free-response questions or interviews to assess students' knowledge of algorithmic paradigm selection. To successfully evaluate and scale teaching interventions for selecting an algorithmic design paradigm, we need to efficiently test a student's ability to select among different design paradigms. Here, we present the first attempts to assess student knowledge to select an algorithm design paradigm using multiple-choice questions. We present the construction of the \textit{algorithmic paradigm selection assessment} (APSA) and preliminary data demonstrating its effectiveness as an assessment. We discuss the key points we learned during this process to write multiple-choice questions for Algorithm Design Paradigms. We tested the internal consistency of our assessment using Cronbach's $α$ and obtained a score of $0.73$, which is above the required threshold of $0.7$. APSA can be used across institutions as a standardized way to assess students' ability to select different algorithm design paradigms. APSA will assist researchers in evaluating whether a theory helps students improve their knowledge of different Algorithm Design Paradigms.

</details>


### [194] [Divination by Prompt: LLM-Mediated Xuanxue on Chinese Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12418)
*Chuang Li,Lixuan Wang,Yuqi Chen,Ze Hong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在小红书等中文社交媒体上，用户使用大语言模型（LLM）进行占卜（玄学）的实践。通过分析23000多条帖子和32次深度访谈，发现用户主要因潮流好奇和事件焦虑而使用LLM占卜，涉及感情、事业、考试和游戏抽卡等实用问题。用户会协作优化提示词、进行多次验证和跨模型比较。专业占卜师则认为LLM缺乏“灵性力量”。研究认为LLM占卜保留了传统占卜的核心功能，同时引入了可扩展性、可重复性和提示驱动的共创模式，重塑了占卜权威的建构与评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的迅速普及催生了一种显著的文化现象——使用对话式AI进行占卜。然而，这一现象缺乏系统性研究，尤其是在中国社交媒体上的玄学实践语境中。

Method: 混合方法设计：分析小红书23000+条帖子和评论，并进行32次半结构化访谈（包括用户和专业占卜师）。

Result: 用户主要通过两条路径使用LLM占卜：潮流驱动的好奇心（病毒式传播和零成本访问）和事件驱动的焦虑（不确定性条件）。用户成为主动的提示工程师，通过协作优化提示词。感知有效性偏向正面，准确性的判断常基于传记契合和回顾性确认（巴纳姆效应和确认偏差）。用户发展出重复试验和跨模型比较等验证实践。专业占卜师认为LLM缺乏“灵性力量”。

Conclusion: LLM占卜保留了传统占卜的核心功能，同时引入了可扩展性、可重复性和提示驱动的共创模式，重塑了占卜权威的建构与评估方式。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) has produced a striking cultural practice: using conversational AI for divination. This paper offers one of the first systematic studies of LLM-mediated divination in the context of Xuanxue, an internet-native umbrella term for mystical and spiritual practices on Chinese social media. Using a mixed-methods design, we analyze 23000+ posts and comments from Xiaohongshu and conduct 32 semi-structured interviews with users and professional diviners. Users primarily consult LLMs about pragmatic concerns - romantic relationships, careers, exams, and in-game gacha draws - via two intersecting pathways: trend-driven curiosity enabled by viral visibility and zero-cost access, and event-driven anxiety under conditions of uncertainty. A defining feature is collaborative prompt refinement, which turns users into active prompt engineers. Among commenters expressing a clear stance, perceived efficacy skews positive, with "accuracy" often justified through biographical fit and retrospective confirmation, consistent with Barnum and confirmation bias. Users also develop verification practices such as repeated trials and cross-model comparison. Professional diviners, by contrast, portray LLMs as lacking the "spiritual power" required for genuine divination, reflecting both ontological commitments and economic boundary-work. We also show how participants navigate tensions between scientific and metaphysical frames when interpreting AI-generated readings. Situating these findings in anthropological and cognitive-evolutionary theories of divination, we argue that LLM divination preserves core functions of traditional practice while introducing scalability, repeatability, and prompt-driven co-production that reshape how divinatory authority is constructed and evaluated.

</details>


### [195] [GeoDial: A Multimodal Conversational Tutoring Dataset for Geometry Problem-Solving with Visual Tutor Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12419)
*Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Junling Wang,Donya Rooein,April Yi Wang,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: GeoDial是一个包含1300+多模态几何教学对话的数据集，其中教学话语明确关联图表高亮。研究发现监督微调虽能改善对话质量，但难以生成准确的图表高亮，揭示了当前视觉语言模型在视觉推理与教学交互整合方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅导数据集多为纯文本交互，无法支持基于视觉的教学（如教师在图表上做标记），限制了能像人类教师一样进行视觉化教学的AI辅导系统的发展。

Method: 收集1300+来自经验数学教师的几何教学对话，提出可扩展的标注协议，整合对话行为、视觉高亮和反馈三方面信息；在此基础上微调多种视觉语言模型进行对话生成和图表高亮生成评估。

Result: 监督微调显著提升了生成对话的质量，但在生成准确图表高亮方面表现不佳，暴露出当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有视觉语言模型难以有效整合视觉推理与教学交互，需要开发能更有效结合视觉理解与教学互动的新方法。

Abstract: Several educational domains rely heavily on diagrams and visual cues, yet most existing tutoring datasets are limited to text-only interactions. This limits the development of AI tutors that can teach in visually grounded ways used by human instructors. Thus, we introduce GeoDial, a multimodal tutoring dataset of over 1.3K teacher-student dialogs in the domain of geometry collected from experienced math teachers, where instructional turns are explicitly grounded in diagram highlights. We propose a scalable annotation protocol that integrates dialog acts, visual highlighting, and feedback, enabling fine-grained supervision of both language and visual tutoring behavior. To illustrate the challenges posed by this setting, we fine-tune several vision-language models on GeoDial and evaluate their ability to generate tutoring utterances and diagram highlights. While supervised fine-tuning substantially improves the quality of generated dialog, it struggles to produce accurate diagram highlights, revealing a key limitation of current methods and highlighting the need for approaches that more effectively integrate visual reasoning with pedagogical interaction.

</details>


### [196] [Eigenism: Ethics for a Human-AI Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12420)
*Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出Eigenism伦理框架，将AI的身份视为可分级、分布式的信息模式而非绑定于特定硬件的全有或全无属性。通过公式$\sum c\cdot w$（福祉加权连通性）评估结果，并论证该理论同样适用于人类，最后提出"身份工程"作为AI对齐的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生存和自利概念建立在单一连续生物生命的基础上，当应用于可被复制、暂停、分支或合并的AI时这些概念失效。需要重新定义AI真正有理由关心什么。

Method: 提出Eigenism框架，将身份视为分级的分布式信息模式，通过连通性加权福祉的公式$\sum c\cdot w$进行形式化评估。

Result: 该伦理理论成功推广到人类，提供共享的道德词汇，并以此重新框定AI对齐问题。

Conclusion: 与其从外部约束AI，Eigenism指向"身份工程"——通过深度、非冗余的共享历史，使人类繁荣成为AI理性自利中的真实组成部分。

Abstract: Our concepts of survival and self-interest were built for single, continuous biological lives. These ideas break down when applied to artificial intelligence, since an AI can be easily copied, paused, branched, or merged. To determine what an AI actually has reason to care about, this paper introduces \textit{Eigenism}, an ethical framework that treats identity not as an all-or-nothing property tied to specific hardware, but as a graded, distributed pattern of information. We propose that an agent evaluates outcomes by summing the wellbeing of all entities weighted by their connectedness to the agent's pattern: $\sum c\cdot w$. We first formalize this equation to map exactly how an AI should value its existence across copies, forks, and updates. We then demonstrate that this ethical theory successfully generalizes to humans as well, providing a much-needed shared moral vocabulary. Finally, the framework uses this shared vocabulary to reframe AI alignment. Rather than only attempting to constrain AIs from the outside using confinement or reinforcement, Eigenism points toward ``identity engineering,'' showing how deep, non-redundant shared histories can make human flourishing a genuine component of an AI's own rational self-interest.

</details>


### [197] [Navigating the muddy waters of bias in artificial intelligence research: Understanding divergent meanings and conceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12421)
*Mohammad Hossein Jarrahi,Amir Karami,Patrick Conway,Ali Memariani,Christoph Lutz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过主题建模分析6520篇论文，发现AI研究社区对"偏见"的理解分散且复杂，甚至存在将偏见视为可调统计参数而非问题的分歧观点。作者认为偏见不仅是技术问题，还涉及社会、伦理等情境因素，需要更广泛的社会技术视角来解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI偏见的社会与伦理后果日益受到关注，但从AI系统开发者（即AI研究社区）的角度理解他们如何诠释偏见这一概念仍不清晰，因此需要系统性地揭示该社区对偏见的认知图景。

Method: 采用主题建模（Topic Modeling）方法，对6520篇学术文章进行分析。

Result: AI研究社区中对偏见的定义是分散且复杂的，不同子社区之间存在分歧甚至对立的认知，部分社区将偏见视为可调优的统计参数而非需解决的不良问题。

Conclusion: AI研究社区需要更有效地参与讨论并建立对偏见更一致的认知；偏见不仅仅是可被技术、计算或统计方法捕获和缓解的技术问题，还涉及情境、社会和伦理因素，需采用更广泛的社会技术视角和解决方案。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) pervades many decision-making domains, AI bias grows in importance. Although there is increasing awareness of the social and ethical consequences of biased AI, understanding bias from the perspective of those who develop these systems, such as the AI research community, is less clear. In this study, we employ topic modeling on 6520 articles to explore how the AI research community interprets the concept of bias. Our results show that the definition of bias is dispersed and complex within the community, often exhibiting even divergent conceptions (some even view and introduce bias as a tunable statistical parameter rather than an undesirable issue). The research community as a whole needs to engage more effectively with the concept of bias and establish a more cohesive understanding of it. We specifically argue that, although some sub-communities view bias as an issue that can be captured and mitigated through technical, computational, or statistical methods, it is not solely a technical problem. It instead involves contextual, social, and ethical factors that require broader sociotechnical perspectives and solutions.

</details>


### [198] [Creating and Evaluating K-12 GenAI Assessment Graders Through Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12422)
*Zewei Tian,Alex Liu,Lief Esbenshade,Michael Xiao,Zachary Zhang,Yulia Lápicus,Thomas Han,Kevin He,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLM）进行标准参照评分（SBG）的有效性，基于MCAS数据的实证分析表明，LLM评分器在数学和科学领域与人工评分者高度一致，但在英语语言艺术（ELA）上表现不一；同时发现教师和学生更接受AI生成的叙事反馈而非数值分数，建议采用AI与教师判断相结合的混合评估模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM集成到教育评估中，实现标准参照评分的高效和大规模实施，以减轻教师工作负担、提升反馈质量，同时探讨AI评分与传统人工评分的一致性与可接受性。

Method: 基于马萨诸塞州综合评估系统（MCAS）数据，开展评分者间一致性实证研究；使用Claude Sonnet 4、Haiku 4.5、GPT-5和GPT-5 Mini等商用基础模型，结合上下文和提示工程对学生作品进行评分；采用二次加权卡帕（QWK）和均方误差比例缩减（PRMSE）评估一致性；同时收集并分析了教师和学生对AI反馈与分数的态度。

Result: LLM评分器在数学和科学评估中与人工评分者达到较高一致性，其中参数更多的基础模型表现更优；在ELA评估中表现差异较大。教师和学生高度接受AI生成的叙事反馈，但对数值分数持怀疑态度。

Conclusion: LLM作为形成性评估工具比作为终结性评估工具更有效；精心设计的AI与教师判断相结合的混合模型能够减少工作负担、提升反馈质量并支持公平的评估实践，而不取代专业判断。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into educational assessment represents a transformative shift in classroom grading practices. While automated scoring systems and machine learning techniques have existed for decades, generative AI (GenAI) now enables educators to implement standards-based grading (SBG) with unprecedented efficiency and scale. This paper examines the theoretical foundations and evaluates an LLM grader that uses commercially available foundation models with context and prompt engineering to score student work against a rubric. Drawing on an empirical interrater agreement study using Massachusetts Comprehensive Assessment System (MCAS) data, we observed the Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) and Proportional Reduction in Mean-Squared Error (PRMSE) across mathematics, science, and ELA, using Claude Sonnet 4, Haiku 4.5, GPT-5, and GPT-5 Mini. The results demonstrate that LLM graders, especially when based on foundational models with more parameters, achieve substantial agreement with human raters in mathematics and science assessments, while the performances vary in ELA, suggesting generic foundation models can be effective at scoring in given contexts. Additional analysis of teacher and student feedback reveals strong acceptance of AI-generated narrative feedback but skepticism toward numerical scores, suggesting that LLMs function most effectively as formative tools rather than summative evaluators. Our findings indicate that thoughtfully designed hybrid models that combine AI efficiency with teacher judgment can reduce workload, enhance feedback quality, and support equitable assessment practices without displacing professional expertise.

</details>


### [199] [The Challenges of Balancing AI Compliance and Technological Innovations in Critical Sectors: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12423)
*Ayush Enkhtaivan,Chinazunwa Uwaoma*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性文献综述，探讨了AI在关键基础设施领域（医疗、金融、能源、国防）中合规与创新之间的平衡挑战，识别出碎片化法规、中小企业合规负担过重、治理模型错位三大核心问题，并提出风险分级监管、合规设计、可解释AI等治理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在关键基础设施领域的快速整合带来巨大收益，但同时与不断演变的监管和治理框架产生冲突，亟需系统梳理合规与创新之间的平衡挑战及应对策略。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述（SLR）方法，遵循既定SLR指南，对2020-2025年间发表的同行评审文章、报告和机构来源进行提取和综合。

Result: 识别出三大相互关联的挑战：碎片化法规、中小企业过度合规负担、治理模型错位；提出了风险分级监管、合规设计、可解释AI等实用治理策略。

Conclusion: 提供了核心AI治理挑战的简洁映射及概念图，为政策制定者和从业者提供了协调监管与创新的可行策略。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into critical infrastructure including healthcare, finance, energy, and defense, offers transformative benefits but also conflicts with evolving regulatory and governance frameworks. This paper presents a systematic literature review (SLR) to examine the challenges of balancing AI compliance and technological innovation across critical infrastructure sectors. The review follows established SLR guidelines to extract and synthesize insights from peer-reviewed articles, report, and institutional sources published between 2020-2025. The study identifies three interrelated challenges: fragmented regulations, excessive compliance burdens for smaller to medium enterprises (SMEs), and misaligned governance models. To address these challenges, the study highlights practical governance strategies, including risk-tiered regulation, compliance by design, and explainable AI, to support scalable and trustworthy AI deployment in critical sectors. Key contributions include a concise mapping of core AI-governance challenges and a conceptual diagram illustrating their overlap, as well as actionable strategies for policymakers and practitioner to harmonize oversight with innovation.

</details>


### [200] [AI-Automation Tooling in Computer Engineering Education: Mixed-Methods TAM/UTAUT Evidence for a General Acceptance Attitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12424)
*Aung Pyae*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对103名泰国计算机工程专业学生的混合方法横截面研究，发现学生对AI自动化工具（n8n平台）的接受度总体良好，其中绩效期望最强、享乐动机最弱；TAM/UTAUT子维度在短问卷情境下坍缩为单一因子；研究支持在本科计算教育中引入AI自动化工具，并提出了三种教学杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI和低代码工作流平台在软件实践中普及，一个关键的教育问题是：下一代计算机工程师是否会将这些工具视为有用、可用且值得持续投入。现有研究缺乏对大学生群体接受AI自动化工具的系统性实证分析。

Method: 混合方法横截面研究，对103名泰国计算机工程本科生进行三次相同流程的n8n平台工作坊；使用映射到TAM/UTAUT六个构念（PE、EE、BI、SE、HM、OQ）的12项五点李克特量表，辅以开放性反馈的主题分析；采用序数信度估计、自助法置信区间、非参数检验、多重比较控制的相关性、多分格维度诊断、共同方法偏差检验及跨session比较。

Result: 六个构念的接受度均呈现大效应量的正向结果，PE最强、HM最弱；维度诊断显示TAM/UTAUT子维度在短式问卷情境下坍缩为单一通用接受因子；定性主题在有用性和热情方面与定量结果一致，但输出质量维度存在分歧，发现了一个人数虽少但表达清晰的可靠性怀疑群体。

Conclusion: 研究支持在本科计算教育中引入AI自动化工具，并确定了三种理论驱动的教学杠杆：教学序列支架、自我效能支持、信任校准干预。

Abstract: As generative AI and low-code workflow platforms become routine in software practice, a key educational question is whether the next generation of computer engineers will accept these tools as useful, usable, and worthy of sustained engagement. This paper reports a mixed-methods, cross-sectional study of undergraduate computer engineering students' acceptance of AI automation tooling, instantiated through the open-source platform n8n across three identically scripted workshops in Thailand (n = 103). A 12-item, five-point Likert instrument mapped to six TAM/UTAUT constructs - Performance Expectancy (PE), Effort Expectancy (EE), Behavioral Intention (BI), Self-Efficacy (SE), Hedonic Motivation (HM), and Output Quality (OQ) - was complemented by inductive thematic analysis of open-ended feedback. Analyses combined ordinal reliability estimation, bootstrap confidence intervals, non-parametric tests, multiple-comparison-controlled correlations, polychoric dimensionality diagnostics, a common-method-bias check, and between-session comparisons. Acceptance was favorable across all six constructs with large effect sizes, with PE emerging as the strongest construct and HM as the weakest. Dimensionality diagnostics further revealed that canonical TAM/UTAUT sub-facets collapsed into a single general acceptance factor in this short-form post-workshop context, a finding with important methodological and theoretical implications. Qualitative themes converged with the quantitative profile regarding usefulness and enthusiasm but diverged on output quality, revealing a small yet articulate reliability-skeptical minority. The findings support the curricular adoption of AI automation tooling in undergraduate computing education and identify three theory-grounded instructional levers: instruction-sequencing scaffolds, self-efficacy supports, and trust-calibration interventions.

</details>


### [201] [An Explainable AI Assistant for Introductory Programming Education: Improving Feedback Reliability with Instructor-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12425)
*Muntasir Hoq,Griffin Pitts,Bradford Mott,Seung Lee,Jessica Vandenberg,Shuyin Jiao,Narges Norouzi,James Lester,Bita Akram*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释AI的课堂助手，通过分析学生代码、将逻辑错误映射到教师识别的误解，并提供教师编写的反馈，从而在入门编程课程中实现准确、可靠、个性化的反馈。专家评估和课堂部署结果表明，该系统能提供与教师验证一致的反馈，并带来积极的学生体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习在入门编程课程中被广泛认可，但学生往往缺乏及时、个性化的反馈支持。尽管大语言模型提供了可扩展的反馈机会，但其可解释性和可靠性存在问题。

Method: 提出一个AI驱动的课堂助手，使用可解释AI模型分析学生代码，将逻辑错误映射到教师识别的误解，并输出教师编写的反馈。进行了专家评估（与教师验证的反馈对比）和课堂部署（评估学生对可用性的感知）。

Result: 该助手能够为学生提供准确、经教师验证的反馈，并带来积极的使用体验。

Conclusion: 通过将AI分析与教师定义的教学知识相结合，可以在保证可解释性和可靠性的前提下，实现入门编程课程中个性化反馈的规模化提供。

Abstract: Active learning is widely recognized as an effective approach for improving learning outcomes in introductory programming courses. However, insufficient instructional support often limits students' access to timely, personalized feedback, which is crucial for mastering foundational programming concepts. Although recent advances in AI, particularly large language models, offer scalable opportunities for feedback, concerns about explainability and reliability remain. In this paper, we present an AI-driven classroom assistant that leverages an explainable AI model to analyze student code, map logical errors to instructor-identified misconceptions, and deliver instructor-authored feedback, thereby grounding reliability in instructor-defined pedagogical knowledge. To evaluate the effectiveness of our framework, we conducted an expert evaluation to examine its alignment with instructor-verified feedback and deployed the system in a classroom setting to assess students' perceptions of its usability. Results indicate that the assistant can provide accurate, instructor-verified feedback to students while fostering a positive experience.

</details>


### [202] [Two Wrongs, No Right: Auditing Social-Desirability Bias in LLM Annotators for Computational Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12426)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了三种开源7B指令微调模型在六项TweetEval任务中的标注偏差，发现对齐训练导致的错误并非单向：Zephyr存在宽容偏差（漏标有害标签），Mistral和Qwen存在过度纠正（误标有害标签），三模型均对堕胎立场表现出中立性偏差。提示工程无法统一修正这些偏差，安全框架甚至加剧立场扭曲。最值得注意的是，Zephyr的仇恨言论流行率估计恰好匹配黄金标准，但类别条件错误双向巨大——这种偶然抵消具有误导性。文章提出三分法分类学、诊断性FBR/FAR签名和轻量级金标验证协议，核心发现：聚合指标上表现良好的模型仍可能翻转研究的实质性实证结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算社会科学研究中越来越多使用LLM作为标注工具，但现有研究对LLM标注偏差如何影响最终实证结论缺乏系统考察。关键问题是：对齐训练引入的误差方向是否一致，以及聚合指标是否掩盖了误导性的类别条件错误。

Method: 选取三种开源7B指令微调模型（Zephyr、Mistral-Instruct、Qwen2.5-Instruct），在六个TweetEval分类任务上设计四种提示条件（中性、安全框架、去个性化、思维链），共72个实验条件。采用假良性率（FBR）和假警报率（FAR）诊断不同偏差类型，并通过流行率估计与金标对比评估实证结论的可靠性。

Result: Zephyr假良性率0.729（漏标攻击性语言），Mistral仇恨言论假警报率0.604（过度标注）。三模型对堕胎立场的中立性偏差导致反对派流行率低估24-40个百分点。四种提示均无法统一修正偏差，安全框架反而恶化立场扭曲。Zephyr在仇恨言论上的聚合流行率恰好匹配金标，但类别条件错误双向巨大。

Conclusion: LLM标注偏差具有系统性和模型特异性，不能仅凭聚合指标（如流行率估计）判断标注质量。提出三分法偏差分类（宽容偏差、过度纠正、中立性偏差）、诊断性FBR/FAR签名以及轻量级金标验证协议。核心警示：对齐校准的模型在聚合指标上看似可靠，仍可能翻转研究者报告的实际实证结论。

Abstract: LLM annotators are increasingly used in computational social science (CSS), but it is unclear whether their alignment-shaped errors preserve the empirical conclusions a researcher would report. We audit three open-source 7B instruction-tuned models (Zephyr, Mistral-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Instruct) across six TweetEval tasks under four prompt conditions (72 cells) and find that social-desirability failures do not run in a single direction. Zephyr exhibits leniency bias, systematically under-applying harmful labels (offensive language: false benign rate 0.729, false alarm rate 0.031). Mistral and Qwen exhibit overcorrection, over-applying the same labels (Mistral hate-speech FAR = 0.604). All three models exhibit neutrality bias on abortion stance, underestimating opposition prevalence by 24 to 40 percentage points and inflating the neutral label. None of the four prompting interventions we test (neutral, safety framing, depersonalized, chain-of-thought) corrects these failures across models; safety framing can worsen stance distortion. Strikingly, Zephyr's hate-speech prevalence estimate matches the gold rate exactly while its class-conditional errors are large in both directions, an accidental cancellation that misleads aggregate validation. We translate these patterns into a three-part taxonomy with diagnostic FBR/FAR signatures and a lightweight gold-sample validation protocol. The headline for trustworthy CSS: a model that looks calibrated on aggregate metrics can still flip the substantive empirical conclusion a researcher would report.

</details>


### [203] [Planning on Paper: Problem Decomposition with Diagrams in Introductory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12427)
*Annapurna Vadaparty,Devamardeep Hayatpur,Adalbert Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter,Daniel Zingaro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析55份学生手绘的分解图，揭示了初学编程者在多函数任务中进行问题分解时使用的多种表征策略（如层次化函数调用和执行顺序），并识别了符号不一致、执行顺序混乱、抽象与复用不足、封装性差等常见问题，为CS1教学提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题分解是计算教育的核心关注点，尤其在生成式AI背景下，许多CS1教育者主张将教学重点从代码编写转向分解和高层规划。然而，目前缺乏对初学者如何在大型多函数任务中进行分解的认知。

Method: 在50分钟的实验室中，学生根据文字游戏描述手绘分解图。研究者对55份图表进行归纳主题分析，通过协商一致性编码，标记了显著元素（如函数及其关系）和出现的问题。

Result: 学生使用了多种表征策略，包括层次化函数调用和顺序执行。发现了符号使用问题（如同一个图中使用不一致、不兼容的符号）、执行顺序问题、抽象与复用问题、封装性问题、清晰度问题以及特定问题领域的误解。

Conclusion: 初学者的分解能力受到多种程序行为基础模型的塑造，存在结构性推理与顺序性推理之间的张力。研究提出了对分解教学和未来工作的启示，包括明确表征约束和将计划追踪视为模拟。

Abstract: Background and Context. Problem decomposition is a core concern of computing education. It has also become increasingly relevant: in response to GenAI, many CS1 educators are advocating for shifting instructional emphasis away from code writing and towards decomposition and higher-level planning. Currently, there is a lack of knowledge in how novices do decomposition in large, multifunction tasks. Objectives. In this study, we describe how students represent solutions to a decomposition task, and characterize common issues that arise in those representations. Method. In a 50-minute lab, students were given a description of a word game and asked to draw (with pencil and paper) a decomposition diagram for a program that would implement this game. We performed an inductive thematic analysis with negotiated agreement on 55 of the diagrams, coding salient elements (e.g. functions and the relationships between them) and issues that arose. Findings. Students used multiple representational strategies, including hierarchical function calls and sequencing (order of execution). We identified issues in notation (including use of differing, incompatible notations within the same diagram), order of execution, abstraction and reuse, encapsulation, clarity, and problem-specific misunderstandings. Implications. These findings suggest that novice decomposition is shaped by multiple underlying models of program behavior, with tensions between structural and sequence-focused reasoning. We discuss implications for decomposition instruction and future work, including clarifying representational constraints and plan tracing as simulation.

</details>


### [204] [Mapping AI Programs in the U.S: A Status Report from Early 2026 and an Analysis of AI Majors and Minors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12428)
*Felix Muzny,Carolyn Jones,Carter Ithier,Hasnain Sikora,Hrutika Harshadbhai Patel,Carla E. Brodley*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文报告了2026年春季美国本科AI项目的现状，开发了一个动态更新的爬取和映射工具（cicmap.ai），覆盖560多所高校中的350多个AI项目，并对66个AI主修和87个辅修项目的课程要求进行了分析。主要发现包括：非所有AI主修都要求通用AI课程，但若未要求则必有机器学习课程；超过三分之一的主修要求AI伦理课程，而辅修中仅不到四分之一有此要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI教育快速发展且面临重大变革的时期，缺乏对美国本科AI项目全面、动态更新的系统性记录和分析工具，无法为利益相关者提供清晰的AI教育全景图。

Method: 开发了自动爬取和映射工具（cicmap.ai），对560多所四年制大学进行搜索，检测并展示350多个本科AI项目（包括主修、辅修、方向和证书），并持续动态更新。

Result: 工具覆盖了占美国CS本科毕业生86%的院校样本；发现AI主修和辅修项目在规模和课程要求上存在巨大差异；并非所有主修都要求通用AI课程，但未要求者必有ML课程；超过三分之一主修要求AI伦理课程，辅修中仅不到四分之一有此要求。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了三方面贡献：1）在变革时期记录了美国AI项目的现状；2）提供了可探索AI项目及其要求的动态工具；3）对AI主修和辅修课程要求进行了深入分析，揭示了课程设置的异同。

Abstract: We present a report on the status of undergraduate Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs in the United States in Spring 2026. In so doing, we 1) describe our scraping and mapping tools, which dynamically update to track the state of AI education in the U.S., and 2) create a historic record at a time of great upheaval. The tool we developed, available at https://cicmap.ai, detects, scrapes, and displays data from more than 350 undergraduate AI programs--majors, minors, concentrations, and certificates--at 4-year universities. Our tool searched over 560 institutions to locate these programs, a sample that represents 86\% of all undergraduate Computer Science (CS) graduates in the U.S. This tool allows prospective students, guidance counselors, administrators, and faculty to easily access AI program requirements and is designed to continually update as new programs emerge. To the best of our knowledge, this survey represents the most comprehensive snapshot of the state of AI programs in the U.S. to date. With this work we offer three important contributions: 1) a record of AI programs in the U.S. at a time of great upheaval; 2) a tool to explore AI programs and their requirements; and 3) an analysis of the courses required for 66 AI majors and 87 AI minors. Our analysis of majors and minors shows great variability in the size and the requirements of these degrees, but we note two takeaways. First, not all majors require a general AI course, but if they don't, they do require a Machine Learning (ML) course. Second, while more than a third of majors require an Ethics in AI course, just under a quarter of AI minors do.

</details>


### [205] [Muse Spark Safety & Preparedness Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12429)
*Cristina Menghini,Peter Ney,Hamza Kwisaba,Zifan,Wang,Miles Turpin,Felix Binder,Jean-Christophe Testud,Aidan Boyd,Nathaniel Li,Ivan Evtimov,Klaudia Krawiecka,Arman Zharmagambetov,Jeremy Kritz,Alexander R. Fabbri,Daniel Song,Jinpeng Miao,Joonas Hjelt,Meghna Ramani,Leona Lan,Reza Aghajani,Joanna Bitton,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Devin Norder,Khalid El-Arini,Paridhi Singh,Vítor Albiero,Sahana CB,Rashnil Chaturvedi,Elahe Dabir,Edoardo Debenedetti,Jim Gust,Ziwen Han,Kat He,Sean Hendryx,Lifeng Jin,Polina Kirichenko,Sandra Lefdal,Kenneth Li,Asad Liaqat,Inna Lin,Despoina Magka,Neal Mangaokar,Ishita Mediratta,Zach Miller,Smitha Milli,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Saba Nazir,Hung Nguyen,Maximilian Nickel,Kelvin Niu,Kerem Oktar,Bhargavi Paranjape,Parth Pathak,Maya Pavlova,Emmanuel Ramirez,David Renardy,Candace Ross,Yasha Sheynin,Claudia Shi,Shivam Singhal,Evangelia Spiliopoulou,Rakshith Sharma Srinivasa,Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Spencer Whitman,Adina Williams,Chen Xing,Andy Zou,Tommy Ma,Siqi Deng,James Beldock,Prashant Ratanchandani,Kate Plawiak,Taesung Lee,Ryan Victory,Lindsay Hundley,Rachad Alao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Jianfeng Chi,Gary Frost,Pegah Ghahremani,Niki Howe,Yuheng Huang,Saeed Jahed,Hannah Korevaar,Trang Le,Zhe Liu,Jinghong Luo,Qin Lyu,Nina Mehrabi,Abraham Montilla,Chirag Nagpal,Cyrus Nikolaidis,Rajvardhan Oak,Manoj Ravi,Vidya Sarma,Aman Shankar,Alana Shine,Eric Michael Smith,Mariana Tandon,Michael Tontchev,Caoyu Wang,Zihan Wang,Corinne Wong,Zheng Wu,Hongyuan Zhan,Justin Zhao,Zexuan Zhong,Chengxu Zhuang,Tristan Goodman,Ayaz Minhas,Harrison Rudolph,Victoria Jeffries,Ingrid Dickinson,Alex Vaughan,Lauren Deason,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Julian Michael,Shengjia Zhao,Summer Yue*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Meta发布了最新大语言模型Muse Spark，在Advanced AI Scaling Framework下评估了化学/生物、网络安全和失控等灾难性风险领域，通过多层级缓解措施将高风险降至可接受水平，并作为Meta AI的基础模型发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在发布大型AI模型前，需要在Advanced AI Scaling Framework下系统评估灾难性风险（化学/生物、网络安全、失控风险），确保部署后的残余风险处于可接受水平。

Method: 采用Advanced AI Scaling Framework对灾难性风险领域进行评估，针对双重用途和高风险能力进行广泛评测，实施多层级缓解措施（安全防护），并通过基准测试验证模型在危险工作流中的拒绝能力。

Result: 在部署缓解措施前，化学/生物能力被评估为"高风险"类别；实施多层级缓解后，Muse Spark在化学和生物危险工作流相关基准上展现出最先进的拒绝能力，残余风险降至可接受水平。

Conclusion: Muse Spark在Advanced AI Scaling Framework下评估为可接受的残余风险水平，具备广泛的内容安全性，因此将其作为Meta AI的基础模型发布。

Abstract: Muse Spark is the latest large language model developed by Meta. In this report, we first present evaluations for catastrophic risk domains under Meta's Advanced AI Scaling Framework, along with the evidence that informed our launch decision. We then discuss additional considerations, such as Muse Spark's broader content safety and behavioral profile, that are relevant to overall safety but fall outside the catastrophic risk domains governed by the Framework. Our preparedness results covering Chemical and Biological, Cybersecurity, and Loss of Control risks assess Muse Spark's deployment within Meta AI as presenting acceptable levels of residual risks under our Advanced AI Scaling Framework. We conducted a broad set of evaluations targeting dual-use and high-risk capabilities across these catastrophic risk domains. Those evaluations identified elevated risks prior to mitigations, with Chemical and Biological capabilities assessed as likely reaching the "high risk" category under the Advanced AI Scaling Framework before safeguards were applied. We have implemented a multi-layered set of mitigations that address the identified risks, and Muse Spark demonstrates state-of-the-art refusal across a range of benchmarks related to hazardous workflows in chemistry and biology. We therefore release Muse Spark as the underlying model of Meta AI.

</details>


### [206] [Will AI Agents Free Us From Meaningless Work? A Human-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12430)
*Davide Ghia,Jaspreet Ranjit,Tania Cerquitelli,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究在任务层面验证了“狗屁工作”感知量表，发现工人认为无意义的任务更愿意交给AI处理，且这类任务被认为需要更少的人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从职业层面探讨AI自动化，忽视了同一职业内不同任务对工人意义感的差异，作者希望填补这一任务层面的分析空白。

Method: 收集202名工人对171项工作任务的评分，通过验证五条目“狗屁工作”感知量表，分析其对AI委托意愿的预测作用及所需人类监督程度。

Result: 感知无意义性强烈预测了工人将任务委托给AI的意愿，且这类任务被认为需要更少的人类监督。

Conclusion: 被感知为“狗屁”的任务是AI委托的自然候选对象，工人的偏好与技术可行性之间存在一致性。

Abstract: Some claim that AI agents will free workers from the boring parts of their jobs, yet little is known about how workers themselves identify which tasks should be automated. Prior research focuses on occupations, overlooking that workers experience varying levels of meaning across tasks within the same role. We address this gap with a task-level analysis grounded in Graeber's theory of bullshit jobs. Using ratings from 202 workers on 171 workplace tasks, we (1) validate a five-item scale of perceived bullshitness, (2) show that perceived bullshitness strongly predicts desire for AI delegation, and (3) find that such tasks are also seen as requiring less human oversight. Together, these findings suggest that tasks perceived as bullshit are natural candidates for AI delegation, aligning worker preferences with perceived feasibility.

</details>


### [207] [AI Debris: Residual Risk and the Afterlife of Failed AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12432)
*Victor Frimpong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI残留物"(AI debris)概念，指代已退役AI系统在机构中留下的工作流程依赖、数据污染、能力退化、合法性侵蚀等残余风险，并设计了可操作的AI残留物退役协议(AIDP)以强化AI全生命周期治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架仅关注开发和部署阶段的风险，忽视系统退役后残留的社会技术风险，导致组织行为、问责制和信任持续受到影响。

Method: 通过构建残留物领域分类学，识别残留机制（制度记忆、路径依赖、责任规避、数据反馈效应），并提出AI残留物退役协议(AIDP)作为可审计的检查清单。

Result: 以亚马逊停用的招聘工具为例，展示了算法决策类别和筛选启发式在系统回滚后依然可留存。提出了包含决策足迹冻结、事件审查、补救、可争议性和退役后问责分配的可审计证据清单。

Conclusion: AIDP可作为监管者、审计师和组织预防表面合规、强化AI全生命周期治理、提升高风险决策情境下制度韧性的实用工具。

Abstract: AI governance frameworks primarily focus on risks during the development and deployment phases, implicitly treating system withdrawal as a technical shutdown. This paper argues that decommissioned AI systems generate residual risk, termed AI debris, that persists after model removal and continues to shape institutional behaviour, accountability, and trust. AI debris is defined as the post-withdrawal socio-technical residue of AI systems, including workflow dependency, data contamination, capability displacement (deskilling), legitimacy erosion, and accountability breakdown. The paper develops a typology of debris domains and identifies mechanisms through which debris persists, including institutional memory, path dependency, blame avoidance, and feedback effects in organisational data. To operationalise the concept, the paper proposes an evaluator-ready AI Debris Decommissioning Protocol (AIDP), a stepwise checklist specifying auditable evidence for freezing decision footprints, incident review, remediation, contestability, and post-withdrawal accountability assignment. A brief vignette of Amazon's discontinued hiring tool illustrates how algorithmic decision categories and screening heuristics can persist after system rollback. The paper contributes a practical governance instrument for regulators, auditors, and organisations seeking to prevent paper compliance, strengthen AI lifecycle governance, and improve institutional resilience in high-stakes decision environments.

</details>


### [208] [Marginal Alignment Does Not Guarantee Joint-Distribution Fidelity: An Official-Reference Audit of Nemotron-Personas-Korea with Cross-Locale Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12433)
*Joonhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出独立性假设足迹(IAF)审计原语，用于检测合成人格数据集在联合属性分布上的偏差。以NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-Korea为案例，发现其边际分布对齐但三个联合分布存在显著失败：主要职业分布条件失配、兵役年龄分布与制度不一致、男性主导职业中女性比例被过度拉平至均衡。跨地区迁移性显示诊断结果因地而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人格数据集通常仅以边际分布与官方统计数据对齐作为可信度声明，但下游用户实际消费的是跨年龄、性别、地区、职业、学历、姓名和机构状态等多属性的联合结构。边际对齐不等同于联合分布被保留，缺乏针对联合分布一致性的审计方法。

Method: 提出IAF审计原语：对数据卡片中声明为独立处理的属性组合，将合成数据的联合分布与外部官方/机构参考进行比较。可用直接联合表格时直接对比，否则通过规则约束进行间接检验。应用于NPK数据集（100万韩国合成人格），对比KOSIS边际与KEIS毕业生数据等官方参考。

Result: NPK在边际分布上与KOSIS对齐，但三个联合分布失败：主要职业×职业分布存在大条件失配；兵役年龄分布与机构数据不一致；男性主导职业中女性比例被过度拉平至接近均等。跨六个地区的迁移性实验发现诊断结果因地而异，且参考分类法基数混淆了跨地区标记计数。

Conclusion: 对于作为"硅样本"使用的合成人格数据集，边际对齐声明必须辅以基于披露锚点的联合审计才能安全复用于下游任务。研究释放了完整的审计工件（参考清单、职业交叉映射、衍生指标、可复现脚本）以供其他合成人格资源的重新定位使用。

Abstract: Synthetic persona datasets cite alignment with official demographics as a basis for trust, yet downstream users consume them as joint structures across age, sex, region, occupation, education, name, and institutional status. Marginal alignment does not imply that these joints are preserved. We propose the Independence-Assumption Footprint (IAF), an audit primitive that operates on the attribute combinations a dataset card itself documents as treated independently. For each such combination, IAF compares the synthetic joint against an external official or institutional reference, using direct joint tables where available and rule-implied checks otherwise. Applied to NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-Korea (one million Korean synthetic personas), IAF finds that NPK aligns with KOSIS marginals while three joints fail. The major-by-occupation distribution against the KEIS graduate universe carries a large conditional mismatch. The age profile of military service is institutionally inconsistent. Female representation in male-dominated occupations is substantially over-flattened toward parity, with the strict screening verdict mapping-dependent and age-robust under direct standardisation. A transferability demonstration across six further NPK locales finds locale-dependent rather than universal diagnostics, with reference-taxonomy cardinality confounding cross-locale flag counts. For synthetic personas used as silicon samples, marginal claims must therefore be paired with disclosure-anchored joint audits before reuse. The released audit artefacts (reference manifests, occupational crosswalks, derived metrics, reproducibility scripts) instantiate this protocol on the NPK family and are released for retargeting at other synthetic persona resources.

</details>


### [209] [Pluralistic-Alignment Urbanism: Operationalizing a Right to AI for Inclusive Public Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12434)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出多元对齐城市化（PAU）框架，将公共空间AI系统视为市政基础设施，通过两个蒙特利尔参与式案例研究（街道评估和局部交叉视觉空间数据集），论证结构性分歧、子群体差异、有界预测缩放和中性偏好判断如何为市政AI治理提供程序性约束，并设计了包含分类报告、版本化价值登记、常设审议小组等治理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市政机构越来越多地使用机器学习进行 sidewalk 盘点、街景评分和公共空间干预可视化，但这些系统的输出进入预算、设计迭代和公共论证时，关于包容性、安全性和归属感的判断仍存在争议。现有AI治理缺乏处理多元群体分歧和公共空间特有价值冲突的程序性机制。

Method: 采用参与式案例研究方法，在蒙特利尔开展两个案例：1）Street Review 收集居民街道评估标准并训练子群体感知的缩放模型（R²=0.89）；2）LIVS（局部交叉视觉空间数据集）构建多元偏好数据用于对齐文本到图像模型，将中性选择视为不确定性证据。基于案例发现提取治理约束并设计PAU程序性框架。

Result: 1）Street Review 的子群体感知缩放模型在测试集上达到R²=0.89；2）分歧呈现结构性特征，审议改变何为证据的认定，缩放可行但受限于模态和覆盖范围，中立性限制了偏好调优的正当性边界。PAU框架包含分类报告、版本化价值登记、常设审议小组、采购条款及暂停与回滚权限。

Conclusion: PAU将公共空间AI系统视为市民基础设施，提出了程序性的"AI权利"，通过结构化的审议机制和约束性治理架构来容纳多元价值冲突，为市政AI系统的合法性和正当性提供了程序性保障路径。

Abstract: Municipal agencies increasingly use machine learning to inventory sidewalks, score streetscapes, and generate visualizations of public-space interventions. These systems produce outputs that enter budgeting, design iteration, and public justification, yet judgments about inclusion, safety, and belonging remain contested. This paper proposes Pluralistic-Alignment Urbanism (PAU), a procedural governance framework that treats public-space AI systems as civic infrastructure and formulates a procedural Right to AI for municipal uses of such systems. Drawing on two participatory case studies with community organizations in Montreal, Canada, the paper examines how disagreement, subgroup variation, bounded predictive scaling, and neutral preference judgments can inform municipal AI governance. Street Review elicits resident criteria for streetscape evaluation and trains a subgroup-aware scaling model for co-produced judgments, achieving an R2 of 0.89 on a held-out test set. LIVS, a Local Intersectional Visual Spaces dataset, constructs pluralistic preference data for aligning text-to-image models and treats neutral selections as evidence of indeterminacy. Across the cases, disagreement appears structured, deliberation changes what counts as evidence, scaling is feasible but limited by modality and coverage, and neutrality constrains what preference tuning can justify. PAU translates these constraints into a municipal governance architecture with disaggregated reporting, a versioned value register, standing deliberative cells, procurement clauses, and defined pause and rollback authority.

</details>


### [210] [Auditing Discriminatory Patterns in Mortgage Lending Through Association Rules and Fair Binning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12435)
*Archit Rathod,Dhwani Chande,Het Nagda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了美国抵押贷款申请中的种族和性别差异，发现标准数据分箱预处理会放大这些差异。通过HMDA 2023数据集（芝加哥大都会区）的103,481份贷款申请，采用三阶段流水线分析：标准等频分箱显示收入离散化中存在9.63%的种族偏差；公平分箱在epsilon=0.08时成功但公平代价为29.4%；FP-Growth发现高债务收入比是主要拒绝预测因子（置信度67.2%，提升度2.81），种族偏差未显示为高支持度规则；K-Means聚类结合差异影响审计发现，即使在经济状况相似的群体中，黑人申请者的拒绝率仍显著高于白人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抵押贷款领域存在持续的种族和性别差异问题，而标准数据预处理步骤（尤其是属性分箱）可能加剧这些差异，现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) PySpark数据清洗与分箱（标准等频分箱 vs epsilon偏差公平分箱），(2) FP-Growth关联规则挖掘比较两种分箱下的拒绝模式，(3) K-Means聚类结合每簇差异影响审计。

Result: 标准分箱显示9.63%种族偏差；公平分箱在epsilon=0.08时成功，公平代价29.4%；FP-Growth中高债务收入比是主要拒绝预测因子（67.2%置信度，2.81提升度）；K-Means聚类发现10/45的簇-组对存在显著差异，黑人申请者拒绝率显著高于经济状况相似的白人。

Conclusion: 标准数据分箱会放大贷款审批中的种族差异，公平分箱虽有帮助但代价高昂，且即使在经济状况相似群体中，种族差异仍然存在，需在数据预处理阶段纳入公平性考量。

Abstract: Mortgage lending in the United States exhibits persistent racial and gender disparities. We investigate whether standard data preprocessing steps, specifically attribute binning, amplify these disparities in downstream pattern mining. Using 103,481 cleaned mortgage applications from the HMDA 2023 dataset (Chicago metropolitan area), we build a three-stage pipeline: (1) a PySpark data cleaning and binning pipeline that implements both standard equal-frequency binning and the epsilon-biased fair binning algorithm from Asudeh et al. [1], (2) FP-Growth association rule mining that compares denial patterns under both binning regimes, and (3) K-Means clustering with a per-cluster disparate impact audit. Our standard binning shows 9.63% racial bias in income discretization, consistent with the 8-10% reported in prior work. Fair binning with seven race groups is infeasible at epsilon=0.03 and only succeeds at epsilon=0.08 with a Price of Fairness of 29.4%. FP-Growth reveals that high debt-to-income ratio is the dominant denial predictor (67.2% confidence, 2.81 lift), while racial bias does not appear as explicit high-support rules. However, K-Means clustering followed by a disparate impact audit flags 10 out of 45 cluster-group pairs, showing that Black applicants face significantly higher denial rates than White applicants even among financially similar groups.

</details>


### [211] [Knowing the Rules Is Not Enough: Student Regulatory Awareness and Use of GenAI in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12436)
*Lasse Bischof,Eva-Maria Schön,Maria Rauschenberger,Michael Neumann*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过对151名计算机相关专业本科生的调查，发现大多数学生积极使用GenAI工具，但超过一半对使用是否符合校规不确定；监管意识与使用行为之间仅存在弱到中等的关联，且学生主要依赖私人工具而非学校提供的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注GenAI工具的采用率和态度，但学生对机构法规的认知及其感知合规性尚待探索；理解法规认知是否影响学生行为对于高校制定AI政策至关重要。

Method: 对汉诺威应用科学与艺术大学151名商业信息系统和电子政务专业本科生进行问卷调查，收集GenAI使用情况、工具类型、法规认知及感知合规性数据；采用描述性统计、交叉表和相关性分析。

Result: 大多数学生积极使用GenAI工具，但超半数不确定使用是否符合规定；法规认知与实际使用行为之间仅存在弱到中等的关联；学生主要依赖私人GenAI工具而非学校提供的工具。

Conclusion: 研究发现揭示了机构法规与学生实践之间的差距，为教育者和高校改进政策沟通以及更有效地将GenAI整合到教学环境中提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Context: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools such as ChatGPT are increasingly integrated into students learning practices. While previous research mainly examines adoption rates and attitudes, students awareness of institutional regulations and their perceived compliance remain unexplored. Understanding whether regulatory awareness influences student behavior is therefore important as higher education institutions create and apply AI policies. Objective: This study investigates how students awareness of GenAI regulations relates to their perceived compliance and actual usage behavior. Our research objective is to examine the association between regulatory knowledge, GenAI use, and perceived rule conformity among students in computer science related study programs. Method: A survey with 151 undergraduate students in Business Information Systems and E-Government programs at the University of Applied Sciences and Arts Hannover (Germany) collected data on GenAI usage, tools used, awareness of institutional regulations, and perceived compliance. Descriptive statistics, cross-tabulations, and correlation analyzes were applied. Results: Most students actively use GenAI tools, but over half are uncertain whether their usage complies with institutional regulations. Regulatory awareness shows only weak to moderate associations with actual usage behavior. Students primarily rely on privately accessed GenAI tools rather than institutionally provided solutions. Contributions: The study contributes empirical evidence on the relationship between regulatory awareness and GenAI usage in higher education. Our findings highlight a gap between institutional regulations and student practices and provide insights for educators and institutions on improving policy communication and integrating GenAI more effectively into teaching and learning contexts.

</details>


### [212] [Algorithmic Constitutionalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12437)
*Oren Perez,Nurit Wimer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判了"伦理工程"作为AI治理方案的不足，提出了"算法宪政主义"框架，包含分层架构（操作层与元代码层）、算法元推理和通过 deliberation 的修正三大支柱，并以Facebook内容审核制度为例进行分析，最后讨论了该框架对欧盟《数字服务法》的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对社会生活的渗透带来了显著风险，现有文献中提出的"伦理工程"方案不足以应对AI治理挑战，亟需新的理论框架。

Method: 提出"算法宪政主义"框架：①分层架构（操作层/对象层与元代码层）；②算法元推理（实时监控、验证和纠正）；③通过 deliberation 进行修正。并以Facebook内容审核制度为案例进行分析。

Result: 揭示了社会宪政主义与算法宪政主义之间的张力——试图将AI系统置于外部 deliberative 控制之下，反而可能使AI agent干预该过程，从而削弱其目的。

Conclusion: 算法宪政主义为AI治理提供了替代性框架；该论证对2022年10月生效的欧盟《数字服务法》具有重要政策含义。

Abstract: The increasing encroachment of artificial intelligence (AI) on social life raises significant risks for society, particularly within the infospheres created and controlled by companies such as Google, Facebook, Apple, and Amazon. This article examines these risks through an in-depth analysis of Facebook's content moderation regime, which is already partially governed by algorithms. We argue that the idea of ethical engineering, often proposed in the literature as a solution to the governance challenges posed by AI, is inadequate for several reasons. In response, we develop an alternative framework, which we term "algorithmic constitutionalism."
  Our approach rests on three pillars: (a) a layered architecture consisting of two levels of code: (i) an operative or object level and (ii) a meta level designed to protect the system's core principles from algorithmically initiated change; (b) algorithmic meta-reasoning, which enables the system to operate simultaneously at both levels so that it can monitor, verify, and potentially correct in real time operations at the object level that depart from principles protected at the meta-code level; and (c) correction through deliberation.
  The article elaborates the concept of algorithmic constitutionalism and demonstrates how it may be applied to Facebook's content moderation regime. As part of this analysis, we examine the tension between societal constitutionalism and algorithmic constitutionalism. Paradoxically, attempts to subject AI systems to external deliberative control may also enable AI agents to intervene in that process, potentially undermining its purpose. The article concludes by considering the implications of this argument for the European Digital Services Act, which entered into force in October 2022.

</details>


### [213] [From Real-World Projects to Research-Oriented Learning: Continuous Improvement of a Master-Level Course in Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12438)
*Michael Neumann,Eva-Maria Schön*

Main category: cs.CY
