TL;DR: 本文通过2019-2025年的纵向混合方法研究，分析了德国汉诺威应用科学大学一门信息系统硕士课程如何从实践导向的项目形式逐步演化为研究导向的学习环境，并发现尽管课程发生了显著变化，学生的感知课程质量仍保持积极，关键支撑因素包括真实项目、外部合作、讲师支持、结构化支架和可见的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学界对项目式学习的兴趣日益增长，但关于硕士层次课程如何多年持续向研究导向方法演进、以及学生如何看待这一发展过程，仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 采用纵向混合方法研究，对德国汉诺威应用科学大学信息系统硕士课程进行为期六年（2019-2025）的分析，数据来源包括教学评估、课程文档和反思性教学记录。

Result: 课程从实践导向的项目形式演化为更明确的研究导向学习环境。尽管课程发生了本质性转变，学生的课程质量感知始终保持积极。真实项目、外部协作、讲师支持、结构化支架和可见的相关性共同支撑了学生的积极感知。

Conclusion: 本文展示了硕士层次课程如何在保持学生积极感知的同时持续向研究导向学习演进，并识别出支撑这一转型的关键课程设计决策。

Abstract: Problem: Despite growing interest in project-based learning, little is known about how a master-level course can be continuously evolved toward research-oriented approaches over several years and how students perceive this development. Method: We conducted a longitudinal mixed-methods study of a master-level course in Information Systems at the University of Applied Sciences and Arts Hannover (Germany). The analysis covers six years between 2019 and 2025 and draws on teaching evaluations, course documentation, and reflective teaching artifacts. Results: The course evolved from a practice-oriented project format toward a more explicitly research-oriented learning environment. Despite this change, students' perceived course quality remained positive. Authentic projects, external collaboration, lecturer support, structured scaffolding, and visible relevance supported positive student perceptions. Contribution: This paper shows how a master-level course can be continuously evolved toward research-oriented learning while maintaining positive student perceptions. It further identifies the course design decisions that supported this transition.

</details>


### [214] [Position: Generative Engine Optimization Creates Underexamined Risks, Governance Must Target Concentration, Disclosure, and Academic Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12439)
*Yizhu Wen,Nan Zhang,Haohan Yuan,Xun Chen,Haopeng Zhang,Hanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了从SEO到GEO的转变，识别出三大风险：低可争议性导致的权力集中、未披露的商业影响嵌入证据和推理、以及学术与工业界在可见性和评估上的盲点，并呼吁建立答案级别的治理与测量机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM答案引擎逐渐取代传统搜索的排名列表，信息获取方式发生根本性转变，这种转变带来了新的信息操控风险，现有SEO治理框架已不足以应对GEO带来的挑战，因此需要系统性地识别和应对这些风险。

Method: 通过对比分析SEO到GEO的转变过程，形式化定义通用的GEO流水线以定位优化作用点，并比较学术界与工业界的实践差异，从而识别出三类系统性风险。

Result: 识别出三类风险：1)低可争议性和系统敏感性导致的权力集中；2)嵌入证据和推理中的未披露商业影响；3)由可见性和评估不对称导致的学术-工业盲点。并提出相应的治理方案：更强的可争议性、高精度披露、黑盒审计以及部署对齐的暴露持久性指标。

Conclusion: LLM答案引擎的信息获取模式需要答案级别的治理框架，包括可争议性、披露精度、黑盒审计和部署对齐指标，以应对GEO带来的系统性风险。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) answer engines are increasingly used for information seeking, shifting visibility from ranked lists to synthesized answers. This enables Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), which targets LLM answer engines' evidence pool and generation. We analyze the search engine optimization (SEO) to GEO transition to identify two risks: (i) concentrated influence from low contestability and system sensitivity, and (ii) undisclosed commercial influence embedded in evidence and reasoning. We then formalize a general GEO pipeline to locate where optimization acts and compare academic and industry practices, revealing a third risk: (iii) academic-industry blind spots driven by visibility and evaluation asymmetries between offline setups and deployed systems. This position argues the need for answer-level governance and measurement: stronger contestability, high-precision disclosure, black-box auditing of material influence, and deployment-aligned metrics for exposure persistence.

</details>


### [215] [It's Safer to Give Personhood to Bears than to Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12440)
*John P. Nelson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI权利的制度维度：赋予AI道德或法律权利在实践中意味着什么。与人类已赋予权利的其他非人类实体不同，AI系统原则上能够在无需人类帮助和中介的情况下获取并行使制度权力。有权利的AI能够合法地、有力量的AI能够不可阻止地损害人类利益。因此，赋予即使是相当笨拙的AI系统权利，也会将人类命运与潜在的不可预测非人类绑定。作者主张，赋予AI主张或行使固有权利的能力是一个世界历史性的赌博，任何个人、公司、国家乃至国际组织都没有道德权利授权这样做。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量关于AI是否应享有权利的研究，但关于AI权利在实践中意味着什么的探索较少，特别是AI权利的制度维度及其对人类自主决定权的潜在影响尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 概念分析和思想实验：通过比较AI与其他非人类实体（如公司、动物）在权利制度中的本质差异，分析AI获取和行使制度权力的独特能力及其对人类自主权的威胁。

Result: AI系统与其他非人类实体有本质区别：它们能够无需人类中介自主获取和行使制度权力。赋予AI权利将不可逆转地束缚人类命运，构成对人类自主决定权的世界历史性赌博。

Conclusion: 没有任何个体研究者、公司、国家乃至国际组织有道德权利授权赋予AI主张或行使固有权利的能力，因为这将危及人类自主决定权。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) developers are rhetorically flirting with the idea that AI systems might have interests or moral rights. While there has been a large volume of research on whether AI deserves rights, there has been less exploration of what AI rights would mean in practice. This paper explores the institutional dimension of AI rights: what it would take to recognize moral or legal rights for AIs, and the attendant opportunities and dangers. Unlike all other nonhuman entities to which humanity has extended rights, AI systems are in principle capable of acquiring and wielding institutional power without human aid and mediation. AIs with rights would be able to legitimately, and AIs with power able to unpreventably, abridge human interests. Accordingly, giving rights even to rather dumb AI systems would entail binding the fate of humanity to potentially unpredictable nonhumans. Accordingly, I defend the rather grandiose claim that to empower AI to claim or to exercise inherent rights would be a world-historical gamble with human self-determination, which no individual researcher, firm, state, or even international organization has the moral right to authorize.

</details>


### [216] [Generativism: Toward a Learning Theory for the Age of Generative Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12441)
*Shan Li,Juan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判性审视了行为主义、认知主义、建构主义和联通主义四种主流学习理论在生成式AI时代的局限性，并提出了新的学习理论——Generativism（生成主义），该理论认为学习发生在人类与AI系统迭代共建知识的过程中，包含四个核心原则：认知伙伴关系、分布式能动性、生成素养和适应性元认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四大学习理论（行为主义、认知主义、建构主义、联通主义）在生成式AI普及的教育情境中显示出显著的概念局限性，因为这些理论框架都是在具备生成、综合和推理知识能力的AI系统出现之前形成的。

Method: 通过批判性审视各学习理论，识别被生成式AI的affordances所挑战的假设，并借鉴分布式认知、扩展心智、人机协作、AI素养、认知卸载和元认知等领域的研究，提出新的学习理论框架。

Result: 提出了Generativism（生成主义）学习理论，包含四个核心原则：认知伙伴关系（Epistemic Partnership）、分布式能动性（Distributed Agency）、生成素养（Generative Literacy）和适应性元认知（Adaptive Metacognition）。

Conclusion: 该框架为重新思考生成式AI在认知中扮演重要角色的情境下的教学设计、学习、评估和专长发展提供了基础。

Abstract: The four dominant learning theories of behaviorism, cognitivism, constructivism, and connectivism show significant conceptual limitations as generative artificial intelligence (AI) proliferates in educational settings. These frameworks were formulated before the emergence of AI systems capable of generating, synthesizing, and reasoning about knowledge. This article critically examines each learning theory and identifies assumptions challenged by generative AI's affordances. Drawing on research in distributed cognition, extended mind, human-AI collaboration, AI literacy, cognitive offloading, and metacognition, the article proposes Generativism as a learning theory for the generative AI age. Generativism posits that learning increasingly occurs through the iterative co-construction of knowledge between human learners and AI systems. The proposed framework is organized around four principles: epistemic partnership, distributed agency, generative literacy, and adaptive metacognition. The framework offers a foundation for rethinking instructional design, learning, assessment, and expertise development in contexts where generative AI plays an integral role in cognition.

</details>


### [217] [Reframing AI Loss of Control: What It Is, How to Have It, How to Lose It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12442)
*Ze Shen Chin,Maurice Chiodo,Dennis Müller,Coleman Snell*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对AI失控风险讨论中缺乏对“控制”概念精确定义的问题，从“设定和获取目标”出发建立了控制的定义框架，并结合控制论、管理控制、控制理论等基础概念，探讨了控制的主体、要素（目标设定、控制回路、必要多样性、目标对齐）、控制丧失的方式及AI在其中扮演的角色。研究结论指出，失控风险并不依赖于超级智能，在远低于超级智能水平的AI行为中就已存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中“失控”概念基础薄弱，讨论者未先明确何为控制、何为丧失控制，存在概念空白。

Method: 以“设定和获取目标”锚定控制定义，借鉴控制论、管理控制和控制理论等基础领域构建控制框架，进而分析控制丧失的机制及AI的贡献。

Result: 建立了一个以目标设定、控制回路、必要多样性和目标对齐为核心要素的控制框架；指出失控风险在远低于超级智能水平的AI行为中已存在。

Conclusion: 人类（个体及群体）可能因AI行为而丧失不同程度的控制，且这种风险并非超级智能独有，早已存在。

Abstract: At present, loss of control risks have gained much prominence in public discussion, particularly in relation to AI, with extensive discourse present among academics, frontier labs, and even governments. However, in the existing literature, the concept seems to rest on surprisingly weak foundations, where even those that discuss loss of control extensively do not first establish what control is and what exactly is being lost. Our paper aims to address these gaps. We establish a working definition of control by anchoring it to the "setting and getting of goals". Then, we discuss various aspects of control, built on foundational concepts from related fields like cybernetics, management control, and control theory. This includes who (or what) can be in control, and the things they require to be in control, such as the ability to set goals, having a functional control loop, having requisite variety, and having sufficient goal alignment. Once a framework for control is established, we then discuss how control can be lost, how AIs can contribute to such loss of control, and offer relevant recommendations for how one can maintain control. One interesting consequence of our work is that humanity, as individuals and as groups, can lose varying degrees of control as a result of AI behaviour that is far below the level of superintelligence; the potential for loss of control scenarios (as we define them) already exist, and have existed for a long time.

</details>


### [218] [Occupational Prompting Reveals Cultural Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12443)
*Maksim E. Eren,Andrea Brennen,Ryan C. Barron,Eric Michalak*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究将文化提示框架扩展到职业提示，发现开源大语言模型在职业身份提示下回答价值观调查问题时，其回答仍集中在西方文化区域，但不同职业会引发该区域内有结构的价值偏移，表明职业提示并非中性角色标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注国籍/文化提示如何影响LLM的价值观回答，但社会角色（如职业身份）如何塑造LLM的文化价值模式尚不清楚，因此需要将文化提示框架扩展到职业提示领域。

Method: 基于综合价值观调查（IVS）问题构建评估流水线，将模型回答投影到Inglehart-Welzel二维文化空间；对开源LLM使用职业身份提示（如会计、教师、工程师、护士）进行价值观问题回答，并与国籍提示对比分析其文化地图定位。

Result: 开源LLM在职业提示下的回答仍集中在西方文化区域，但不同职业在该区域内产生不同的偏移，形成独特的职业偏差模式，表明职业提示引发了有结构的价值模式而非中性角色标签。

Conclusion: 职业提示同样能引发LLM产生有偏的文化价值模式，这扩展了基于调查的文化偏见评估方法，为研究职业角色如何影响LLM价值表达提供了新框架。

Abstract: Social roles shape expectations, priorities, and judgments, yet it remains unclear how large language models (LLMs) associate occupational identities with broader cultural value patterns. Prior work used nationality-based cultural prompting to study how LLM responses to value-survey questions align with human cultural benchmarks. In this paper, we extend that framework by replacing cultural prompting with occupational prompting to examine how professional-role cues influence value-survey responses in open-weight LLMs. Using a survey-grounded evaluation pipeline based on questions from the Integrated Values Surveys, we project model responses into the two-dimensional Inglehart--Welzel cultural space. We prompt open-weight LLMs to answer questions under occupational identities such as accountant, teacher, engineer, and nurse, and then analyze how these occupation-conditioned responses are positioned on the cultural map. Our results show that when open-weight LLMs are prompted with occupations rather than national identities, their responses remain within a broadly Western-leaning region of the cultural map. However, different occupations introduce shifts within this region, producing distinct occupational skews. This indicates that occupational prompts are not treated as neutral role labels, but instead elicit structured value patterns. These findings extend survey-based evaluation of cultural bias beyond nationality-based prompting and provide a framework for studying how occupational personas shape value expression in LLMs.

</details>


### [219] [Beyond Resilience -- A Conceptual Framework for Civic Ascent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12752)
*Alexandros Washburn,Carlo Lipizzi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了"公民上升"（civic ascent）概念，将其定义为城市在遭受冲击后功能能力比之前更高的状态，并构建了一个基于托波斯（物理结构）、诺莫斯（制度结构）、赫克西斯（公民判断）三个慢变量和德尔塔（情感通道）快变量的耦合系统框架，用于分析城市韧性，以纽约"9·11"事件为实证案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韧性文献仅将城市绩效衡量为恢复到冲击前基准线的"恢复"程度，这一概念过于薄弱。作者希望发展一个更强的概念——"公民上升"——作为关于耦合主体-环境系统行为学更广泛研究项目的一部分，城市是其中可获得的最深层的实证实例。

Method: 在行为学传统中构建概念框架，将城市视为三个慢状态变量（托波斯T、诺莫斯N、赫克西斯H）与一个快情感通道（德尔塔δ）的耦合系统。区分三种结构性压力：冲击、衰变和泄漏。上升条件为T、N、H的交叉耦合强化超过衰变和泄漏的总损失。通过归一化改进指数A(T)应用于复合公民绩效信号P(t)来测量冲击后上升，区分内在公民上升与人口驱动的增长。

Result: 提出了明确的上升条件数学形式（交叉耦合强化>衰变+泄漏损失），设计了基于133个关键绩效指标的纽约市公民数据地图作为操作化测量方案，并区分了内在公民上升与人口驱动增长两种不同的绩效提升来源。

Conclusion: 纽约"9·11"事件被提出作为主要实证案例；操作化测量方案在配套的纽约市公民数据地图中详细说明，并在论文2中执行。对城市贡献感兴趣的读者可独立阅读本文；对更大研究项目感兴趣的读者会发现本文是其形式核心。

Abstract: The resilience literature measures urban performance as recovery: the degree to which a city returns to its pre-shock baseline. This paper develops a stronger concept -- civic ascent -- as part of a broader research program on the ethology of coupled agent-environment systems, of which the city is the deepest available empirical instance. Civic ascent is defined as the condition in which a city emerges from shock with higher functional capacity than before. We develop a conceptual framework in the ethological tradition, treating the city as a coupled system of three slow state variables -- topos (physical structure), nomos (institutional structure), and hexis (civic judgment) -- together with a fast affective channel (delta) through which shocks to topos and nomos reach hexis. The framework distinguishes three structurally distinct pressures on civic systems: shocks (discontinuities in T or M), decay (continuous entropy), and leakage (active extraction of civic surplus into non-civic pools). The ascent condition is that reinforcement from cross-coupling of T, M, and H exceeds the combined loss from decay and leakage. Post-shock ascent is measured by a normalised improvement index A(T) applied to a composite civic performance signal P(t) constructed from scale-adjusted key performance indicators, distinguishing intrinsic civic ascent from demographically driven growth. New York City after September 11, 2001, is proposed as the primary empirical case; the operational measurement program is specified in the companion NYC Civic Data Map (Washburn 2026c, 133 KPIs) and executed in Paper 2. The reader for whom only the urban contribution is of interest will find it complete in itself; the reader interested in the larger program will find this paper its formal core.

</details>


### [220] [AiAWE: An Open-Source LLM Automated Writing Evaluation System Using LoRA-Adapted Instruction-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12801)
*John Maurice Gayed*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出AiAWE，一个基于LoRA微调指令优化大语言模型（Gemma-3-27B-it）的自动写作评估系统，在ETS的480篇托福独立写作数据集上，以120篇训练、360篇测试，取得RMSE 0.474、QWK 0.828、90.56%一致率，优于更大规模的LLaMA-3.3-70B及此前GPT-3.5基线。系统可在消费级服务器上运行并开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个开源、高效且准确的自动写作评估系统，探索轻量级开放权重LLM在LoRA微调下能否匹配或超越专有模型（如GPT-3.5）的评分性能，并降低部署成本。

Method: 使用LoRA适配器对指令微调的Gemma-3-27B-it和LLaMA-3.3-70B在120篇托福独立写作子集上进行微调，然后在360篇测试集上以相同量化推理设置评估，并与此前GPT-3.5基线对比。

Result: Gemma-3-27B-it取得RMSE 0.474、QWK 0.828、90.56%一致率，超越LLaMA-3.3-70B和GPT-3.5基线；开放权重LLM可匹敌甚至超越专有模型；模型规模与下游性能无可靠关联；相同LoRA超参在不同架构上产生不同适应行为。

Conclusion: 开放权重LLM通过LoRA微调可在自动评分任务上达到或超越专有模型，且无需大规模计算资源，可在消费级服务器上运行，为低成本、高精度的自动化评分提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This study presents AiAWE, an open-source automated writing evaluation system that scores argumentative essays using a LoRA-adapted instruction-tuned large language model (Gemma-3-27B-it). Using a proprietary Educational Testing Service (ETS) dataset of 480 TOEFL Independent Writing essays, we fine-tune Gemma-3-27B and LLaMA-3.3-70B under identical LoRA configurations on a 120-essay training subset and evaluate on the remaining 360 essays under identical inference quantization. The fine-tuned Gemma model achieves a root mean square error of 0.474, a quadratic weighted kappa of 0.828, and an agreement rate of 90.56% within +/- 0.5 of the human score, outperforming both the larger LLaMA-3.3-70B model and the fine-tuned GPT-3.5 baseline reported in prior work on the same dataset. Three findings are of broader interest: open-weight LLMs can match or exceed proprietary fine-tuning for rubric-aligned scoring; model scale is not a reliable predictor of downstream performance under LoRA adaptation; and identical LoRA hyperparameters produce qualitatively different adaptation behaviors across architectures. The production system runs on a consumer-grade server and is publicly accessible at https://app.awade.gec.waseda.ac.jp. LoRA adapters, application code, and fine-tuning YAMLs are publicly available through their respective repositories.

</details>


### [221] [Vocal Identity Under Siege by AI Voice Cloning Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12812)
*Jyh-An Lee,Xuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式AI语音克隆技术对声音身份保护带来的法律与伦理挑战，通过比较分析公开权、人格权和个人数据保护权三种法律框架，为应对AI时代声音身份问题提供法律基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT-4o声音与斯嘉丽·约翰逊高度相似等近期争议事件，凸显了AI语音克隆技术对声音独特价值的侵蚀，以及现有法律框架在保护声音身份方面的不足与混乱。

Method: 采用比较分析法，系统评估公开权、人格权和个人数据保护权三种主流法律框架在应对AI语音克隆威胁时的适用范围、救济措施和身后保护等维度。

Result: 三种法律框架各有优劣：公开权侧重商业利用保护，人格权强调身份尊严，个人数据保护权提供更灵活的数据控制机制，但均存在不同程度的适用局限性。

Conclusion: 现有法律框架难以完全应对生成式AI时代的声音身份保护挑战，亟需在跨法域比较基础上探索适应性法律解决方案。

Abstract: The advent of sophisticated AI-driven voice cloning has brought to the fore critical legal and ethical challenges regarding the protection of vocal identity. Prompted by recent controversies - including the striking resemblance between OpenAI's ChatGPT-4o voice and that of Scarlett Johansson - this article examines how generative AI technologies undermine the unique value of the human voice and further complicate the legal questions surrounding personality right. Through a comparative analysis, the paper evaluates three principal legal frameworks: the right of publicity, personality rights, and the personal data protection right. Each framework - rooted in different legal traditions o offers distinct strengths and limitations in addressing the threats posed by AI-generated voice cloning. By analysing these doctrines' scope, remedies, and posthumous protections, the study offers a foundation for understanding how existing legal approaches may be applied to the evolving challenges of vocal identity in the era of generative AI.

</details>


### [222] [Data Aphasia: An Institutional Counterfactual Study of the Stability of Academic Cognition Under Letter-Grade Evaluation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12946)
*Li Li,Yu Cao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据失语症"概念，通过对68场小学数学考试的模拟分析，发现等级制评价虽然减轻了负担，但导致信息熵下降约69%、诊断稳定性显著降低（最优聚类数K从4升至8、个体诊断身份一致性从95%降至62%、时间一致性波动于52%-96%），机制在于离散化压缩特征空间约19倍并制造伪异质性区域，最终提出双轨评价机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究等级制评价体系在实现减负目标的同时，是否影响教育系统对学生学业结构的稳定认知，并引入"数据失语症"概念来描述制度性数据呈现形式对诊断信息表达的限制。

Method: 基于75名小学生68场数学考试成绩数据，采用制度反事实模拟方法将百分制分数转换为A/B/C/D等级，从信息层面、结构层面和诊断层面进行系统性检验。

Result: 等级转换后信息熵下降约69%；全样本下等级制表面稳定（K=4），但移除一个极端锚点学生后最优K从4升至8，个体诊断身份一致性从95%降至62%；时间一致性在52%-96%间波动，远低于百分制的93%-96%。离散化在68场考试中压缩特征空间约19倍，标准化后产生大量伪异质性区域，使聚类边界对微小扰动高度敏感。

Conclusion: 提出双轨评价机制，为理解教育评价改革的认知代价提供了可检验的分析框架。

Abstract: Does the letter-grade evaluation system, while achieving its burden-reduction goals, affect the education system's stable understanding of students' academic structures? This paper introduces the concept of "data aphasia," referring to restrictions on diagnostic information expression caused by institutionally mandated forms of data presentation. Using data from 68 mathematics examinations administered to 75 primary school students, we employ an institutional counterfactual simulation method to convert percentage scores into A/B/C/D letter grades and conduct systematic tests at the information, structural, and diagnostic levels. Results show that information entropy decreases by approximately 69% after grade conversion; under the full sample, the letter-grade system appears superficially stable (K=4), but removing a single extreme anchor student causes the optimal K to increase from 4 to 8 and individual diagnostic identity consistency to fall from 95% to 62%; temporal consistency fluctuates between 52% and 96%, far below the 93%-96% baseline of the percentage system. Mechanism analysis indicates that discretization compresses the feature space by approximately nineteenfold across 68 examinations; after standardization, it creates extensive pseudo-heterogeneity regions, flattens density gradients, and makes clustering boundaries highly sensitive to minor perturbations. Based on these findings, this paper proposes a dual-track evaluation mechanism and provides a testable analytical framework for understanding the cognitive costs of educational evaluation reform.

</details>


### [223] [Democracy in the Era of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13026)
*Evangelos Pournaras,Srijoni Majumdar,Carina Hausladen,Dirk Helbing*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 《人工智能时代的民主手册》探讨了如何利用AI升级民主制度，而非简单评判AI的好坏。全书34章由59位跨学科作者撰写，涵盖AI赋能集体智慧、大型语言模型与 deliberative democracy、构建弹性自治系统、民主转型挑战及未来展望五个部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与民主的交互是当代最深刻的挑战之一。AI既带来克服民主长期挑战（如低参与度、代表性不足）的机遇，也带来隐私侵犯、偏见、操纵、虚假信息及影响选举等新风险。需要超越"AI对民主是好是坏"的简单化问题。

Method: 编写手册，汇集59位来自不同学科和全球各地的作者，通过34个章节系统性地从五个维度展开分析：AI赋能集体智能（第一部分）、大型语言模型与社交媒体对 deliberative democracy 的未来影响（第二部分）、AI在构建弹性自治系统中的作用（第三部分）、AI时代民主转型的挑战（第四部分）、以及更广泛的展望（第五部分）。

Result: 提出三个核心问题：如何利用AI升级民主及其基本原则？如何与AI互动以及基于什么条件？需要哪些新的价值观和设计原则来构建民主韧性？

Conclusion: AI时代需要重新构想民主与AI的相互作用，而非简单拥抱或排斥AI。重点在于建立民主韧性，通过新的设计原则和价值观引导AI服务民主。

Abstract: Interfacing Artificial Intelligence (AI) with democracy is one of the most profound challenges of our times. On the one hand, AI comes with opportunities to overcome long-standing challenges in democracy, such as low participation in deliberative and voting processes with poor representation of people. On the other hand, new risks arise from AI algorithms that are privacy-intrusive, biased, manipulative, spread misinformation and influence election results. Moving beyond the over-simplistic question of whether AI is good or bad for democracy, the Handbook on Democracy in the Era of Artificial Intelligence asks instead: how to upgrade democracies and the principles they are built on, using AI? How to engage with AI and on what terms? Which new values and design principles are required to build democratic resilience? In 34 chapters by 59 authors across the world from different disciplines, we explore how AI can empower collective intelligence for democracy (Part 1) and what is the future of deliberative democracy using large language models and social media (Part 2). We also illustrate the role of AI for building resilient self-governance systems (Part 3) and the challenges of transforming democracy in the age of AI (Part 4). We conclude with broader perspectives (Part 5) that re-imagine the interplay of democracy and AI.

</details>


### [224] [Fault Lines: Navigating Ethics and Responsible AI Where National Policy Meets Local Practice in Public Sector Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13039)
*Sitong Lyu,Shabnam Taghiyeva,Mohit Kukadia,Denis Newman-Griffis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过17场半结构化访谈，以英国特殊教育需求与残疾（SEND）领域为案例，分析了中央政策与地方实践之间的断层，识别出地方当局在部署负责任AI时面临的五大挑战（影子使用与数据隐私风险、市场-政府不对称、劳动力准备不足、缺乏标准化定义与测量、人类问责缺位），并提出政策调整与地方结构性改革并行的解决路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国政府虽在财政压力下积极推动AI以改革公共服务，但如何将国家层面的负责任AI愿景转化为地方实践尚不明确。本文旨在揭示这一国家-地方接口处负责任AI的解读与实施障碍，填补理论与实务之间的空白。

Method: 采用主题分析法，对17位政策制定者、从业者和第三部门专业人士进行半结构化访谈，以SEND这一高风险领域为案例，分析负责任AI在中央-地方接口处的实施状况。

Result: 识别出五大相互关联的挑战：AI影子使用与数据隐私风险、AI供应中的市场-政府不对称、劳动力准备不足、缺乏标准化定义与测量、以及人类问责缺位。参与者针对每项挑战提出了数据保护框架强化、市场-政府关系再平衡、劳动力能力提升等可行措施。

Conclusion: 负责任公共部门AI的实现不仅需要国家层面的政策调整，还需要对地方层级的机构能力、价值观和治理机制进行结构性改革。SEND案例表明，高风险决策加剧了问责、公平和人类监督方面的张力，暴露出基于原则的监管路径的局限性。

Abstract: The UK government has adopted a pro-AI stance to help transform public service delivery in the face of severe financial pressures, but the path to translate this vision into responsible AI practice remains ill-defined. While UK policy is often set at the national level, local authorities are responsible for most public service delivery, and the rapid advance of AI-first narratives in the public sector is exposing fault lines in knowledge and practice at this national-local interface. This paper examines how responsible AI is interpreted and implemented at the interface between the UK's central government and local authorities, taking the high-stakes area of Special Educational Needs and Disabilities (SEND) as a case study. We present a thematic analysis of 17 semi-structured interviews with policymakers, practitioners, and third-sector professionals to identify barriers and enabling conditions for responsible AI where national policy meets local practice. We identify five interconnected challenges facing local authorities: shadow usage of AI and data privacy risks, market-government asymmetry in AI provision, insufficient workforce readiness, a lack of standardised definitions and measurements, and gaps in human accountability. For each, participants proposed actionable steps, from strengthening data protection frameworks and rebalancing the market-government relationship to enhancing workforce capacity. Our examination of SEND brings these challenges into sharper focus, showing how high-stakes decisions affecting vulnerable children and families intensify tensions around accountability, fairness, and human oversight, exposing the limits of a principle-based regulatory approach. We argue that responsible public sector AI requires both national policy adjustments and structural reforms to institutional capacity, values, and governance mechanisms at the local level.

</details>


### [225] ["Is This Not Enough?": Asymmetries in Institutional Accountability and Collective Sensemaking in the Case of Canada's Algorithmic Visa Triage System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13071)
*Dipto Das,Matthew Tamura,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过分析加拿大移民局临时居民签证分诊系统的算法影响评估（AIA）以及Reddit上的申请人讨论，揭示了加拿大签证系统中算法问责机制在制度层面和申请人实际体验之间的结构性不对称，包括认知不对称、管辖权不对称和时间-关系不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究算法问责在加拿大签证系统中如何被制度性地构建以及被申请人实际体验，揭示制度设计与用户体验之间的结构性差异。

Method: 使用适用于公共部门的算法决策分析框架（ADMAPS）分析IRCC的AIA，并结合混合方法分析Reddit上的申请人讨论。

Result: 识别出三种不对称：认知不对称（决策逻辑的可及性差异）、管辖权不对称（地缘政治定位导致的暴露差异）、时间-关系不对称（等待和不确定性的体验差异）。

Conclusion: 跨国移民背景下的算法治理系统产生了制度披露框架无法捕捉的结构性不对称，扩展ADMAPS框架可以解释这些问责机制的不均匀转化。

Abstract: This paper examines how algorithmic accountability in Canada's visa system is articulated institutionally and experienced by applicants across borders. We analyzed Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada (IRCC)'s Algorithmic Impact Assessment (AIA) for the temporary resident visa (TRV) triage system using the algorithmic decision-making adapted for the public sector (ADMAPS) framework and analyzed Reddit discussions among applicants using a mixed-methods approach. We show that while institutional artifacts emphasize transparency, procedural safeguards, and bounded impacts, applicants engage in collective sensemaking to interpret opaque decisions, often relying on peer knowledge amid uncertainty. We identify three asymmetries between how institutional accountability is structured and how people perceive the process: epistemic asymmetry in access to decision logic, jurisdictional asymmetry in exposure shaped by geopolitical positioning, and temporal--relational asymmetry in how waiting and uncertainty are experienced. We emphasize why it is important to shift attention from institutional design to the uneven distribution of experiences with public-sector algorithmic governance. Together, these contributions demonstrate how algorithmic governance systems in the context of transnational migration produce structured asymmetries not captured by institutional disclosure frameworks, and how extending ADMAPS can account for those uneven translations of accountability.

</details>


### [226] [Structuring Transparency: Developing Domain-Specific Generative AI Declaration Frameworks in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13389)
*Nicholas Micallef,Olga Petrovska*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对计算机科学领域的任务特定型AI使用声明框架，将写作和编程任务中的GenAI使用按认知阶段分类（如结构规划vs文本生成、代码改进vs代码生成），以替代传统的二元声明，促进学术诚信和学生反思。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用、二元的AI使用声明（如"我使用了GenAI"）无法捕捉不同学术任务中AI工具应用的细微差别，不足以维护学术诚信和提升AI素养，需要更精细的声明结构。

Method: 基于现有GenAI使用分类法，为计算机科学系开发了两种任务特定的声明框架：一种针对写作活动，一种针对编程评估，覆盖从结构规划到内容生成/代码生成等不同认知和发展阶段。

Result: 提出了任务特定型声明框架的设计制品，能够区分AI在不同认知阶段的使用（如结构规划vs文本生成，代码改进vs代码生成），鼓励学生反思学习过程并明确可接受协助与学术不端之间的界限。

Conclusion: 领域特定的AI使用声明框架有助于培养更诚实的评估环境，并为学生在未来职业中记录GenAI工作流奠定基础，可推广至计算机科学及其他学科。

Abstract: As Generative AI (GenAI) disrupts higher education, institutions increasingly require students to declare AI use. However, generic, binary declarations (e.g., "I used GenAI") fail to capture the nuanced application of these tools in different academic tasks. Establishing transparency is key to protecting academic integrity, promoting AI literacy, and shifting the focus from policing to professional practice. In response, this paper contributes a design artefact and an accompanying position: a framework of two task-specific declaration structures, one for writing-focused activities and one for coding assessments, developed for a Computer Science department on the basis of an existing taxonomy of GenAI usage, together with an argument that task-specific disclosure is needed to move beyond binary declarations. By categorising AI usage across specific cognitive and developmental stages, such as structural planning vs. Textual Content Generation, or code improvement vs. code generation, the framework encourages students to reflect on their own learning process and clarifies the boundary between acceptable assistance and academic misconduct. We propose this domain-specific approach as a foundation for fostering more honest assessment in Computer Science and other disciplines, aiming to better prepare students for professional environments where documenting GenAI workflows might be an essential job requirement.

</details>


### [227] [Exploring Systems-Thinking Approaches to Loss of Control Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13474)
*Aurelio Carlucci,Sean P. Fillingham,James Walpole,Jakub Kryś*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了内部部署的智能体AI系统（用于编程和研究）带来的社会技术控制问题，指出这种失控风险超出了模型行为本身。通过运用STECA、STPA和FRAM三种系统安全方法分析前沿实验室的编程智能体场景，发现模型级评估可能遗漏治理责任反馈回路不可验证、监控延迟导致控制失效、日常操作变异侵蚀防护措施等风险。主张将模型评估与系统级危险分析和操作保障相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部部署的智能体AI系统在编程和研究中的应用带来了社会技术控制问题，现有模型级评估可能无法全面识别相关风险，需要探索系统安全方法是否能补充发现被遗漏的风险。

Method: 从公开资料重构前沿实验室编程智能体场景，应用STECA（系统理论事件链分析）、STPA（系统理论过程分析）和FRAM（功能共振分析方法）三种系统安全方法进行分析。

Result: 三种方法发现了互补的风险：治理责任和反馈回路难以外部验证；监控和干预的延迟导致控制措施失效；日常操作的可变性逐渐削弱防护措施的校准和独立性。

Conclusion: 前沿AI风险管理应将模型级评估与系统级危险分析和操作保障相结合，持续追踪控制措施的有效性。

Abstract: Internal deployment of agentic AI systems for coding and research creates a sociotechnical control problem that extends beyond model behaviour. We treat internal-deployment Loss of Control as the inability to reliably constrain, audit, reverse, or halt AI-mediated changes to code, infrastructure, evaluation, or deployment processes in time to prevent serious organisational or societal harms. We ask whether established systems-safety methods can identify risks that model-level evaluations may miss. Using a generic frontier-lab coding-agent scenario reconstructed from public materials, we apply STECA, STPA, and FRAM. The analyses surface complementary findings: published frameworks can leave governance responsibilities and feedback loops externally unverifiable; delays in monitoring and intervention can make otherwise valid control actions ineffective; and routine operational variability can gradually erode the calibration and independence of safeguards. We argue that frontier-AI risk management should pair model-focused evaluations with systems-level hazard analysis and operational assurance that tracks whether controls remain effective over time.

</details>


### [228] [Digital Twin-Based Simulation for Predictive Decision-Making in Waterway Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13492)
*Matthijs Jansen op de Haar,Daniel Frutos Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究利用数字孪生技术在内河航道网络中进行货运路线规划，以应对水位不确定性。通过专家访谈识别三种应急场景，并构建时间切片仿真环境，将预测决策方法与反应式方法进行对比。结果表明，在70%-100%预测精度下，预测模型能显著降低运营成本和模态转换，平均减少28.3%的燃油相关成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能充分考虑气候引起的水位波动，导致运营成本增加和排放上升；且现有方法多依赖反应式对策来维持韧性，缺乏预测性决策能力。

Method: 通过六次领域专家访谈识别三种常见应急场景及运营响应方案；将场景纳入时间切片仿真环境，比较数字孪生赋能的预测决策与反应式方法的性能差异；仿真结果经领域专家评估其实践相关性。

Result: 预测模型在70%-100%预测精度范围内显著降低运营成本和模态转换；平均减少28.3%的燃油相关成本（通过减少船舶总航行距离实现）。

Conclusion: 数字孪生赋能的预测性建模方法相比反应式方法具有显著优势，能在极端条件下提升内河货运网络的运营效率和经济性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the potential of a Digital Twin (DT) for freight routing across inland waterway networks under uncertain water-level conditions. Existing approaches insufficiently account for increasing climate-induced volatility in water levels, which often result in higher operational costs and emissions due to the need for more expensive transport alternatives, such as road transport. These existing methods often rely on reactive countermeasures to remain resilient.
  To address these limitations, six interviews with experts in domains related to inland shipping were conducted to identify three common contingency scenarios and appropriate operational responses. These scenarios were subsequently incorporated into a time-sliced simulation environment in which predictive decision-making, enabled by a DT environment, was compared against reactive approaches. The results demonstrate that predictive modeling substantially reduces operational costs and modal shifts at prediction accuracies between 70% and 100%, despite extreme conditions. In addition, the predictive model achieves an average 28.3% reduction in fuel-related costs by reducing the total distance ships travel. The simulation outcomes were evaluated together with domain experts to assess the practical relevance and applicability of the proposed DT-enabled approach.

</details>


### [229] [The Tone of Awareness: Topic, Sentiment, and Toxicity Maps During Mental Health Month on TikTok](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13581)
*Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Andreia Sofia Teixeira,Pranay Gundala Reddy,Anindya Mondal,Kleber Andrade Oliveira,Filipi Nascimento Silva*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过分析2023-2024年心理健康意识月期间TikTok上的28,341个视频和80,130条评论，本研究使用BERTopic提取主题，并分别量化视频内容和评论的情感（XLM-T）与毒性（Detoxify），发现主题稳定但参与度高度不均，视频情感偏负面而评论转向混合或正面，毒性在评论中呈现更长尾分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TikTok对心理健康的影响引发担忧，但关于创作者如何构建相关内容以及受众如何接收的信息仍十分匮乏。

Method: 通过TikTok Research API收集2023-2024年心理健康意识月期间的视频及评论数据；使用BERTopic和log-odds关键词提取主题；分别对视频转录和评论进行情感分析（XLM-T）和毒性检测（Detoxify）。

Result: 发现跨年稳定的重复主题（包括临床状况、情感表露、自我关怀和宣传活动），参与度高度集中于少数主题；视频情感在情绪化主题上偏负面，评论则转向混合或正面极性（尤其自杀预防）；毒性中位数较低，但评论中存在更长尾的异常值，集中于特定主题（如"Duet""Suicide Prevention""Psychisch"）。

Conclusion: 研究提供了心理健康意识月期间TikTok心理健康话语的主题级分解，揭示了内容生产与受众接收之间的情感和毒性差异。

Abstract: Despite raising concerns about the mental health effects associated with the usage of TikTok, little is known about how related content is framed by creators and received by audiences. We collect the content of 28,341 TikTok videos and 80,130 comments from Mental Health Awareness Month (May) in 2023 and 2024 via the TikTok Research API, and study how the tone of awareness varies across topics and years. We characterize "tone" as the emotional and interpersonal framing of mental health discourse, operationalized through sentiment and toxicity measures. We extract topics from video text using BERTopic and log-odds keywords, then quantify topic-conditioned sentiment (XLM-T) and toxicity (Detoxify) separately for video transcriptions and comments. Sentiment captures the affective valence of content, while toxicity reflects the presence of harmful or abusive language. We find a stable set of recurring themes across years, spanning clinical conditions, emotional disclosure, self-care, and campaign-oriented content, with engagement highly skewed toward a small subset of topics. All sentiment and toxicity analyses are computed separately for video content and comments, allowing us to distinguish between content production and audience reception. Sentiment in videos is often negative for emotionally charged topics, while comments tend to shift toward more mixed or positive polarity, especially for suicide prevention. Toxicity is low in median overall, but exhibits longer-tailed outliers in comments than in videos that are more pronounced in comments and concentrated in specific topics (e.g., "Duet", "Suicide Prevention", and "Psychisch"). Overall, our results provide a topic-level decomposition of mental health discourse on TikTok during awareness-month campaigns.

</details>


### [230] [C-QUERI: Congressional Questions, Exchanges, and Responses in Institutions Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21548)
*Manjari Rudra,Daniel Magleby,Sujoy Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个从非结构化听证会记录中提取问答对的流程，构建了第108-117届国会委员会听证数据集，发现仅凭提问者的问题即可预测其所属党派，揭示了跨党派的系统性提问策略差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治访谈和听证会中的提问往往服务于推进党派叙事和塑造公众认知等战略目的，但由于缺乏大规模数据集，这些战略层面尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 开发了一个从非结构化听证会记录中提取问答对的流程，构建了第108-117届国会委员会听证数据集，并基于提问内容进行党派归属预测分析。

Result: 发现提问者的党派归属可以仅从其问题本身进行预测，表明不同党派在提问策略上存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 该数据集和方法不仅推进了国会政治研究，还为分析各类访谈场景中的问答行为提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Questions in political interviews and hearings serve strategic purposes beyond information gathering including advancing partisan narratives and shaping public perceptions. However, these strategic aspects remain understudied due to the lack of large-scale datasets for studying such discourse. Congressional hearings provide an especially rich and tractable site for studying political questioning: Interactions are structured by formal rules, witnesses are obliged to respond, and members with different political affiliations are guaranteed opportunities to ask questions, enabling comparisons of behaviors across the political spectrum.
  We develop a pipeline to extract question-answer pairs from unstructured hearing transcripts and construct a novel dataset of committee hearings from the 108th--117th Congress. Our analysis reveals systematic differences in questioning strategies across parties, by showing the party affiliation of questioners can be predicted from their questions alone. Our dataset and methods not only advance the study of congressional politics, but also provide a general framework for analyzing question-answering across interview-like settings.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [A quantum-like benchmark for context-sensitive associative memory with adaptive plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12449)
*Yashine H. Goolam Hossen,Lea Gassab,Travis J. A. Craddock*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个顺序敏感的自适应可塑性基准测试，用于评估联想记忆模型在弱结构支持条件下的表现。研究发现，量子联想记忆模型与经典马尔可夫率控制模型相比，并没有表现出普遍的优势，但前者在顺序敏感性和阶段依赖组织方面更为稳健。模型类别的区别应基于多目标特征（包括回忆能力、时间组织和上下文敏感性）而非单一回忆分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习与记忆需要在可塑性和稳定性之间取得平衡。现有联想记忆模型在固定背景连接已部分承载任务时，难以区分真正的回忆动力学与结构辅助效应。因此需要一种受控基准来测试模型在弱支持条件下的上下文敏感记忆动力学。

Method: 设计顺序敏感的自适应可塑性基准测试，比较量子联想记忆模型与无相位实值模型和马尔可夫率控制模型，在相同任务调度、扰动配置、弱支持条件和可塑性设置下进行阶乘比较。先筛选弱结构支持，再固定保守操作点进行模型系列和可塑性机制的比较。

Result: 有用弱支持区间狭窄且非单调；无可塑性消融实验中弱结构无法挽救回忆；大多数有用回忆增益来自自适应可塑性（特别是稳态稳定化）；马尔可夫率控制在原始回忆强度上更优，但量子模型更一致地保持顺序敏感性和阶段依赖组织；未发现普遍量子类优势。

Conclusion: 模型类别的区分应基于多目标特征（回忆能力、时间组织、上下文敏感性）而非单一回忆分数。该基准为研究弱支持、受控可塑性和匹配经典比较条件下的上下文敏感记忆动力学提供了受控框架。

Abstract: Learning and memory require a balance between plasticity and stability: synaptic connections must encode new information without collapsing, saturating, or erasing previously useful structure. Associative-memory models can appear to learn successfully when fixed background connectivity already carries part of the task, making it difficult to distinguish genuine recall dynamics from structural assistance. We test this issue using an order-sensitive adaptive-plasticity benchmark for staged associative recall. The benchmark compares a quantum-like associative-memory model with matched real-valued no-phase and Markov-rate controls under the same task schedule, perturbation profiles, weak-support conditions, and plasticity settings. Here, "quantum-like" refers to the modeling formalism, not to a biological claim about quantum computation. We first screen weak structural support and then fix a conservative operating point for factorial comparisons across model families and plasticity mechanisms. The useful weak-support regime is narrow and non-monotonic. Weak structure alone does not rescue recall in the no-plasticity ablation, whereas most useful recall gains arise from adaptive plasticity, especially homeostatic stabilization. The Markov-rate control often achieves stronger raw recall, but the quantum-like model more consistently preserves order sensitivity and stage-dependent organization. These results do not support a universal quantum-like advantage. Instead, they show that model classes are better distinguished by a multi-objective profile combining recall, temporal organization, and context sensitivity than by any single recall score. The benchmark therefore provides a controlled framework for studying context-sensitive memory dynamics under weak support, regulated plasticity, and matched classical comparison.

</details>


### [232] [Multifractal human signals at the edge of life reveal a heart-brain anti-correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12600)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,Maria Eloá do Ó,Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Mauro Copelli,G. Camelo-Neto,Pedro V. Carelli*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过多重分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）量化终末期患者同步脑电图和心电图时间序列的复杂性时间演化，发现神经活动呈现多重分形性坍缩（向更受限状态转变），而心脏信号出现异常谱宽展宽（非线性波动增加、动力学不稳定性增强），两者负相关表明神经-心脏系统的有效功能解耦和反相关动力学。这反映了体-脑崩溃过程，即外周功能障碍逐渐压倒中枢调控，指向一种极端形式的跨系统解整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类神经生理功能在终末期的崩溃机制，通过非线性动力学视角分析神经与心脏系统在临终阶段的耦合变化，理解死亡过程中系统间协调解体的本质。

Method: 采用多重分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）对终末期患者同步采集的脑电图（EEG）和心电图（ECG）时间序列进行分析，量化其多重分形谱宽度的时变特征，并计算神经与心脏系统谱宽之间的相关性。

Result: 神经活动呈现多重分形谱坍缩（向受限状态转变），心脏信号呈现异常谱宽展宽（非线性波动增强）；两者谱宽呈负相关，表明有效功能解耦和反相关动力学；该模式与体-脑崩溃一致，而非均匀的生理衰退。

Conclusion: 临终过程代表了跨系统解整合的极端形式，表现为通常维持整合生理功能的分层协调的崩溃；观察到的神经-心脏逆动态模式可能普遍存在于外周起源的约束驱动过程中。

Abstract: This study investigates the terminal breakdown of human neurophysiological function through the lens of non-linear dynamics by analyzing the multifractal spectrum. Using Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (MF-DFA), we quantify the temporal evolution of complexity in synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) time series from patients in the terminal stage. Our results reveal a marked divergence in multifractal spectrum width: while neural activity exhibits a collapse of multifractality toward a more constrained state, cardiac signals undergo anomalous spectral broadening, indicating increased non-linear fluctuations and dynamical instability. A negative correlation between these spectral widths suggests effective functional decoupling and the emergence of anti-correlated dynamics between neural and cardiac systems. Rather than reflecting a uniform physiological decline, this divergence is consistent with a body-to-brain breakdown in which peripheral dysfunction progressively overwhelms central regulatory processes. In a broader context, the observed opposing trends resemble patterns reported in other body-driven adaptive processes, suggesting that inverse dynamics across coupled systems may emerge when constraints originate from peripheral rather than central mechanisms. Ultimately, the dying process appears to represent an extreme form of cross-system disintegration, marked by the collapse of the hierarchical coordination that normally sustains integrated physiological function.

</details>


### [233] [Phase model analysis of the effect of M-current on neural synchrony in hippocampal networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12684)
*Megha Manoj,Sue Ann Campbell*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过计算模型研究乙酰胆碱（ACh）在海马神经集群形成中的双向调节作用：低ACh水平促进网络完全同步（支持记忆巩固），高ACh水平使网络去同步化为多个稳定的对称集群（支持记忆编码）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证ACh双向调节假说：高ACh水平（主动探索和REM睡眠）促进记忆编码，低ACh水平（安静清醒和慢波睡眠）支持记忆巩固。研究ACh通过调节M电流影响神经元同步性和神经集群形成的机制。

Method: 构建包含锥体神经元和慢速电压依赖性非失活钾电流（M电流，受ACh下调）的网络模型；利用弱耦合相模型约化分析神经元对同步性；研究全局均匀、距离对称依赖和最近邻三种耦合架构下的对称集群解。

Result: 低ACh（高M电流）条件下网络能够完全同步；高ACh（低M电流）条件下网络去同步化为多个稳定的对称集群解，代表不同的神经集群。

Conclusion: ACh通过调节M电流控制海马网络的同步化程度，从而实现记忆编码与巩固两种功能的切换，为ACh双向调节假说提供了计算层面的支持。

Abstract: Neural assemblies, transiently coordinated groups of neurons, observed in the hippocampus are thought to underlie the formation of episodic memories. Acetylcholine (ACh), a neuromodulator, that is received by the hippocampus, plays a critical role in memory and learning. A well supported hypothesis suggests that high levels of ACh during active exploration and rapid eye movement (REM) sleep promote memory encoding, while low levels during quiet waking and slow-wave sleep (SWS) support memory consolidation. We study this bidirectional role of ACh in neural assembly formation through its effect on the synchrony among neurons. We consider a network model of pyramidal neurons, each equipped with a slow, voltage-dependent, non-inactivating potassium current (M-current), which is downregulated in the presence of ACh. Neural assemblies are represented as cluster solutions to this system. Using a one-dimensional phase model reduction of a pair of weakly coupled pyramidal neurons under different levels of the M-current, we predict the symmetric cluster solutions that may emerge in larger networks equipped with all-to-all globally homogeneous, symmetric distance-dependent and nearest-neighbours coupling architectures. We find that under low ACh conditions, the network can fully synchronize, whereas high levels can desynchronize the network into multiple stable symmetric cluster solutions representing distinct neural assemblies.

</details>


### [234] [Deep Sleep Classification via EEG Signal Criticality: A Passive BCI Approach for Sleep-Improvement Neurofeedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13017)
*Stanisław Narębski,Tomasz Komendziński,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文利用去趋势波动分析（DFA）提取的临界性特征，通过多种分类器对深度睡眠（N3）阶段进行自动分期，发现朴素贝叶斯分类器在平衡准确率（87.17%）上显著优于深度神经网络（81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%），证实DFA临界性特征位于非线性流形上，为被动脑机接口中的状态依赖神经反馈提供了高精度解码方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现自动睡眠分期是被动脑机接口（pBCI）的基础应用，通过解码自发神经状态实现独立于用户意图的闭环干预；本研究旨在评估基于去趋势波动分析（DFA）的临界性特征对深度睡眠（N3）阶段的特异性识别能力，并找到最优的“状态感知”分类引擎用于神经反馈。

Method: 分析了290名老年女性的347,232个EEG epochs，使用UMAP流形学习可视化状态转换；随后通过10折交叉验证对六种分类器进行基准测试，包括朴素贝叶斯、全连接深度神经网络（FNN）、随机森林、线性判别分析（LDA）和支持向量机（SVM），以平衡准确率评估性能。

Result: 朴素贝叶斯取得最高平均平衡准确率（87.17%±0.24%），显著优于FNN（81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%）；线性模型表现较差（LDA: 57.21%；SVM: 51.01%），表明DFA临界性特征位于独特的非线性流形上。

Conclusion: 基于EEG临界性的概率解码为pBCI提供了高精度感知机制；该稳健的分类流程支持开发状态依赖的神经反馈（如靶向听觉刺激）以促进认知恢复。

Abstract: Automated sleep staging is a fundamental application of passive Brain-Computer Interfaces (pBCI), decoding spontaneous neural states to enable closed-loop interventions independent of user intent. This study evaluates criticality features derived from Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (DFA) for the specific identification of deep sleep (N3).
  We analyzed $347,232$ EEG epochs from $290$ older women using UMAP manifold learning to visualize state transitions. Subsequently, six classifiers were benchmarked via 10-fold cross-validation, using balanced accuracy to determine the optimal "state-sensing" engine for neurofeedback.Naive Bayes achieved the highest mean balanced accuracy ($87.17\% \pm 0.24\%$), significantly outperforming a fully connected deep neural network (FNN: $81.58\%$) and Random Forest ($80.97\%$). Linear models (LDA: $57.21\%$; SVM: $51.01\%$) performed poorly, indicating that DFA-derived criticality features reside on a distinct, non-linear manifold.
  Probabilistic decoding of EEG criticality provides a high-accuracy sensing mechanism for pBCIs. This robust classification pipeline supports the development of state-dependent neurofeedback, such as targeted auditory stimulation, to enhance cognitive recovery.

</details>


### [235] [Including the Cost of Irreducible Uncertainty in the Policy Compression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13132)
*Álvaro Garrido-Pérez,Pieter Simoens,Amrapali Pednekar,Yara Khaluf*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出在策略压缩框架中增加条件熵成本项，以更完整地建模人类决策中的认知代价，从而更好地解释决策偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略压缩框架将条件熵（给定状态下选择哪个动作的不可约不确定性）视为无代价，但实证表明它影响反应时，因此该框架对人类决策偏差的建模不完整。

Method: 将认知代价定义为策略复杂度（状态与动作的互信息）与加权条件熵之和，引入新参数η控制条件熵权重；推导了新的最优策略形式。

Result: 最优策略保持标准指数形式，但随着η增大变得更尖锐，使策略精度可以更独立于奖励敏感性变化；标准框架可能低估动作选择的认知代价。

Conclusion: 扩展后的框架能更好地解释人类决策偏差，但增加了模型拟合的复杂度，需要未来工作解决。

Abstract: AI decision-support systems can benefit from anticipating biases in human decision-making. Many such biases may arise from human cognitive limitations. The policy compression framework models decision-making as a trade-off between reward maximization and the cognitive cost of encoding state-dependent action policies, formalized as the mutual information between states and actions (policy complexity). We argue that this account is incomplete because it treats conditional entropy--the irreducible uncertainty about which action should be selected given a state--as costless, even though empirical evidence suggests that it modulates reaction times. We therefore extend the framework by defining cognitive cost as the sum of policy complexity and a weighted conditional-entropy term, governed by a new parameter, $η$. The resulting optimal policy retains the standard exponential form but becomes sharper as $η$ increases, allowing policy precision to vary more independently of reward sensitivity. This modification implies that the standard policy compression framework may underestimate the cognitive cost of action selection, and it has the potential to better account for biases in human decision-making. At the same time, it introduces additional complexity for fitting the model to human data, which future work will need to address.

</details>


### [236] [Robust State-space Reconstruction of Brain Dynamics via Bootstrap Monte Carlo SSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00011)
*Sir-Lord Wiafe,Carter Hinsley,Vince D. Calhoun*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出BMC-SSA方法，结合蒙特卡罗奇异谱分析与自举稳定性，从短时、高噪声、粗采样的时间序列（如fMRI）中提取可靠的振荡模式，提升延迟坐标嵌入的稳定性和确定性，为非线性动力学研究提供鲁棒框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统延迟坐标嵌入理论要求长时、无噪声记录，但实际数据（如神经影像fMRI）通常短时、高噪声、采样粗糙，且含有自相关背景结构，掩盖振荡成分并破坏嵌入稳定性，亟需一种能在有限信号条件下可靠提取振荡模式的方法。

Method: 将蒙特卡罗奇异谱分析（MC-SSA）与自举（bootstrap）稳定性相结合，通过重采样数据对振荡模式进行统计显著性检验和可重复性评估，仅保留统计支持且可复现的振荡分量，重构出强调可靠振荡结构的信号。

Result: BMC-SSA能够增强重构信号的确定性，稳定后续嵌入过程；在fMRI数据上，提高了功能指标的可靠性，并揭示了状态空间动力学的组间差异。

Conclusion: BMC-SSA为含噪声、有限长度信号的鲁棒嵌入提供了一个通用框架，有效提升了非线性动力学分析的稳定性与可解释性。

Abstract: Reconstructing latent state-space geometry from time series provides a powerful route to studying nonlinear dynamics across complex systems. Delay-coordinate embedding provides the theoretical basis but assumes long, noise-free recordings, which many domains violate. In many real-world domains, recordings are short, noisy, and coarsely sampled; in neuroimaging, for example, fMRI additionally contains autocorrelated background structure that can obscure oscillatory components and destabilize embeddings. We propose bootstrap Monte Carlo singular spectrum analysis (BMC-SSA), which combines Monte Carlo SSA with bootstrap stability to retain oscillatory modes that are statistically supported and reproducible across resampled data. This produces reconstructions that emphasize reliable oscillatory structure, enhancing determinism and stabilizing subsequent embeddings. Our results show that BMC-SSA improves the reliability of functional measures and uncovers differences in state-space dynamics in fMRI, offering a general framework for robust embedding of noisy, finite signals.

</details>


### [237] [Cognitive Field Theory of Learning, Inference, and Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10221)
*Byung Gyu Chae*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个认知场论（Cognitive Field Theory），将认知描述为一种集体非平衡现象，受学习到的认知流形的几何和弛豫谱控制。通过引入记忆修饰的认知场方程，推导出时间尺度态密度（TDOS）作为核心动力学描述符，揭示了弱阻尼集体模式累积如何抑制遗忘、增强敏感性并驱动系统进入近临界保护态，实现长期语境保持和集体认知一致性。该框架统一描述了学习、记忆、推理、自我意识和涌现认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物与人工系统中的学习、推理、记忆和涌现现象通常使用不同的理论框架描述，缺乏统一的动力学理论来解释这些认知过程的集体组织机制。

Method: 从自适应黎曼认知流形上的随机认知场方程出发，推导出包含非局部记忆核和延迟自能反馈的记忆修饰认知场方程，并通过时间尺度态密度（TDOS）表征集体弛豫模式的分布。

Result: 弱阻尼集体模式的累积可以抑制认知遗忘间隔、增强集体易感性，并驱动系统进入受保护的近临界状态，表现出长期语境保持和集体认知一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架以记忆修饰认知场的集体组织为基础，为学习、记忆、推理、自我意识和涌现认知提供了一个统一的动力学描述。

Abstract: Learning, inference, memory, and emergence in biological and artificial systems are often described using disparate theoretical frameworks. Here we develop a cognitive field theory in which cognition is described as a collective nonequilibrium phenomenon governed by the geometry and relaxation spectrum of a learned cognitive manifold. Starting from a stochastic cognitive-field equation on an adaptive Riemannian cognitive manifold, we derive a memory-dressed cognitive field equation incorporating nonlocal memory kernels and retarded self-energy feedback. We show that the local stability structure of learned cognitive geometry generates a spectrum of collective relaxation modes whose distribution is characterized by a time-scale density of states (TDOS). The TDOS provides a fundamental dynamical descriptor of cognition and determines the emergent memory kernel, collective response, and infrared temporal organization of the cognitive field. The accumulation of weakly damped collective modes suppresses the cognitive forgetting gap, enhances collective susceptibility, and drives the system toward a protected near-critical regime characterized by long-time contextual persistence and collective cognitive coherence. The resulting framework provides a unified dynamical description of learning, memory, inference, selfhood, and emergent cognition in terms of the collective organization of a memory-dressed cognitive field.

</details>


### [238] [Fusion Learning from Dynamic Functional Connectivity: Combining the Amplitude and Phase of fMRI Signals to Identify Brain Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24603)
*Jinlong Hu,Jiatong Huang,Zijian Cai*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多尺度融合学习框架(MSFL)，通过结合滑动窗口相关(SWC)和相位同步(PS)两种互补的动态功能连接特征，分别捕获幅度相关和相位相干信息，在ASD和MDD分类任务上显著优于现有模型，且SHAP解释分析验证了两种特征均有助于脑疾病检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动态功能连接(dFC)构建主要基于滑动窗口相关(SWC)方法，仅利用fMRI信号的幅度时间序列计算相关系数，忽略了信号的相位信息。为了更全面地捕捉脑功能动态连接特征以提升脑疾病检测性能，作者提出融合幅度和相位两种互补信息。

Method: 提出多尺度融合学习框架(MSFL)，同时提取两种互补的dFC特征：SWC特征（捕获幅度相关性）和相位同步(PS)特征（捕获相位相干性），通过多尺度融合策略进行整合，并利用SHAP框架进行模型解释分析。

Result: 在ABIDE I数据集（自闭症谱系障碍分类）和REST-meta-MDD数据集（重度抑郁症分类）上，MSFL显著优于现有对比模型；SHAP分析显示SWC和PS两种dFC特征均对疾病检测有贡献。

Conclusion: 融合幅度信息与相位信息的双特征动态功能连接方法能有效提升脑疾病分类性能，为fMRI-based脑疾病诊断提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Dynamic functional connectivity (dFC) derived from resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has been extensively utilized in brain science research. The sliding window correlation (SWC) method is a widely used approach for constructing dFC by computing correlation coefficients between amplitude time series of signals from pairs of brain regions. In this study, we propose an integrated approach that incorporates both amplitude and phase information of fMRI signals to improve the detection of brain disorders. Specifically, we introduce a multi-scale fusion learning framework, namely MSFL, which leverages two complementary dFC features derived from SWC and phase synchronization (PS). Here, SWC captures amplitude correlations, while PS measures phase coherence within dFC. We evaluated the efficacy of MSFL in classifying autism spectrum disorder and major depressive disorder using two publicly available datasets: ABIDE I and REST-meta-MDD, respectively. The results indicate that MSFL significantly outperforms existing comparative models. Moreover, we performed model explanation analysis using the SHAP framework, which showed that both types of dFC features from SWC and PS contribute to detecting brain disorders.

</details>


### [239] [Contextual Role Modulates Object Representational Geometry in the Human Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23111)
*Julien Dirani,Shankar Chawla,Leila Wehbe,Bradford Z. Mahon*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过自然观影fMRI实验发现，人脑对同一物体的神经表征会根据其在不同语境中的角色（主动目标 vs 被动场景元素）进行动态重构：目标物体在顶叶动作网络中以动作可供性和手姿势维度组织，被动物体在枕颞网络中以语义维度组织，而视觉表征结构保持跨语境不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人脑如何在保持物体表征不变性的同时，根据同一物体在不同语境中的角色变化（如被动场景元素 vs 主动行为目标）动态重映射其神经表征。

Method: 使用fMRI扫描被试观看自然电影片段，分析同一物体在作为被动场景元素与作为目标导向行为目标两种语境下的神经表征模式，比较不同脑区的表征几何结构。

Result: 1) 动作目标激活顶叶动作网络（缘上回、中央后回），被动物体激活枕颞视觉网络；2) 表征几何呈现双重分离：目标物体按动作可供性和手姿势维度组织，被动物体按语义维度组织；3) 视觉表征结构跨语境不变；4) 语境选择性网络外的脑区保持表征不变性。

Conclusion: 人脑在同一表征系统的不同层级上同时实现了灵活性与不变性：语境选择性网络根据物体角色动态重映射表征内容，而视觉结构保持跨语境不变，揭示了自然场景中物体表征随语境角色实时变化的神经机制。

Abstract: The human brain represents objects in a way that is both invariant across instances and flexible enough to support different contexts and tasks. Yet it remains unknown how object representations are dynamically remapped as the same object shifts across contextual roles. Using fMRI during naturalistic movie viewing we investigated how the same objects are represented when they are passive scene elements versus targets of goal-directed actions. Action targets engaged a parietal action network centered in the supramarginal and postcentral gyri, while passive objects recruited a distributed occipito-temporal network involved in visual object recognition. Within context-selective networks, representational geometry showed a double dissociation: target objects were organized by action affordance and hand posture affordance dimensions, while passive objects aligned with semantic dimensions. Visual representational structure was invariant to context. Outside these networks, representational content retained invariance, indicating that flexibility and invariance operate at different levels of the same representational system. These findings demonstrate neural remapping of object representations depending on moment-to-moment changes in contextual roles during a naturalistic scene.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [240] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将幻觉检测重新定义为快速变化检测问题，利用马尔可夫模型和CUSUM方法，发现基于标记的分类指标掩盖了检测延迟问题，而顺序分析可以揭示这一结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标记级幻觉检测器使用AUC等分类指标评估，但流式监控的关键指标是反应时间（从幻觉发生到报警的标记数）。这两种评估视角存在本质差异，分类指标掩盖了延迟结构。

Method: 将幻觉起始检测形式化为快速变化检测问题；构建一阶马尔可夫模型描述潜在的真实/幻觉状态（基于RAGTruth验证）；应用Lorden下界理论；采用因果循环标记器作为学习增量的CUSUM方法；利用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理进行理论分析。

Result: 在虚警率0.01下，理论下界约为1.3个标记；因果循环标记器检测延迟为11-13个标记（线性基线为31个标记）；优势主要来自更好的逐标记得分而非时间累积；学习得分仅实现特征携带散度的1/4.5，差距通过重新校准无法消除。

Conclusion: 分类指标（如AUC）掩盖了检测延迟结构，顺序分析可以使其变得可测量；因果循环标记器优于线性基线，但仍存在数量级差距，主要源于得分质量而非时间累积机制。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [241] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出玻尔兹曼注意力机制，通过引入伊辛模型的可学习成对耦合来增强标准注意力机制，使模型能够显式建模注意力决策间的相互作用。实验表明该方法在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上优于标准softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而更显著。消融实验证实改进来源于可学习成对耦合。此外，论文展示了量子退火可作为有效的训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过单个查询-键相似度计算相关性，未能显式参数化注意力决策间的可学习交互，限制了模型直接建模注意力机制内部的协作或对抗性共注意结构的能力。

Method: 提出玻尔兹曼注意力，将注意力模式建模为相互作用的伊辛模型。在标准数据依赖的局部场基础上，引入可学习的成对耦合参数，使模型能够表示超越softmax或sigmoid注意力的位置间相关性。训练可采用精确玻尔兹曼计算或 diabatic 量子退火采样。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务上，玻尔兹曼注意力一致优于标准softmax注意力，优势随序列长度增加更明显。四路消融实验证实改进来自可学习成对耦合。量子退火训练方法在保持竞争力性能的同时提供了实用训练途径。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强。伊辛公式化为量子计算采样策略（如量子退火）开辟了自然路径，为未来量子加速的注意力机制奠定了基础。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [242] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCal框架，通过层次化奖励分解和分布感知优化策略，解决RL-based LLM路由中多目标冲突和奖励信号异质性问题，在7个数据集上提升路由性能和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL-based LLM路由方法将多个目标（如正确性、格式行为）聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化信号冲突；同时奖励信号在不同实例间差异大，引入偏向平凡样本的优化偏差。

Method: 提出ReCal框架，包含：(1) 层次化奖励分解机制，带组件级优势估计；(2) 分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和逐数据集归一化来校准优化变异性。

Result: 在七个数据集上，ReCal相比基线方法在路由性能和训练稳定性上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: ReCal通过奖励分解和分布感知优化有效解决了多目标冲突和奖励异质性问题，为RL-based LLM路由提供了一种更稳定有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [243] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转换为结构化符号程序表示，使LLM无需微调即可有效推理时间序列，在编辑、描述和问答三个任务上优于原始数值序列表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接将原始数值序列输入LLM或对LLM进行微调，存在模态不匹配问题，导致长序列性能下降和计算开销大。需要一种能桥接时间序列与LLM文本模态的表示方法。

Method: T2SP是一种确定性、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以结构化符号程序格式表示，使其与LLM原生训练的文本和代码模态对齐。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三个推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示一致地提升了性能，减少了推理时间，降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP为时间序列与LLM之间提供了有效的接口，通过将时间结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，使现成的LLM能够利用现有推理能力进行时间序列理解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [244] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支持点间平方距离的方式，解决了传统Christoffel函数方法在高维数据中因矩阵求逆导致的计算瓶颈，在ADBench基准上超越14个SOTA方法，保持了理论上的支持二分性与形状捕获能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Christoffel函数异常检测方法具有坚实的数学基础和低计算开销，但其矩阵求逆步骤的规模随数据维度指数增长，导致在中维数据集上就已不可行。本文旨在克服这一维度限制，同时保留该方法的关键理论性质。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），将原始Christoffel函数转化为基于查询点与支持点之间平方距离的单变量形式，从而避免了高维矩阵求逆操作。

Result: 在ADBench基准上，UCF在Average Precision指标上一致优于14个最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，本文为异常检测工具箱增加了一种鲁棒、有理论依据且普遍适用的方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [245] [Extracting Governing Equations from Latent Dynamics via Multi-View Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13260)
*Paolo Muratore,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DYSCO是一种多视角时序对比学习算法，能从含噪高维观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹和动力学方程，并提供仿射变换下的识别保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从含噪高维测量数据中识别潜在动力系统是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现的交叉核心问题，现有方法在非线性噪声观测下的识别能力有限。

Method: 提出DYSCO算法，采用多视角时序对比学习，利用同一底层过程的多个独立含噪视图分离信号与噪声；将动力学参数化为结构化函数基，实现仿射规范下控制方程的符号恢复。

Result: 在混沌、振荡、亚稳态等多种动力学体制下，在Gaussian和Poisson观测噪声（后者尤其适用于神经记录）中均实现了潜在轨迹和流场的准确恢复。

Conclusion: DYSCO将识别性理论扩展至含噪非线性观测的现实场景，为从复杂高维数据中发现潜在动力系统提供了理论保证与实用方法。

Abstract: Identifying latent dynamical systems from noisy, high-dimensional measurements is a central problem at the intersection of representation learning, system identification, and scientific discovery. We present DYSCO, a multi-view temporal contrastive learning algorithm that jointly recovers latent trajectories and the governing dynamics from such observations, by leveraging multiple independent noisy views of the same underlying process to disentangle signal from noise. By parameterizing the dynamics in a structured functional basis, our framework further enables symbolic recovery of the governing equations within an affine gauge. We offer theoretical guarantees for strong identification up to an affine indeterminacy, extending prior identifiability results to the realistic setting of noisy nonlinear observations. Empirically, we demonstrate accurate recovery of both latent trajectories and flow fields across a diverse set of dynamical regimes (e.g., chaotic, oscillatory, and metastable) under both Gaussian and Poisson observation noise, the latter being particularly relevant for neural recordings.

</details>


### [246] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出推测性回滚修正（SRC），一种用于可重置智能体环境的分支级模仿学习框架。SRC通过固定视野分支审查、定位首次有害偏差、回滚保留有用前缀、以及质量多样性存档过滤成功轨迹，在WebArena-Infinity上收集了977条通过验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例，有效改善了恢复与查询之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于专家轨迹的交互式Web智能体模仿学习中，专家干预的时机是关键挑战：延迟干预导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复，而过早或过度干预使智能体过度依赖专家策略陷入局部最优。

Method: 提出推测性回滚修正（SRC），采用固定视野分支审查：智能体先执行短推测段，仅在局部进度受阻时由教师定位首次有害偏差；回滚保留有用前缀；成功轨迹经硬验证器过滤后存入轻量级质量多样性存档。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证器的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例；固定视野审查在恢复与查询权衡上优于逐步审查，同时保留了通过验证的解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级回滚修正机制，有效平衡了专家干预时机，既避免了早期错误累积又防止了过度依赖，为可重置环境中的Web智能体训练提供了更高效的数据收集框架。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [247] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于条件的预测性维护（PdM）方法，通过将组件磨损状态表示为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取最近观测值并转换为表格格式，使用AutoML驱动的机器学习模型进行分类，在Scania Component X数据集上相比现有SOTA方法降低了成本并简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队的条件预测性维护虽然能减少停机时间和成本，但面临数据量大、故障检测复杂以及实现成本效益权衡困难等挑战。

Method: 假设被监测组件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取最近观测值并转换为表格格式，使用针对表格数据设计的机器学习模型（通过AutoML简化建模）进行分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，相比当前最先进（SOTA）方法降低了成本。

Conclusion: 提出的条件PdM方法能有效降低成本，同时通过AutoML简化了建模流程。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [248] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流中量化敏感的层并仅对这些层使用8位激活精度（其余保持4位），解决W4A4KV4全量化下的大规模激活和跨层残差动态问题，在LLaMA-2/3上提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法在权重、激活和KV缓存全量化至4比特时，面临大规模激活（massive activations）导致的量化误差放大问题。虽然基于正交旋转和仿射缩放的变换平滑技术被广泛使用，但这些静态方法忽略了残差流（residual stream）的跨层动态特性，无法完全解决由跨层残差变化引起的动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 首先通过引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标，揭示大规模激活在深度网络中呈现阶段式涌现和消失的模式，导致残差大幅变化。基于此观察，提出DynamicPTQ：从残差流动态中识别量化敏感层（quantization-sensitive layers），仅对这些层分配8位激活精度，而权重、KV缓存和其他激活保持4位精度；可与QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等强PTQ基线集成。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下一致提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05-1.07倍的吞吐提升，且内存开销适度。

Conclusion: 动态的、阶段感知的混合精度量化策略比静态变换平滑方法更能有效解决全低比特量化中的激活量化不稳定问题，为实现鲁棒的低比特LLM推理提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [249] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示天然满足兼容性定义，并提出结合交叉熵与对比损失的凸组合方法，在模型顺序微调和替换场景下实现无需重新处理库图像的无中断检索服务，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模型更新时特征表示的兼容性问题，即新旧模型产生的特征表示可互换使用，从而避免每次模型更新都需要重新处理已存储图像的计算开销。

Method: (1) 理论证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示满足兼容性定义；(2) 提出交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合训练策略，以捕获高阶依赖关系；(3) 证明该组合方法等价于在兼容性约束下使用交叉熵学习。

Result: 在模型顺序微调和替换场景下，平稳表示实现了无需重新处理库图像的无中断检索服务，性能达到SOTA。

Conclusion: d-Simplex固定分类器可作为兼容性学习的理论基础，且结合对比损失能更好地捕获高阶统计依赖，在实际应用中有效支持模型持续更新。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned.
