Method: 提出广义图上的薛定谔桥（GSBoG），通过似然优化方法学习轨迹级策略，避免稠密全局求解器，在满足端点边际分布的同时优化中间态运行成本。

Result: 在挑战性真实图拓扑上的大量实验表明，GSBoG能够可靠地学习准确且尊重拓扑的策略，同时优化特定应用的中问态成本。

Conclusion: GSBoG具有广泛的适用性，为通用图上成本感知的动态传输开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Transportation on graphs is a fundamental challenge across many domains, where decisions must respect topological and operational constraints. Despite the need for actionable policies, existing graph-transport methods lack this expressivity. They rely on restrictive assumptions, fail to generalize across sparse topologies, and scale poorly with graph size and time horizon. To address these issues, we introduce Generalized Schrödinger Bridge on Graphs (GSBoG), a novel scalable data-driven framework for learning executable controlled continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) policies on arbitrary graphs under state cost augmented dynamics. Notably, GSBoG learns trajectory-level policies, avoiding dense global solvers and thereby enhancing scalability. This is achieved via a likelihood optimization approach, satisfying the endpoint marginals, while simultaneously optimizing intermediate behavior under state-dependent running costs. Extensive experimentation on challenging real-world graph topologies shows that GSBoG reliably learns accurate, topology-respecting policies while optimizing application-specific intermediate state costs, highlighting its broad applicability and paving new avenues for cost-aware dynamical transport on general graphs.

</details>


### [401] [GEMSS: A Variational Bayesian Method for Discovering Multiple Sparse Solutions in Classification and Regression Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08913)
*Kateřina Henclová,Václav Šmídl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEMSS（高斯集成多稀疏解算法），一种能够同时发现多个多样化的稀疏特征组合的变分算法。该方法使用尖峰-平板先验实现稀疏性、高斯混合近似难处理的 multimodal 后验，并通过Jaccard惩罚控制解的多样性。在128个实验和3个真实世界数据集上，GEMSS优于五种特征选择方法，并能成功识别多个等优的稀疏解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维、欠定且高度相关的系统中，多个不同的稀疏特征子集可能对响应变量具有同等的解释力。传统特征选择方法通常只输出单一解，掩盖了其他同样合理的解释，这限制了从数据中获取领域见解的能力。

Method: GEMSS采用结构化尖峰-平板先验实现稀疏性，利用高斯混合模型近似多模态后验分布，引入基于Jaccard系数的惩罚项控制解的多样性，通过随机梯度下降优化单一目标函数。同时设计了一个新的基准测试框架，用于生成具有多个等优稀疏解的人工问题。

Result: 在128个综合实验中，GEMSS在检索真实特征而非仅评估预测性能方面，一致优于通过ALFESE框架适配的五种主流特征选择方法。在代谢组学和物理化学的3个挑战性真实数据集上，GEMSS成功分离出多个不同但质量优良的解。

Conclusion: GEMSS能有效发现多个多样化的稀疏解，弥补了传统特征选择方法只输出单一解的不足，在需要探索多种可能解释的科学数据分析中具有重要实用价值。该方法已作为PyPI包'gemss'发布。

Abstract: High-dimensional, underdetermined and highly correlated systems are common in data science practice, especially when analyzing physical measurements. In such settings, feature selection poses a fundamental challenge because multiple distinct sparse subsets may explain the response equally well. Their identification is crucial not only for predictive modeling but also for generating domain-specific insights into the underlying mechanisms. Yet, conventional methods typically isolate a single solution, obscuring the full spectrum of plausible explanations. This work introduces GEMSS (Gaussian Ensemble for Multiple Sparse Solutions), a variational algorithm designed to simultaneously discover multiple, diverse sparse feature combinations. The method employs a structured spike-and-slab prior for sparsity, a mixture of Gaussians to approximate the intractable multimodal posterior, and a Jaccard-based penalty to further control solution diversity. A single objective function is optimized via stochastic gradient descent. The method is tested on 128 comprehensive experiments by a novel benchmarking framework designed to generate artificial problems with multiple sparse solutions of equal predictive properties. This allows us to measure the retrieval of ground truth features rather than only evaluating predictive performance -- characteristics more fitting to our practical needs. A comparative analysis shows that GEMSS consistently outperforms five prominent feature selection methods adapted through the ALFESE framework. Finally, we demonstrate practical usability through 3 challenging real-world datasets from metabolomics and physical chemistry: GEMSS successfully isolates multiple distinct yet quality solutions. GEMSS is available as a PyPI package 'gemss'. The corresponding repository github.com/kat-er-ina/gemss/ includes the full codebase and a free, no-code application GEMSS Explorer.

</details>


### [402] [Hierarchical Successor Representation for Robust Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12753)
*Changmin Yu,Máté Lengyel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层级继代表征（HSR），通过时间抽象构建预测表征，结合非负矩阵分解获得稀疏低秩状态表征，解决了经典SR的策略依赖性和谱扩散问题，实现了跨任务的策略无关迁移与高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典继代表征（SR）存在两个关键局限：一是预测表征严重依赖当前策略，而策略会因持续学习、环境非平稳性或任务需求变化而改变，导致已建立的表征失效；二是在拓扑复杂环境中，SR会出现谱扩散现象，导致表征稠密且重叠，可扩展性差。

Method: 提出层级继代表征（HSR），将时间抽象（temporal abstractions）引入预测表征的构建中，学习对策略变化鲁棒的稳定状态特征；进一步对HSR应用非负矩阵分解（NMF），获得稀疏、低秩的状态表征。

Result: HSR-NMF在多隔间环境的迁移任务中实现了高度样本高效的学习；发现HSR-NMF能够发现可解释的拓扑结构，提供策略无关的层级地图；HSR的时域扩展预测结构还可驱动高效探索，有效扩展到大规模程序化生成环境。

Conclusion: HSR弥合了无模型最优性与有模型灵活性之间的鸿沟，为任务迁移提供了有效基础，同时兼顾了策略鲁棒性、表征稀疏性和探索效率。

Abstract: The successor representation (SR) provides a powerful framework for decoupling predictive dynamics from rewards, enabling rapid generalisation across reward configurations. However, the classical SR is limited by its inherent policy dependence: policies change due to ongoing learning, environmental non-stationarities, and changes in task demands, making established predictive representations obsolete. Furthermore, in topologically complex environments, SRs suffer from spectral diffusion, leading to dense and overlapping features that scale poorly. Here we propose the Hierarchical Successor Representation (HSR) for overcoming these limitations. By incorporating temporal abstractions into the construction of predictive representations, HSR learns stable state features which are robust to task-induced policy changes. Applying non-negative matrix factorisation (NMF) to the HSR yields a sparse, low-rank state representation that facilitates highly sample-efficient transfer to novel tasks in multi-compartmental environments. Further analysis reveals that HSR-NMF discovers interpretable topological structures, providing a policy-agnostic hierarchical map that effectively bridges model-free optimality and model-based flexibility. Beyond providing a useful basis for task-transfer, we show that HSR's temporally extended predictive structure can also be leveraged to drive efficient exploration, effectively scaling to large, procedurally generated environments.

</details>


### [403] [Structured vs. Unstructured Pruning: An Exponential Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02234)
*Davide Ferre',Frédéric Giroire,Frederik Mallmann-Trenn,Emanuele Natale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文表明，在随机初始化两层ReLU网络中，通过剪枝隐藏单元（神经元剪枝）来逼近单个无偏置ReLU神经元时，需要Ω(1/ε)的起始网络规模，而权重剪枝仅需O(log(1/ε))，揭示了两种剪枝方法之间存在指数级差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强彩票假设（SLTH）声称随机初始化的大规模网络中仅通过剪枝就能找到逼近目标函数的子网络，但现有理论几乎全集中在非结构化（权重）剪枝上，而对更具实际硬件加速价值的神经元剪枝（结构化剪枝）缺乏理论分析。本文旨在理论上揭示神经元剪枝与权重剪枝在逼近能力上的本质差异。

Method: 考虑在随机初始化的两层无偏置ReLU网络中，通过剪枝隐藏单元（神经元级剪枝）来逼近单个ReLU神经元的问题。分别推导了神经元剪枝和权重剪枝所需要的网络规模下界与上界，并进行了理论对比。

Result: 神经元剪枝需要Ω(1/ε)的起始网络规模才能实现ε-逼近，而权重剪枝仅需O(log(1/ε))个隐藏单元，两者之间存在指数级差距。

Conclusion: 神经元剪枝（结构化剪枝）在逼近能力上显著弱于权重剪枝（非结构化剪枝），说明仅靠剪除整个神经元的方式在参数效率上存在固有局限。

Abstract: The Strong Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (SLTH) states that large, randomly initialized neural networks contain sparse subnetworks capable of approximating a target function at initialization without training, suggesting that pruning alone is sufficient. Pruning methods are typically classified as unstructured, where individual weights can be removed from the network, and structured, where parameters are removed according to specific patterns, as in neuron pruning. Existing theoretical results supporting the SLTH rely almost exclusively on unstructured pruning, showing that logarithmic overparameterization suffices to approximate simple target networks. In contrast, neuron pruning has received limited theoretical attention, despite its practical appeal for direct hardware speedups. In this work, we consider the problem of approximating a single bias-free ReLU neuron by pruning hidden units of a randomly initialized two-layer ReLU network, effectively isolating the intrinsic limitations of neuron pruning. We show that achieving an $\varepsilon$-approximation requires a starting network size of $Ω(1/\varepsilon)$ for neuron pruning, whereas weight pruning succeeds with only $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ hidden units, revealing an exponential separation between the two approaches.

</details>


### [404] [Echo2ECG: Enhancing ECG Representations with Cardiac Morphology from Multi-View Echos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08505)
*Michelle Espranita Liman,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Eimo Martens,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Echo2ECG，一种多模态自监督学习框架，利用多视角超声心动图（Echo）增强心电图（ECG）表征，使ECG能够预测心脏形态学表型（如左心室射血分数）。该方法在结构表型分类和形态学Echo检索任务上显著优于现有单模态和多模态基线，且模型大小仅为最大基线的1/18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG无法直接测量心脏形态学表型（如LVEF），而现有自监督方法将ECG与单视角Echo对齐存在表征失配问题（仅捕获局部解剖快照），限制了ECG对形态学信息的获取。因此需要一种能从多视角Echo中丰富ECG形态学表征的方法。

Method: 提出Echo2ECG多模态自监督学习框架，通过将ECG与多视角Echo（而非单视角）进行对齐，使ECG表征融入心脏的形态学结构信息。

Result: 在两个临床任务（结构性心脏表型分类和形态学Echo检索）上，Echo2ECG提取的ECG表征一致优于现有SOTA单模态和多模态基线，且模型大小仅为最大基线的1/18。

Conclusion: Echo2ECG是一个鲁棒且强大的ECG特征提取器，能够有效弥合ECG与形态学表型之间的鸿沟，实现早期可及的健康筛查。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG) is a low-cost, widely used modality for diagnosing electrical abnormalities like atrial fibrillation by capturing the heart's electrical activity. However, it cannot directly measure cardiac morphological phenotypes, such as left ventricular ejection fraction (LVEF), which typically require echocardiography (Echo). Predicting these phenotypes from ECG would enable early, accessible health screening. Existing self-supervised methods suffer from a representational mismatch by aligning ECGs to single-view Echos, which only capture local, spatially restricted anatomical snapshots. To address this, we propose Echo2ECG, a multimodal self-supervised learning framework that enriches ECG representations with the heart's morphological structure captured in multi-view Echos. We evaluate Echo2ECG as an ECG feature extractor on two clinically relevant tasks that fundamentally require morphological information: (1) classification of structural cardiac phenotypes across three datasets, and (2) retrieval of Echo studies with similar morphological characteristics using ECG queries. Our extracted ECG representations consistently outperform those of state-of-the-art unimodal and multimodal baselines across both tasks, despite being 18x smaller than the largest baseline. These results demonstrate that Echo2ECG is a robust, powerful ECG feature extractor. Our code is accessible at https://github.com/michelleespranita/Echo2ECG.

</details>


### [405] [Differentiable Thermodynamic Phase-Equilibria for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11249)
*Karim K. Ben Hicham,Moreno Ascani,Jan G. Rittig,Alexander Mitsos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISCOMAX，一种可微分的相平衡计算算法，通过离散枚举可行相态、前向传播真实平衡态、反向传播使用掩码softmax聚合和直通梯度估计器，确保热力学一致性，在二元液液平衡数据上优于现有替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理一致的机器学习方法在活度系数建模中表现良好，但将这类方法扩展到基于极值原理的平衡数据（如液液平衡）仍然困难。

Method: 结合离散枚举可行相态、前向传播真实平衡态、反向传播使用掩码softmax聚合的直通梯度估计器，实现端到端可微分的相平衡计算，保证热力学一致性。

Result: 在二元液液平衡数据上优于现有基于替代模型的方法。

Conclusion: DISCOMAX提供了一个从不同类型平衡数据中学习的通用框架，在保证热力学一致性的同时实现了优异的预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of phase equilibria remains a central challenge in chemical engineering. Physics-consistent machine learning methods that incorporate thermodynamic structure into neural networks have recently shown strong performance for activity-coefficient modeling. However, extending such approaches to equilibrium data arising from an extremum principle, such as liquid-liquid equilibria, remains difficult. Here we present DISCOMAX, a differentiable algorithm for phase-equilibrium calculation that guarantees thermodynamic consistency at both training and inference, only subject to a user-specified discretization. The method combines discrete enumeration of feasible phase states with masked softmax aggregation in the backward pass, with the propagation of the true equilibrium state in the forward pass, using a straight-through gradient estimator to enable physics-consistent end-to-end learning of neural \gls{gE}-models. We show that this approach bears analogy to statistical thermodynamics, and we evaluate it on binary liquid-liquid equilibrium data where it outperforms existing surrogate-based methods, while offering a general framework for learning from different kinds of equilibrium data.

</details>


### [406] [ARROW: Augmented Replay for RObust World models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11395)
*Abdulaziz Alyahya,Abdallah Al Siyabi,Markus R. Ernst,Luke Yang,Levin Kuhlmann,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARROW是一种基于模型的持续强化学习算法，通过模仿神经科学的经验回放机制，使用记忆高效的双缓冲区（短期+长期分布匹配）增强DreamerV3的世界模型，在无共享结构（Atari）和有共享结构（Procgen CoinRun）任务中显著减少了灾难性遗忘，同时保持前向迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习方法大多依赖基于模型的回放缓冲区来缓解灾难性遗忘，但这些方案因内存需求大而面临可扩展性挑战。受神经科学启发（大脑将经验回放到预测性世界模型而非直接回放到策略），作者提出一种更高效的模型-based持续RL方法。

Method: 基于DreamerV3，提出ARROW算法：维护两个互补缓冲区——短期缓冲区（近期经验）和长期缓冲区（通过智能采样保持任务多样性的分布匹配缓冲区）。通过记忆高效的回放机制增强世界模型训练。

Result: 在Atari（无共享结构任务）上，ARROW相比同规模缓冲区的model-free和model-based基线，遗忘显著减少；在Procgen CoinRun（有共享结构任务）上，保持可比的前向迁移能力。

Conclusion: 基于模型的RL和生物启发方法在持续强化学习中具有巨大潜力，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning challenges agents to acquire new skills while retaining previously learned ones with the goal of improving performance in both past and future tasks. Most existing approaches rely on model-free methods with replay buffers to mitigate catastrophic forgetting; however, these solutions often face significant scalability challenges due to large memory demands. Drawing inspiration from neuroscience, where the brain replays experiences to a predictive World Model rather than directly to the policy, we present ARROW (Augmented Replay for RObust World models), a model-based continual RL algorithm that extends DreamerV3 with a memory-efficient, distribution-matching replay buffer. Unlike standard fixed-size FIFO buffers, ARROW maintains two complementary buffers: a short-term buffer for recent experiences and a long-term buffer that preserves task diversity through intelligent sampling. We evaluate ARROW on two challenging continual RL settings: Tasks without shared structure (Atari), and tasks with shared structure, where knowledge transfer is possible (Procgen CoinRun variants). Compared to model-free and model-based baselines with replay buffers of the same-size, ARROW demonstrates substantially less forgetting on tasks without shared structure, while maintaining comparable forward transfer. Our findings highlight the potential of model-based RL and bio-inspired approaches for continual reinforcement learning, warranting further research.

</details>


### [407] [Grammar of the Wave: Towards Explainable Multivariate Time Series Event Detection via Neuro-Symbolic VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11479)
*Sky Chenwei Wan,Yifei Y. Wang,Tianjun Hou,Xiqing Chang,Aymeric Jan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向时间序列事件检测的语言引导方法，通过事件逻辑树（ELT）将自然语言描述转化为结构化时态逻辑，并基于神经符号VLM智能体框架SELA在无/少标注数据下实现事件定位与可解释推理，在能源与气候领域的真实基准上超越监督微调和零样本/少样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列事件检测中，事件通常由自然语言描述定义且具有跨通道的时态逻辑结构，但密集标注成本高昂，纯监督学习难以适用，因此需要一种能够在少量或无标注数据下根据文本描述定位事件的方法。

Method: 提出事件逻辑树（ELT）将语言描述转化为结构化时态逻辑约束；在此基础上构建SELA——一种神经符号VLM智能体框架，通过可视化信号迭代接地基元并在ELT约束下组合，同时输出事件区间和树状解释。

Result: 在能源与气候领域的真实基准上，SELA在事件检测性能上超越了监督微调以及现有的零样本/少样本时间序列推理方法。

Conclusion: ELT与SELA为语言引导的时间序列事件检测提供了一种高效、可解释且数据效率优越的解决方案，特别适用于标注稀缺的高风险应用场景。

Abstract: Time Series Event Detection (TSED) aims to localize semantically meaningful events in time series data, with critical applications in high-stakes domains. Unlike statistical anomalies, events are often defined by natural-language descriptions with internal temporal-logic structures across multiple physical channels. However, in real-world settings, dense event annotations are expensive to obtain, making purely supervised learning difficult. We introduce Language-guided TSED, a setting where a model is given textual event descriptions and must ground them to intervals in multivariate signals with little or no labeled data. To address this problem, we propose Event Logic Tree (ELT), a knowledge representation framework that converts linguistic descriptions into structured temporal logic over signal primitives. Building on ELT, we present SELA, a neuro-symbolic VLM agent framework that iteratively grounds primitives from signal visualizations and composes them under ELT constraints, producing both event intervals and faithful tree-structured explanations. We further release a real-world benchmark across energy and climate domains with expert knowledge and annotations. Experiments show that SELA improves over supervised fine-tuning and existing zero/few-shot time series reasoning baselines.

</details>


### [408] [Mixing Makes Markovian Contexts Cheap for Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12530)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Osama Hanna,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将马尔可夫上下文线性老虎机问题简化为标准线性老虎机问题的归约方法，通过构建平稳替代动作集和延迟更新方案，在均匀几何遍历条件下实现了与底层线性老虎机算法相匹配的最坏情况遗憾界，并在真实数据集上验证了其优于LinUCB基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明在独立同分布上下文下，线性上下文老虎机可简化为单上下文线性老虎机，但该归约依赖于上下文独立性，无法处理实践中常见的时域相关（如马尔可夫）上下文。本文旨在将这一高效视角扩展到马尔可夫上下文过程。

Method: 1) 在均匀几何遍历条件下，构造平稳替代动作集，采用延迟更新方案控制非平稳条件上下文分布引起的偏差；2) 对于未知平稳分布，提出分阶段在线学习替代映射的算法。

Result: 在两种设置下均获得了与底层线性老虎机算法相匹配的高概率最坏情况遗憾界（在充分快速混合条件下），并在真实数据集上展示了优于LinUCB基线的实际效果。

Conclusion: 成功将"上下文是廉价的"视角从独立同分布上下文扩展到马尔可夫上下文场景，为处理实际中时序相关的动作可用性问题提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Recent work shows that when contexts are drawn i.i.d., linear contextual bandits can be reduced to single-context linear bandits. This ``contexts are cheap'' perspective is highly advantageous, as it allows for sharper finite-time analyses and leverages mature techniques from the linear bandit literature, such as those for misspecification and adversarial corruption. However, this reduction crucially relies on the independence of contexts and does not extend to settings with temporally correlated (e.g., Markovian) contexts, which arise frequently in practice. Motivated by applications with temporally correlated availability, we extend this perspective to linear bandits with Markovian context processes, where the action set evolves via an exogenous Markov chain. Our main contribution is a reduction that applies under uniform geometric ergodicity. We construct a stationary surrogate action set to solve the problem using a standard linear bandit oracle, employing a delayed-update scheme to control the bias induced by the nonstationary conditional context distributions. We further provide a phased algorithm for unknown stationary distributions that learns the surrogate mapping online. In both settings, we obtain a high-probability worst-case regret bound matching that of the underlying linear bandit oracle in sufficiently fast mixing regimes. We then validate our results on a real-world instance, where we show practical gains over a LinUCB baseline.

</details>


### [409] [Towards One-for-All Anomaly Detection for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14407)
*Shiyuan Li,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Xiaofeng Cao,Shirui Pan,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OFA-TAD，一个一次性训练即可泛化到多领域未见表数据的一体化表格异常检测框架，通过多视图邻近距离表示和MoE评分网络实现跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格异常检测方法遵循"一数据集一模"范式，需要针对每个数据集单独训练，计算成本高且难以泛化到未见领域。

Method: (1) 提取邻近距离模式作为可迁移线索；(2) 从多个变换诱导的度量空间构建多视图邻近距离表示，缓解变换敏感性；(3) 采用MoE评分网络进行视图特定异常评分和熵正则化门控融合；(4) 设计多策略异常合成机制支持单类约束下的训练。

Result: 在14个领域的34个数据集上，OFA-TAD在严格的OFA设置下实现了优异的异常检测性能和强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: OFA-TAD验证了一体化范式在表格异常检测中的可行性，通过一次性训练即可泛化到未见表数据，显著降低了计算开销。

Abstract: Tabular anomaly detection (TAD) aims to identify samples that deviate from the majority in tabular data and is critical in many real-world applications. However, existing methods follow a ``one model for one dataset (OFO)'' paradigm, which relies on dataset-specific training and thus incurs high computational cost and yields limited generalization to unseen domains. To address these limitations, we propose OFA-TAD, a generalist one-for-all (OFA) TAD framework that only requires one-time training on multiple source datasets and can generalize to unseen datasets from diverse domains on-the-fly. To realize one-for-all tabular anomaly detection, OFA-TAD extracts neighbor-distance patterns as transferable cues, and introduces multi-view neighbor-distance representations from multiple transformation-induced metric spaces to mitigate the transformation sensitivity of distance profiles. To adaptively combine multi-view distance evidence, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scoring network is employed for view-specific anomaly scoring and entropy-regularized gated fusion, with a multi-strategy anomaly synthesis mechanism to support training under the one-class constraint. Extensive experiments on 34 datasets from 14 domains demonstrate that OFA-TAD achieves superior anomaly detection performance and strong cross-domain generalizability under the strict OFA setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/Shiy-Li/OFA-TAD.

</details>


### [410] [Disentangling Dynamical Systems: Causal Representation Learning Meets Local Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14483)
*Markus W. Baumgartner,Anson Lei,Joe Watson,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合因果表示学习与稀疏正则化Transformer的参数化系统辨识方法，无需预定义函数库即可从原始轨迹数据中解耦出系统参数，并证明全局因果结构为可辨识性提供下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数化系统辨识方法需要预定义候选函数库（依赖领域知识），而深度学习的黑箱建模虽能处理复杂系统，但无法产生显式的、解耦的系统结构表示。因此，需要一种无需结构假设即可从数据中解耦系统参数的方法。

Method: 1. 提出基于因果表示学习的可辨识性定理，推导出系统参数可从原始轨迹数据中唯一解耦的图准则（到置换和微分同胚为止）。2. 分析全局因果结构与局部状态依赖因果结构对解耦保证的下界关系。3. 将系统参数辨识实例化为变分推断问题，利用稀疏正则化Transformer学习状态依赖的因果结构。

Result: 在四个合成域上验证，该方法能恢复高度解耦的表示，而基线方法无法做到。实验结果证实了理论分析：强制局部因果结构对完全可辨识性通常是必要的。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上证明了因果结构对系统参数可辨识性的下界作用，在实践上提出了一种无需先验函数库、能自动发现解耦表示的系统辨识方法，为参数化系统辨识与深度学习之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: Parametric system identification methods estimate the parameters of explicitly defined physical systems from data. Yet, they remain constrained by the need to provide an explicit function space, typically through a predefined library of candidate functions chosen via available domain knowledge. In contrast, deep learning can demonstrably model systems of broad complexity with high fidelity, but black-box function approximation typically fails to yield explicit descriptive or disentangled representations revealing the structure of a system. We develop a novel identifiability theorem, leveraging causal representation learning, to uncover disentangled representations of system parameters without structural assumptions. We derive a graphical criterion specifying when system parameters can be uniquely disentangled from raw trajectory data, up to permutation and diffeomorphism. Crucially, our analysis demonstrates that global causal structures provide a lower bound on the disentanglement guarantees achievable when considering local state-dependent causal structures. We instantiate system parameter identification as a variational inference problem, leveraging a sparsity-regularised transformer to uncover state-dependent causal structures. We empirically validate our approach across four synthetic domains, demonstrating its ability to recover highly disentangled representations that baselines fail to recover. Corroborating our theoretical analysis, our results confirm that enforcing local causal structure is often necessary for full identifiability.

</details>


### [411] [Point-Identification of a Robust Predictor Under Latent Shift with Imperfect Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15158)
*Zahra Rahiminasab,Reza Soumi,Arto Klami,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在等价类（LECs）概念，发现当多个领域在混合代理诱导的LECs形成鲁棒预测器的方式上足够不同时，即使在代理不完美的情况下也能实现点识别，并引入主动查询多样化域以满足该条件的PQAL框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代理的域自适应方法依赖于完备性假设（completeness assumption）来唯一确定鲁棒预测器，但当代理不完美时，从潜混杂到代理分布的映射非单射，导致多个预测器与观测数据兼容（集合识别），破坏了完备性假设。

Method: 引入潜在等价类（LECs）概念；提出跨域秩条件作为比完备性更弱的可识别条件；设计主动学习框架PQAL，通过主动查询满足秩条件的多样化域来实现点识别。

Result: 在合成数据、半合成dSprites、IHDP和ACS Folktables数据集上，PQAL能够恢复点识别预测器，对不同程度的分布偏移具有鲁棒性，性能优于此前的方法。

Conclusion: 通过域多样性的跨域秩条件替代强完备性假设，可在弱假设下实现鲁棒预测器的点识别，主动查询多样化域是解决域自适应中潜在混杂问题的有效策略。

Abstract: Addressing the domain adaptation problem becomes more challenging when distribution shifts across domains stem from latent confounders that affect both covariates and outcomes. Existing proxy-based approaches that address latent shift rely on a strong completeness assumption to uniquely determine (point-identify) a robust predictor. Completeness requires that proxies have sufficient information about variations in latent confounders. For imperfect proxies the mapping from confounders to the space of proxy distributions is non-injective, and multiple latent confounder values can generate the same proxy distribution. This breaks the completeness assumption and observed data are consistent with multiple potential predictors (set-identified). To address this, we introduce latent equivalent classes (LECs). LECs are defined as groups of latent confounders that induce the same conditional proxy distribution. We show that point-identification for the robust predictor remains achievable as long as multiple domains differ sufficiently in how they mix proxy-induced LECs to form the robust predictor. This domain diversity condition is formalized as a cross-domain rank condition on the mixture weights, which is substantially weaker assumption than completeness.
  We introduce the Proximal Quasi-Bayesian Active learning (PQAL) framework, which actively queries
  a small, targeted set of diverse domains that satisfy this rank condition. PQAL can recover the point-identified predictor, demonstrates robustness to varying degrees of shift and outperforms previous methods on synthetic data and semi-synthetic dSprites, IHDP, ACS Folktables datasets.

</details>


### [412] [Variational Graph Neural Networks for Uncertainty Quantification in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29515)
*David Gonzalez,Alba Muixi,Beatriz Moya,Elias Cueto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种变分图神经网络（VGNN）架构，通过在解码器部分集成变分层来建模权重概率分布，以较低计算成本同时估计认知不确定性和统计不确定性。在固体力学两个案例（2D弹性问题中非线性分布弹性模量识别、3D超弹性梁载荷定位与量化）中验证，模型不仅高精度恢复物理参数，还能提供与物理问题一致的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字孪生等关键应用中，快速响应不足以保证可靠性，传统确定性方法无法提供预测置信度，尤其是在反问题中解不唯一或初始数据含噪声时。经典深度神经网络也缺乏量化预测不确定性的明确指标。

Method: 提出VGNN架构，将变分层集成到图神经网络的解码器部分，以建模权重概率分布，相比全贝叶斯网络显著降低计算成本，同时能够估计认知不确定性（认知不确定性）和统计不确定性（偶然不确定性）。

Result: 在2D弹性问题中成功识别非线性分布弹性模量，在3D超弹性梁问题中准确定位载荷位置并量化其值；两者均给出与物理问题一致的置信区间，且恢复物理参数具有高精度。

Conclusion: 所提VGNN方法能以较低计算代价提供可靠的物理参数反演结果及置信度度量，适用于对不确定性敏感的工程应用场景。

Abstract: The increasingly wide use of deep machine learning techniques in computational mechanics has significantly accelerated simulations of problems that were considered unapproachable just a few years ago. However, in critical applications such as Digital Twins for engineering or medicine, fast responses are not enough; reliable results must also be provided. In certain cases, traditional deterministic methods may not be optimal as they do not provide a measure of confidence in their predictions or results, especially in inverse problems where the solution may not be unique or the initial data may not be entirely reliable due to the presence of noise, for instance. Classic deep neural networks also lack a clear measure to quantify the uncertainty of their predictions. In this work, we present a variational graph neural network (VGNN) architecture that integrates variational layers into its architecture to model the probability distribution of weights. Unlike computationally expensive full Bayesian networks, our approach strategically introduces variational layers exclusively in the decoder, allowing us to estimate cognitive uncertainty and statistical uncertainty at a relatively lower cost.
  In this work, we validate the proposed methodology in two cases of solid mechanics: the identification of the value of the elastic modulus with nonlinear distribution in a 2D elastic problem and the location and quantification of the loads applied to a 3D hyperelastic beam, in both cases using only the displacement field of each test as input data. The results show that the model not only recovers the physical parameters with high precision, but also provides confidence intervals consistent with the physics of the problem, as well as being able to locate the position of the applied load and estimate its value, giving a confidence interval for that experiment.

</details>


### [413] [Allocating Human Oversight in AI-Enabled Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12497)
*Zikun Ye,Jiameng Lyu,Rui Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究如何在AI辅助决策中，将有限的人工验证预算最优分配给可靠性异质且未知的任务，提出基于上置信界的学习策略，理论证明其效率损失随预算增长趋于零，并在合成实验和真实数字孪生调查中验证其优于均匀分配、epsilon-greedy和先探索后利用等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在客户-facing决策中广泛部署AI预测层，但AI信号在不同任务、产品和客户群体间可靠性不均，仍需昂贵的人工验证来锚定真实值。由于人工标注本身存在噪声，需多次标注取平均，使得验证成本高昂。如何在有限预算下最优分配人工验证资源是一个关键问题。

Method: 将问题建模为调谐预测驱动推断（tuned prediction-powered inference），每个标注同时优化AI估计并揭示任务的校正难度（即AI作为控制变量后的残余方差）。若难度已知则遵循Neyman平方根规则；因未知，提出基于上置信界（UCB）的在线学习策略，动态引导验证流向AI最不可靠的任务。

Result: 理论证明策略的终期效率损失随预算增长趋于零。合成实验和真实数字孪生调查（68任务，2000+受访者）中，该方法在可靠性异质时大幅缩小了与Oracle的差距，优于均匀分配、epsilon-greedy和先探索后利用等基线，将均匀分配的10-12%差距缩小至2-6%。

Conclusion: AI的价值不仅取决于模型精度，还取决于将人工监督精准投向AI易错处的运营策略。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy AI as a low-cost prediction layer in customer-facing decision processes, including demand sensing, service-quality monitoring, product testing, and market research, but AI-generated signals are unevenly reliable across tasks, products, and customer segments. Firms therefore still need scarce human validation (labels, audits, survey responses, or follow-up measurements) to anchor AI outputs to ground truth. Because human ground truth is itself noisy, varying across labelers and even across repeated judgments, the firm must collect and average several human labels per task, which makes human validation costly. We study how to allocate a limited human-validation budget across many AI-assisted tasks when reliability is heterogeneous and unknown before deployment. We cast this within tuned prediction-powered inference. Each human label both sharpens the AI-assisted estimate and reveals the task's rectification difficulty, the variance that remains after the AI prediction is optimally used as a control variate. If difficulties were known, the optimal allocation would follow a Neyman square-root rule; because they are unknown, we propose a policy based on upper confidence bounds that learns them online and steers validation toward tasks where AI is least reliable. We prove that the policy's terminal efficiency loss relative to the oracle allocation vanishes as the budget grows. In synthetic experiments and a real digital-twin survey with 68 tasks and over 2000 respondents, it closes most of the gap to the oracle when reliability is heterogeneous, outperforming uniform and epsilon-greedy allocation; on the survey data it also outperforms explore-then-commit pilot designs and cuts uniform's 10--12% gap to 2--6%. The value of AI depends not only on model accuracy but also on the operational policy that targets human oversight where AI errors matter most.

</details>


### [414] [Machine Learning-based Two-Stage Graph Sparsification for the Travelling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20236)
*Bo-Cheng Lin,Yi Mei,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段候选图稀疏化方法：先用α-Nearest和POPMUSIC并集实现近完美召回（~6N边），再训练轻量分类器过滤单源边，在TSP500上将密度降低37-47%的同时保留≥99.69%最优边。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSP稀疏化启发式方法（α-Nearest和POPMUSIC）在密度与覆盖率之间存在权衡，而基于学习的稀疏化方法需要在完整图上对所有边评分，计算成本高且局限于欧氏距离实例。

Method: 两阶段流水线：Stage 1取α-Nearest和POPMUSIC的并集（~6N边），利用双源标注（来源溯源）；Stage 2在此标注边上训练轻量分类器，仅对单源子集进行剪枝，双源边直接保留。

Result: 在4种距离类型、5种空间分布、50-500规模上，候选图密度降低37-47%，最优边保留率≥99.69%；在TSP500上以更低密度达到或超过近期欧氏神经稀疏化方法的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 利用源标注将学习问题简化为单源边过滤，在不牺牲覆盖率的前提下显著降低候选图密度，且泛化于非欧氏距离和多种分布。

Abstract: High-performance TSP solvers such as Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun (LKH) search within a \emph{candidate graph} -- a small subset of edges pre-selected for the solver -- rather than over the complete graph. The two leading sparsification heuristics, $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC, each fall short of the density-coverage balance: $α$-Nearest is dense with stable recall, while POPMUSIC is sparser but its recall degrades with scale. Their union closes the recall gap while remaining far below the complete graph in density, leaving room for further reduction. Existing learning-based sparsifiers score edges on the complete graph, an approach that is expensive and largely limited to Euclidean instances. We propose a two-stage method that inverts this logic. Stage~1 takes the union of $α$-Nearest and POPMUSIC, achieving near-perfect recall at ${\sim}6N$ edges. Crucially, the union annotates each edge with its \emph{source provenance} -- whether it was endorsed by $α$-Nearest, POPMUSIC, or both. Stage~2 trains a lightweight classifier on these annotated edges and prunes the lowest-scoring ones. Because dual-source edges are almost always optimal, the learning problem reduces to filtering the single-source subset -- a substantially easier task than classifying all $O(N^2)$ edges from scratch. Across four distance types, five spatial distributions, and problem sizes from 50 to 500, the pipeline reduces candidate-graph density by $37$-$47\%$ while retaining ${\geq}99.69\%$ of optimal-tour edges, and matches or exceeds the coverage of recent Euclidean-only neural sparsifiers at lower density at TSP500.

</details>


### [415] [Optimal Spatio-Temporal Decoupling for Bayesian Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00432)
*Yu-Hsueh Fang,Chia-Yen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SA-BCP提出基于状态自适应贝叶斯共形预测，通过门控凸组合结合长期时间惯性与局部空间核密度估计证据，在保持渐进有效覆盖的同时显著缩小预测区间（Winkler得分降低约3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线共形预测需要平衡对分布漂移的快速自适应与稳定的覆盖水平：反馈驱动方法反应快但易波动，强折扣贝叶斯方法滞后且在高覆盖率下区间过宽。

Method: 将预测分位数构造成长期时间惯性与局部空间核密度估计证据的门控凸组合，由单一可解释证据阈值K控制；推导了MSE最优阈值的闭式解；提出滚动起点在线选择K的流程，具有次线性动态遗憾界。

Result: 在四个金融波动率和天气数据集、三个目标覆盖率水平、八个基线上，SA-BCP在大部分设置中达到或超过名义覆盖率，且区间显著更窄（最紧覆盖率下Winkler得分比折扣贝叶斯CP低约3倍）。覆盖率匹配审计确认效率提升非覆盖不足所致。

Conclusion: SA-BCP通过状态自适应机制有效平衡了覆盖稳定性与区间效率，主限是波动率特化的conformal-GARCH在其专属数据集上仍更高效，且不可跨领域迁移。

Abstract: Online conformal prediction must balance fast adaptation to distribution shift against stable coverage: feedback-driven methods react quickly but become volatile, while strongly discounted Bayesian methods lag and inflate intervals at tight coverage. We introduce \textbf{State-Adaptive Bayesian Conformal Prediction (SA-BCP)}, which forms the predictive quantile as a gated convex combination of long-term temporal inertia and local spatial evidence from a kernel density estimate, controlled by a single interpretable evidence threshold $K$. We establish three results: (i) asymptotic marginal validity of the resulting intervals; (ii) a closed-form expression for the MSE-optimal threshold, $K^*_{\mathrm{MSE}}=α(1-α)/M^{\mathcal{T}}$, trading the coverage-indicator (Bernoulli) variance against the temporal structural bias $M^{\mathcal{T}}$; and (iii) a rolling-origin procedure for selecting $K$ online -- consistent under stationarity, with $O(\sqrt{T\log N})$ regret against the best fixed $K$ and, for a segmented variant, a sublinear dynamic-regret bound under bounded drift. Across four financial-volatility and weather datasets, three target coverage levels, and eight baselines (including the strongest recent conditional-quantile methods, SPCI and KOWCPI), SA-BCP attains at-or-above-nominal coverage in most settings while producing substantially sharper intervals -- up to roughly $3\times$ lower Winkler score than discounted Bayesian CP at the tightest coverage -- and a coverage-matched audit confirms these efficiency gains are not an artifact of under-coverage. We disclose one principal limitation: a volatility-specialized conformal-GARCH competitor remains more efficient on its home volatility-base series, though it does not transfer across domains.

</details>


### [416] [The Safety-Aware Denoiser for Text Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08116)
*Amman Yusuf,Zhejun Jiang,Mijung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出安全感知去噪器（SAD），一种用于文本扩散模型的推理时安全引导框架，通过在迭代去噪过程中将文本样本引导至可证明安全的区域，有效降低不安全文本生成，同时保持生成质量、多样性和流畅性，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本扩散模型的安全控制研究不足，而面向自回归模型的安全方法（事后过滤、推理时干预）无法有效应对文本扩散模型的安全风险。

Method: 提出安全感知去噪器（SAD），在迭代去噪过程中修改去噪方向，将最终样本引导至文本空间中可证明安全的区域。该方法为推理时方法，无需重新训练扩散模型，可灵活集成安全约束。

Result: 在危害分类、记忆化和越狱攻击等评估中，SAD显著降低不安全生成，同时保持生成质量、多样性和流畅性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 去噪过程中的安全引导为文本扩散模型提供了有效且可扩展的安全机制。

Abstract: Recent work on text diffusion models offers a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, but controlling their safety remains underexplored. Existing safety approaches are geared toward autoregressive models and typically rely on post-hoc filtering or inference-time interventions. These are inadequate for effectively addressing safety risks in text diffusion models. We propose the Safety-Aware Denoiser (SAD), a safety-guidance framework in text diffusion models. The SAD modifies the iterative denoising process such that the text sample at the final denoising step is steered toward provably safe regions of the text space. This inference-time method can integrate safety constraints into the denoiser, avoiding computationally expensive retraining of the underlying diffusion model and enabling flexible, lightweight safety guidance. We evaluate the safety of the generated text using the SAD, with respect to hazard taxonomy, memorization, and jailbreak. Experimental results show that SAD substantially reduces unsafe generations while preserving generation quality, diversity, and fluency, outperforming existing methods. These results demonstrate that our safety guidance during denoising provides an effective and scalable mechanism for enforcing safety in text diffusion models.

</details>


### [417] [COSMOS: Model-Agnostic Personalized Federated Learning with Clustered Server Models and Pseudo-Label-Only Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11165)
*Ben Rachmut,Luise Ge,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COSMOS 是一种模型无关的联邦学习框架，通过服务器端基于伪标签的聚类和蒸馏，同时处理客户端架构异构和数据异构，首次理论证明蒸馏可实现指数级个性化风险收缩，实验超越所有模型无关基线并与最先进个性化方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在异构环境中面临客户端模型架构差异和数据分布差异的双重挑战，现有方法（客户端聚类、知识蒸馏）难以同时处理这两种异构性。

Method: COSMOS 框架：客户端在公共数据上训练本地模型并生成伪标签；服务器根据预测相似性对客户端聚类，为每个簇训练专属模型，再将簇模型蒸馏回客户端。

Result: 实验在所有基准上一致优于所有模型无关的联邦学习基线，与最先进的个性化联邦学习方法竞争力相当；理论首次证明蒸馏可带来指数级个性化风险收缩。

Conclusion: 基于伪标签的服务器端个性化学习是一种有前景的范式，可在高度异构环境中实现可扩展且模型无关的联邦学习。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) in heterogeneous environments remains challenging because client models often differ in both architecture and data distribution. While recent approaches attempt to address this challenge through client clustering and knowledge distillation, simultaneously handling architectural and statistical heterogeneity remains difficult. We introduce COSMOS, a model-agnostic framework that enables server-side personalization using only pseudo-label communication. Clients train local models and predict on the public data; the server clusters clients by prediction similarity, trains a cluster-specific model for each group using its own compute, and distills the resulting models back to clients. We provide the first theoretical analysis showing that distillation from the learned cluster models can yield exponential personalization risk contraction, going beyond the convergence-to-stationarity guarantees typically provided in model-agnostic FL. Experiments across benchmarks demonstrate that COSMOS consistently outperforms all model-agnostic FL baselines while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art personalized FL methods. More broadly, our results highlight personalized server-side learning with pseudo-labels as a promising paradigm for scalable and model-agnostic federated learning in highly heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [418] [Strategic PAC Learnability via Geometric Definability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13426)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova,Nir Rosenfeld,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究策略分类中个体为影响分类器决策而修改特征的问题。作者证明即使VC维为1的简单假设类，在策略行为下也可能变成不可学习的无限VC维类。为解决此问题，引入几何可定义性假设（基于ℝ_exp的一阶公式），在此假设下学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由定义公式的复杂度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究策略分类中，个体策略性修改特征的行为如何影响假设类的样本复杂度，特别是揭示在哪些条件下学习性会被破坏，并寻找保证学习性保持的充分条件。

Method: 首先通过反例证明一般情形下策略行为可能导致VC维爆炸（即使原始类VC维为1）；然后引入几何可定义性假设，要求假设类和成本诱导的邻域关系均可由ℝ_exp上的一阶公式定义，在此框架下证明学习性保持定理。

Result: 存在VC维为1的假设类在简单区间邻域成本下诱导出无限VC维的类，即策略行为可使可学习问题变为不可学习。但在几何可定义性假设下，学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由定义公式的复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 策略行为可能严重破坏学习性，但通过几何可定义性这一结构假设（涵盖ℓ_p距离、Wasserstein距离、信息论散度等广泛场景），可以重新建立起学习性保证。

Abstract: Strategic classification studies learning settings in which individuals can modify their features, at a cost, in order to influence the classifier's decision. A central question is how the sample complexity of the induced (strategic) hypothesis class depends on the complexities of the underlying hypothesis class and the cost structure governing feasible manipulations. Prior work has shown that in several natural settings, such as linear classifiers with norm costs, the induced complexity can be controlled. We begin by showing that such guarantees fail in general - even in simple cases: there exist hypothesis classes of VC dimension $1$ on the real line such that, even under the simplest interval neighborhoods, the induced class has infinite VC dimension. Thus, strategic behavior can turn an easy learning problem into a non-learnable one. To overcome this, we introduce structure via a geometric definability assumption: both the hypothesis class and the cost-induced neighborhood relation can be defined by first-order formulas over $\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$. Intuitively, this means that hypotheses and costs can be described using arithmetic operations, exponentiation, logarithms, and comparisons. This captures a broad range of natural classes and cost functions, including $\ell_p$ distances, Wasserstein distance, and information-theoretic divergences. Under this assumption, we prove that learnability is preserved, with sample complexity controlled by the complexity of the defining formulas.

</details>


### [419] [A Theory of Training Profit-Optimal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16430)
*Sophie Hao,William Merrill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将缩放定律与微观经济学理论结合，构建了一个LLM训练厂商的经济模型，分析在计算受限和数据受限两种情况下，模型规模、训练token数量与利润之间的关系，并探讨了当前行业趋势是否符合利润最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知扩大LLM规模能提升模型质量，但质量提升能否转化为足够收入以抵消训练和推理成本并不明确，亟需一个经济理论来指导LLM训练的理性决策。

Method: 将缩放定律与微观经济学理论结合，建立LLM训练厂商的利润最大化模型，分别分析计算受限和数据受限两种情景下的最优模型规模、token预算与训练支出。

Result: 计算受限下，最优模型规模和token预算与硬件效率E近线性相关，训练成本呈次二次方增长；数据效率提升激励更大模型和更多支出。数据受限下，利润最优训练支出与D²/E成正比。当前行业趋势仅在计算受限情景下与模型一致，在数据受限或硬件进步停滞时并非利润最优。

Conclusion: 本文为利润最优的LLM训练提供了理论基础，可用于批判性审视行业声明并支持长期经济决策。

Abstract: Scaling LLMs requires tremendous computational resources, and recent advances in AI have gone hand in hand with massive amounts of capital expenditure. While it is established that scaling up LLMs reliably increases model quality (quantified in terms of loss or downstream evaluations), it is unclear how these quality improvements translate to potential revenue, and whether revenue increases would offset costs of larger-scale training and inference. In this work, we develop an economic model for characterizing the rational behavior of an LLM training firm by combining scaling laws with microeconomic theory. Under our model of firm behavior, LLM quality can be increased with more parameters and training tokens, leading to more potential adoption by consumers, who each have a quality threshold for using the LLM. On the other hand, additional parameters and training tokens both incur additional costs. We analyze the profit maximization problem for this model under compute-bound and data-bound regimes. In the compute-bound regime, optimal model size and token budget track hardware efficiency $E$ (FLOPs/\$) at a near-linear rate; total training cost then scales sub-quadratically in $E$. Data efficiency improvements incentivize larger models and training expenditure. When we are limited to $D$ data, profit-optimal training expenditure scales as $D^2/E$, i.e, increase with data and decreases with hardware efficiency (as well as data efficiency). Finally, we analyze practical trends in training expenditure: current trends are consistent with our most permissive model variants in the compute-bound regime, but are not profit-optimal in the data-bound regime or assuming hardware advances will stall. Overall, our results provide a theory of profit-optimal LLM training, providing a foundation for engaging critically with industry statements and supporting long-term economic decision making.

</details>


### [420] [Attacking the First-Principle: A Black-Box, Query-Free Targeted Mimicry Attack on Binary Function Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18231)
*Gabriel Sauger,Jean-Yves Marion,Sazzadur Rahaman,Victor Matrat,Vincent Tourneur,Muaz Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kelpie框架，能在黑盒、零查询条件下对二进制函数分类器执行模仿攻击（一种更强的定向逃逸攻击），通过保持恶意负载功能的代码变换实现误分类，实验证明对六种主流分类器有效，并进行了实际演示（键盘记录器和擦除器）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的二进制函数分类器易受对抗攻击，但以往方法多依赖查询目标分类器进行非定向逃逸攻击，缺乏在黑盒、零查询场景下实现强定向逃逸攻击（模仿攻击）的有效方案，因此需要探索更隐蔽、更具威胁的攻击方式以揭示现有分类器的安全漏洞。

Method: Kelpie框架：在零查询黑盒设定下，利用保持功能的代码变换技术修改恶意载荷，使其被目标二进制函数分类器误分类为指定类别（模仿攻击），无需与目标分类器直接交互。

Result: Kelpie成功对六种不同架构的先进二进制函数分类器执行了模仿攻击；在实际演示中，将键盘记录器和擦除器隐藏在良性函数中嵌入应用，实现了有效逃逸。

Conclusion: 这是首次在黑盒、零查询上下文中展示模仿攻击的工作，揭示了现有基于机器学习的二进制函数分类器的可靠性问题，对其安全性提出了重要质疑。

Abstract: Binary function classifiers play a crucial role in maintaining the security and integrity of software systems by detecting malicious code and unauthorized modifications. However, machine learning-based classifiers are vulnerable to adversarial attacks that can evade detection. In this study, we present Kelpie, a novel framework for executing mimicry attacks, a stronger type of targeted evasion attacks, on binary function classifiers in a black-box, zero-query setting. Unlike previous approaches that rely on querying the target classifier to refine untargeted evasion attacks, Kelpie leverages code transformations that preserve the functionality of malicious payloads while causing them to be misclassified as we want. Through extensive experimentation, we demonstrate that Kelpie can successfully execute mimicry attacks against six state-of-the-art binary function classifiers representing different model architectures without requiring direct interaction with them. We further validate our approach with a practical demonstration, involving a keylogger and a wiper concealed within benign-looking functions embedded in an application. This work, to our best knowledge, is the first to demonstrate such a mimicry attack in a black-box, zero-query context, raising important questions about the reliability and security of existing machine learning-based binary function classifiers.

</details>


### [421] [Multi-Token Residual Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18817)
*Yufeng Xu,Zishuo Bao,Qian Wang,Zeshen Zhang,Haoqi Zhang,Bowen Peng,Ang Li,Rahul Chalamala,Yucheng Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多Token残差预测（MRP），一种轻量级模块，利用扩散语言模型相邻去噪步骤中logit分布的相似性，通过从骨干网络隐藏状态预测残差来实现单次前向传播中依赖感知的多Token去噪。在静态去噪场景中，MRP作为投机解码的草稿模型实现无损加速（SGLang中最高1.4x）；在动态去噪场景中，MRP驱动重新掩码方案以撤销过早的Token揭示，在HumanEval上提升22.6个点，在GSM8K上提升17.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在逐迭代去噪掩码Token序列时，每步解码的Token数量由置信度阈值控制，且随着每步去噪更多Token，质量单调下降。现有方法缺乏对多Token去噪间依赖关系的有效建模，导致效率与质量难以兼顾。

Method: MRP利用相邻去噪步骤logit分布高度相似这一关键性质，从骨干网络隐藏状态直接预测步骤间的残差，从而在不进行第二次骨干前向传播的情况下获得下一步logit，实现单次前向传播中更多Token的去噪。在静态去噪中，MRP作为投机解码的草稿模型，其提案经骨干验证实现无损加速；在动态去噪中，MRP驱动重新掩码机制撤销过度激进的Token揭示。

Result: 在静态去噪（高质量低吞吐）场景中，MRP在SGLang上实现最高1.4倍无损加速；在动态去噪（低质量高吞吐）场景中，HumanEval代码生成任务提升22.6个点，GSM8K推理任务提升17.7个点。

Conclusion: MRP作为一种轻量级且通用的模块，能够在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升扩散语言模型的解码效率，并通过重新掩码方案有效弥补因低阈值解码造成的精度损失。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked token sequences, offering a tradeoff between parallelism and quality compared to autoregressive models. In current practice, the number of tokens decoded per step is controlled by a confidence threshold, and quality degrades monotonically as more tokens are denoised per step. We introduce Multi-token Residual Prediction (MRP), a lightweight module that enables dependency-aware multi-token denoising within a single backbone forward pass. MRP exploits a key property of the denoising process: the logit distributions at adjacent denoising steps are remarkably similar. Rather than running the backbone a second time to obtain the next-step logits, MRP predicts the residual between steps from the backbone's hidden states, effectively denoising more tokens per backbone forward at a fraction of the cost. We apply MRP across the two operating regimes of DLM decoding. In the high-quality-low-throughput static denoising regime, MRP serves as a drafter for speculative decoding: its proposals are verified against the backbone, yielding lossless acceleration of up to 1.4x in SGLang. In the low-quality-high-throughput dynamic denoising regime, MRP instead drives a remasking scheme that revokes over-eager reveals, recovering most of the accuracy lost to aggressive low-threshold decoding and improving accuracy by up to 22.6 points on code generation task HumanEval and 17.7 points on reasoning task GSM8K.

</details>


### [422] [ShapeBench: A Scalable Benchmark and Diagnostic Suite for Standardized Evaluation in Aerodynamic Shape Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20763)
*Shaghayegh Fazliani,Krissh Chawla,Jack Guo,Yiren Shen,Matthias Ihme,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ShapeBench，一个包含103个任务、8个形状类别、统一API的开源气动形状优化基准，提供验证过的代理模型和高保真CFD验证流程，并引入ShapeEvolve基线。实验发现优化器排名在不同任务间高度不一致（平均斯皮尔曼ρ=0.013），且基准远未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动形状优化（ASO）的快速发展缺乏标准化的评估框架，现有比较难以公平进行，需要涵盖不同形状类别、目标函数和预算匹配基线的统一基准。

Method: 构建包含103个任务、8个形状类别的统一基准ShapeBench，提供统一API、验证过的代理模型用于快速搜索、高保真CFD流程用于最终验证，引入领域特定的进化LLM基线ShapeEvolve，并采用一致的预算度量进行公平比较。

Result: 优化器排名在不同形状类别和问题公式间差异显著，平均成对斯皮尔曼ρ=0.013，单任务结论不能泛化；基准远未饱和，经典方法难以跨所有类别适用。

Conclusion: 需要更通用的气动形状优化方法，ShapeBench为公平比较和系统性评估提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: Rapid progress in aerodynamic shape optimization (ASO) has outpaced currently-available standardized evaluation frameworks. Fair comparison requires a unified benchmark spanning diverse shape classes, objective formulations, and matched-budget state-of-the-art baselines. We introduce ShapeBench, an open-source ASO benchmark with a unified API spanning 103 tasks across eight shape categories and multiple optimization regimes. Each ShapeBench task includes a validated surrogate for fast search; when feasible, a high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) pipeline for final verification is available, enabling systematic fidelity-gap analysis. ShapeBench provides a reproducible protocol with well-configured baselines to compare fairly using a consistent budget metric, allowing for comparison among both classical and LLM-driven methods, including general-purpose optimizers and a new domain-specialized evolutionary LLM baseline, ShapeEvolve. Results on ShapeBench demonstrate substantial variance in optimizer rankings across shape categories and problem formulations, with mean pairwise Spearman $ρ= 0.013$, so single-task conclusions do not reliably generalize across problem classes. The benchmark is also far from saturation; classical methods are rarely applicable across all shape categories and tasks, further highlighting the need for more general-purpose approaches.

</details>


### [423] [Transformer Field Theory: A Response-Theoretic Approach to Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25225)
*David N. Olivieri,Antonio F. Pérez Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer场论，将残差流视为深度和位置上的场，把激活修补形式化为局部源插入，并用格林函数描述下游传播。实验表明该方法在GPT-2类模型中存在有界线性区域，一阶灵敏度可预测修补效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为机械可解释性中的激活修补、因果追踪等方法提供统一的数学理论基础，将修补操作从经验性方法提升为有严格数学框架的场论形式。

Method: 将残差流视作关于层深度和token位置的场，修补等价于局部源插入，用一阶灵敏度场预测修补效应，格林函数描述下游传播，修补选择转化为伴随逆问题。

Result: 局部干预存在有界线性区域；一阶灵敏度可预测跨层-位置修补效果；局部源产生各向异性的场传播；高灵敏度位置和切片格林算子可降维描述响应。

Conclusion: 灵敏度、Transformer场响应和切片格林算子是组织修补实验的实用工具，为修补位置推断和跨尺度响应迁移提供了前向数学基础。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability often studies Transformer behavior by intervening on internal activations through activation patching, causal tracing, path patching, and steering directions. This paper develops Transformer Field Theory: a response-theoretic framework in which the residual stream of a fixed forward pass is treated as a Transformer field over layer depth and token position. In this formulation, patching becomes a localized source insertion into the Transformer field, first-order sensitivity fields predict patch effects, Green functions describe downstream propagation, and patch selection is posed as an adjoint inverse problem. Empirically, we test the theory's forward response objects in GPT-2-style autoregressive Transformers. Localized Transformer-field interventions exhibit a bounded local linear regime; first-order sensitivities predict patch effects across layer-token sites; localized sources generate structured anisotropic Transformer-field propagation; high-sensitivity sites and sliced Green operators provide reduced response descriptions; and prompt-induced Transformer-field displacements partially transfer answer behavior. These results establish sensitivities, Transformer-field responses, and sliced Green operators as practical objects for organizing patching experiments, while providing the forward mathematical basis for patch-site inference and cross-scale response transfer.

</details>


### [424] [Universal Time Series Generation with Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28507)
*Torben Berndt,Elyes Farjallah,Leif Seute,Raeid Saqur,Benjamin Walker,Jan Stühmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了结构化线性控制微分方程（SLiCEs）是通用时间序列生成器，并提出了生成式SLiCEs（G-SLiCEs），一个用于路径空间流匹配的最大表达能力连续时间模型，在概率预测等任务中表现优异，尤其适用于不规则采样网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型（SSMs）在序列通用性方面的工作主要集中在判别式设置下，本文将其扩展到生成式时间序列建模，旨在证明连续时间模型同样能够成为通用时间序列生成器。

Method: 1. 理论证明：证明最大表达能力的结构化线性控制微分方程（SLiCEs）是通用时间序列生成器，能够逼近紧致潜集上连续因果前推出的路径律。2. 模型构建：提出G-SLiCEs，一个用于路径空间流匹配的最大表达能力连续时间生成模型。

Result: G-SLiCEs在概率预测及下游任务中性能优于现有方法，同时保留了连续时间模型的优势，如能够泛化到任意观测网格，尤其在不规则网格上表现突出。

Conclusion: 表达能力更强的连续时间模型在生成式时间序列建模中具有理论保证和实际优势，尤其是在不规则采样场景下。

Abstract: Recent work on the sequence universality of State Space Models (SSMs) has introduced efficient, maximally expressive continuous-time approaches for time-series modelling. While these works focus on discriminative settings, we extend this perspective to generative time-series modelling by proving that maximally expressive Structured Linear Controlled Differential Equations (SLiCEs) are universal time-series generators, in the sense that they can approximate the induced path laws of continuous causal pushforwards on compact latent sets in $W_\infty$. Building on these theoretical results, we propose Generative SLiCEs (G-SLiCEs), a maximally expressive continuous-time model for flow matching on path-space. Empirically, we show that expressivity improves performance in probabilistic forecasting and downstream tasks, while retaining the advantages of continuous-time models such as generalising to arbitrary observation grids. This is particularly beneficial for irregular grids, where fixed-grid models often struggle.

</details>


### [425] [Plan, Don't Pose: Long Composite Motion Generation with Text-Aligned BFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29906)
*Nikolay Shvetsov,Maksim Bobrin,Nazar Buzun,Anton Bozhedarov,Dmitry V. Dylov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Text2BFM提出了首个将自然语言与预训练行为基础模型（BFM）对齐的文本到动作生成框架，通过解耦语义规划与运动执行，利用冻结BFM的潜在策略空间实现高效、鲁棒的长序列和组合式文本描述驱动的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成方法通常让单一模型同时处理语义理解、长程结构规划和底层物理实现，导致模型成本高昂且在处理长序列、组合式或语义密集的文本提示时不可靠。作者希望解耦语义规划与运动执行，利用预训练的行为基础模型作为可执行的动作先验，从而降低端到端生成模型的负担。

Method: Text2BFM在冻结的BFM潜在策略空间中操作，将其作为可执行动作先验；设计了一个文本对齐的变分行为瓶颈（variational behavioral bottleneck），将BFM策略潜在序列压缩为与语言兼容的紧凑运动表示，保留长程行为结构；使用轻量级条件生成器在该紧凑行为流形中生成，然后将生成的潜在编码行为解码为策略潜在向量，驱动预训练的冻结BFM生成最终动作。

Result: Text2BFM在长序列和组合式文本描述的动作生成任务上实现了高效、鲁棒的性能，无需依赖沉重的端到端运动生成器。

Conclusion: 通过将语义规划与运动执行解耦，Text2BFM在保持效率的同时提升了动作生成的质量和鲁棒性，尤其适用于长文本和组合性强的复杂描述场景。

Abstract: Text-to-motion (T2M) generation has broad applications in character animation, virtual avatars, and human-robot interaction. Existing methods typically generate pose trajectories or motion tokens directly from language, forcing a single model to handle semantic interpretation, long-horizon structure, and low-level physical realization. This coupling makes them costly and often unreliable for long, compositional, or semantically dense prompts. We propose Text2BFM, the first framework that aligns natural language with pretrained Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) for T2M generation without relying on heavy end-to-end motion generators. Text2BFM operates in the latent policy space of a frozen BFM, using it as an executable motion prior. A text-aligned variational behavioral bottleneck compresses BFM policy-latent sequences into compact motion representations that are compatible with language and preserve long-horizon behavioral structure. Generation is performed in this compact behavioral manifold with a lightweight conditional generator, and the resulting latent encoded behaviors are decoded into policy latents that drive the pretrained frozen BFM. By decoupling semantic planning from motion execution, Text2BFM achieves efficient, robust T2M generation and strong performance on long, compositional textual descriptions.

</details>


### [426] [Revisiting Neural Processes via Fourier Transform and Volterra Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01172)
*Peiman Mohseni,Nick Duffield,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Volterra展开和集合傅里叶卷积（SFConvs）的平移等变神经过程，解决了现有方法可解释性差、受限于密集均匀网格或二次方复杂度的问题，实现了线性复杂度、全局感受野且适用于不规则采样数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平移等变神经过程存在两个局限：(i) 堆叠带非线性的通用组件，模糊了诱导函数类，限制了可解释性；(ii) 卷积设计依赖局部感受野的核并需要密集均匀输入网格，而基于注意力的方法虽避免了这些问题但计算复杂度随观测数量呈二次方增长。

Method: (1) 利用Volterra展开将连续平移等变算子表征为高阶卷积的和，提供分析透明度，同时允许通过一阶卷积高效近似；(2) 提出集合傅里叶卷积（SFConvs），一种直接在非均匀采样点上操作的频域参数化方法，实现近似全局感受野和线性复杂度；(3) 基于此提出两种条件神经过程：SFConvCNPs（堆叠SFConv块加非线性）和SFVConvCNPs（融合Volterra公式）。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，所提方法优于现有最先进的基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过Volterra展开和频域参数化，成功构建了兼具可解释性、高效计算（线性复杂度、全局感受野）且适用于不规则采样数据的平移等变神经过程模型。

Abstract: Modeling unknown latent functions from finite, irregularly sampled measurements is a recurring challenge across science and engineering. Neural processes (NPs), a family of probabilistic functional models, are promising solutions -- especially when endowed with domain-specific symmetries like translation equivariance, which improve sample efficiency and generalization. Yet existing translation-equivariant NPs face two limitations: (i) they stack generic components with non-linearities, obscuring the induced function class and limiting interpretability; and (ii) convolutional designs rely on kernels with local receptive fields and require dense uniform input grids, while attention-based methods avoid these issues but scale quadratically with the number of observations. We address both with two contributions. First, using the Volterra expansion, we characterize continuous translation-equivariant operators as sums of higher-order convolutions, yielding analytical transparency while admitting efficient approximation by first-order convolutions. Second, we introduce set Fourier convolutions (SFConvs), a frequency-domain parameterization that operates directly on irregularly sampled points, achieves approximately global receptive fields, and scales linearly in the number of observations. Building on these ideas, we propose two conditional NPs (CNPs): SFConvCNPs, which stack SFConv blocks with non-linearities, and SFVConvCNPs, which integrate the Volterra formulation. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate our methods' efficacy against state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [427] [Realistic noise synthesis reduces bias and improves tissue microstructure estimation with supervised machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02044)
*Bradley G. Karat,Maëliss Jallais,Ali R. Khan,Santiago Aja-Fernández,Jelle Veraart,Marco Palombo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种真实噪声合成(RNS)框架，通过在模拟训练数据中同时建模Rician期望和有效后处理噪声方差，解决了扩散MRI微观结构参数估计中因训练与推理数据噪声特性不匹配导致的协变量偏移问题，显著降低了低SNR条件下的参数估计偏差并提高了精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散MRI微观结构参数估计受噪声影响严重，监督机器学习框架使用模拟数据训练时，模拟信号与采集信号的噪声特性存在差异（协变量偏移），导致参数估计出现系统性偏差，尤其是在低SNR条件下。

Method: 提出真实噪声合成(RNS)框架，在模拟训练信号中融入：(1) 使用MPPCA估计的噪声标准差建模Rician期望；(2) 从球谐残差中导出的有效后处理噪声方差。方法在cylinder-zeppelin和SANDI模型上，跨多个SNR水平的模拟数据集及重复采集的在体扩散数据上进行了评估。

Result: 忽略幅度诱导噪声效应会产生系统性、SNR依赖的参数偏差；融入Rician期望可显著降低偏差至与噪声感知非线性最小二乘拟合相当的水平；进一步建模有效标准差可提升精度；性能对回归架构不敏感，但对噪声估计精度敏感。

Conclusion: 在模拟训练数据中进行真实噪声建模能够缓解信号域的协变量偏移，对于实现无偏的监督微观结构估计至关重要，特别是在高b值或高空间分辨率导致的低SNR场景中。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI enables non-invasive probing of tissue microstructure, but accurate parameter estimation is challenged by noise-related effects. In supervised machine learning frameworks trained on simulated data, discrepancies between the noise characteristics of simulated and acquired signals introduce a form of covariate shift, whereby the input signal distribution differs between training and inference. We investigated the impact of this mismatch on microstructure parameter estimation and propose a realistic noise synthesis (RNS) framework to mitigate it. RNS incorporates both the Rician expectation and the effective post-processing noise variance into simulated training signals. The Rician expectation was modelled using a noise standard deviation estimated with MPPCA, while the effective standard deviation was derived from spherical harmonic residuals of preprocessed data. The method was evaluated using the cylinder-zeppelin and the SANDI models on simulated datasets across multiple SNR levels and on in vivo diffusion data with repeated acquisitions. Sensitivity to noise misestimation was also assessed. Ignoring magnitude-induced noise effects during training produced systematic, SNR-dependent parameter bias, particularly at low SNR. Incorporating the Rician expectation substantially reduced bias to the level of noise-aware nonlinear least-squares fitting. Modelling the effective standard deviation further improved precision. Performance was largely independent of regression architecture but sensitive to accurate noise estimation. These findings demonstrate that realistic noise modelling in simulated training data mitigates signal-domain covariate shift and is essential for unbiased supervised microstructure estimation, particularly in low-SNR regimes associated with high b-values or high spatial resolution.

</details>


### [428] [Variational Learning for Insertion-based Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02133)
*Yangtian Zhang,Zhe Wang,Arthur Gretton,Rex Ying,David van Dijk,Michalis K. Titsias,Jiaxin Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出插入过程（IP），一种通过排列变分推断联合学习插入位置、内容和终止条件的随机生成模型，支持可变长度生成和自适应插入顺序，在目标条件规划和分子字符串生成任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非单调序列生成方法（如掩码扩散模型）是顺序无关的且依赖固定长度网格，无法支持可变长度生成和自适应插入顺序。

Method: 建立插入轨迹与排列之间的双射对应关系，基于此提出插入过程（IP），通过排列变分推断联合学习插入位置、内容和终止条件。

Result: 在目标条件规划和分子字符串生成任务上，学习插入顺序提高了建模质量和泛化能力。

Conclusion: IP原生支持可变长度生成，能学习数据驱动的插入顺序偏好，在缺乏规范从左到右结构的领域中表现更优。

Abstract: Non-monotonic sequence generation methods, such as masked diffusion models, provide a flexible alternative to left-to-right autoregressive modeling by allowing tokens to be generated in non-fixed and prescribed orders. Despite their practical advantages, most existing non-monotonic models are order-agnostic and rely on a fixed-length grid, limiting their ability to support variable-length generation and adaptive insertion order. In this work, we introduce a probabilistic framework for learning insertion order in variable-length insertion models. We formalize a bijective correspondence between insertion trajectories and permutations, which enables an exact reparameterization of the data likelihood as a sum over permutations. Building on this result, we propose the Insertion Process (IP), a stochastic generative model that jointly learns where to insert, what to insert, and when to terminate, trained via permutation-based variational inference. Unlike prior fixed-canvas approaches, IP natively supports variable-length generation and learns data-driven preferences over insertion orders. Experiments on goal-conditioned planning and molecular string generation demonstrate that learning insertion order improves both modeling quality and generalization in domains without a canonical left-to-right structure.

</details>


### [429] [Benchmarking Counterfactual Prediction in Epidemic Time Series with Time-Varying Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05692)
*Wenhao Mu,Facundo Yan,Anik Mumssen,Marisa Eisenberg,Alexander Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模流行病时间序列反事实预测基准，基于校准的智能体模型生成真实反事实轨迹，支持静态/时变治疗和单/多策略干预设置，评估了多种因果推断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推断基准存在两难：真实数据缺乏反事实真值，简化模拟无法捕捉复杂因果动态，亟需一个同时具备真实性和可观测反事实的基准。

Method: 基于真实人口、流动性、流行病学和政策数据，校准智能体模型（ABM），为美国150多个县生成反事实轨迹；支持静态与时变治疗、单策略与多策略干预设置。

Result: 评估了广泛使用和最先进的因果推断方法，揭示了显著的性能差异，突显了现实时间序列因果推理的挑战性。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了现有时间序列因果推断评估的空白，为动态干预下的反事实预测提供了更真实的测试平台。

Abstract: Deep learning has enabled significant advances in time-series causal inference, yet progress remains constrained by the lack of realistic benchmarks with observable counterfactual outcomes. Existing datasets either rely on real-world observations without ground-truth counterfactuals or on simplified simulations that fail to capture complex causal dynamics. To address this gap, we develop a large-scale benchmark for counterfactual prediction in epidemic time series under dynamic interventions. Unlike existing benchmarks, it supports static and time-varying treatments, as well as both single-policy and multi-policy intervention settings, enabling evaluation of causal inference methods across a broad range of causal inference scenarios. Leveraging a calibrated agent-based model grounded in real-world demographic, mobility, epidemiological, and policy data, we generate realistic counterfactual trajectories across more than 150 U.S. counties. Using this benchmark, we evaluate widely used and state-of-the-art causal inference methods, revealing substantial performance differences and highlighting the challenges of realistic time-series causal reasoning.

</details>


### [430] [GenAutoML: An Agentic Framework for Dynamic Architecture Generation and Optimization in Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05860)
*Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil,Cédric Schockaert,Abdulrahman Nahhas,Christian Daase,Mursal Dawodi,Jawid Ahmad Baktash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenAutoML是一个利用大语言模型作为神经架构师，通过智能体框架自动生成轻量级、任务特定神经网络架构（如WaveInterferenceNet）的自动化机器学习系统，在时序预测和异常检测任务上实现了高效能与低延迟，适合边缘AI部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AutoML依赖静态预定义的搜索空间，难以适应多样化数据特征；同时手动设计时序预测和异常检测的神经网络架构需要大量领域专业知识和资源，效率低下。

Method: 提出GenAutoML框架，核心包括：(1) 利用LLM作为神经架构师，将自然语言需求转化为可执行的PyTorch代码；(2) 沙箱反射循环（Sandboxed Reflection Loop）实现自主代码优化；(3) 签名感知运行时（Signature-Aware Runtime）确保架构一致性和执行安全；(4) 动态可逆实例归一化（Dyn-RevIN）增强非平稳条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTm1和Weather基准上，GenAutoML能动态生成适配数据集特性的任务特定架构；生成的WaveInterferenceNet推理延迟低于0.01毫秒/样本，同时保持有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: GenAutoML通过强调计算效率、架构自适应性和稳定优化行为，实现了超轻量级神经网络的自动化生成，适用于资源受限和延迟敏感的边缘AI场景。

Abstract: Designing neural architectures for time-series forecasting and anomaly detection remains a resource-intensive task that often requires substantial domain expertise. Traditional Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) systems typically rely on static, predefined search spaces, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse data characteristics. We present GenAutoML, an agentic framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as neural architects to bridge natural-language requirements and executable PyTorch implementations. The framework incorporates a Sandboxed Reflection Loop for autonomous code refinement and a Signature-Aware Runtime that enforces architectural consistency and execution safety. To improve robustness under non-stationary conditions, we further introduce a Dynamic Reversible Instance Normalization (Dyn-RevIN) wrapper. Experiments on the ETTh1, ETTm1, and Weather benchmarks demonstrate that GenAutoML can dynamically generate task-specific neural architectures tailored to dataset characteristics. Among the generated models, WaveInterferenceNet achieves inference latency below 0.01 ms per sample while maintaining competitive predictive performance. By emphasizing computational efficiency, architectural adaptability, and stable optimization behavior, GenAutoML enables the creation of ultra-lightweight neural networks suitable for resource-constrained and latency-sensitive Edge AI deployments.

</details>


### [431] [A Unifying Lens on Reward Uncertainty in RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09073)
*Ely Hahami,Yoel Zimmermann,Ray Zhou,Jack Benarroch Jedlicki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出RLHF受奖励破解瓶颈制约，提出分布性奖励模型 $p(r|x,y)$，并从贝叶斯推断和KL-DRO角度推导出闭合形式的有效奖励 $\tilde r(x,y) = \pmβ\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/β}]$，统一了悲观聚合中的均值聚合、最坏情况优化和不确定性加权优化等启发式方法，阐明了各自的隐含假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中奖励模型（RM）存在奖励破解问题，即策略利用RM的误差获得高奖励分数但无真实质量提升。现有降低不确定区域奖励的悲观方法缺乏原则性不确定性度量，因为标准标量RM无法提供不确定性信息。

Method: 引入分布性奖励模型 $p(r|x,y)$，通过贝叶斯推断或KL-DRO视角，推导出KL正则化RLHF目标的有效奖励闭合形式 $\tilde r(x,y) = \pmβ\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/β}]$，并分析其悲观分支与现有启发式方法的关系。

Result: 均值聚合、最坏情况优化和不确定性加权优化等现有RM集成启发式方法均是该闭合表达式的极限或截断形式，统一了不同的悲观聚合策略。

Conclusion: 该工作为RLHF中的悲观主义提供了理论统一视角，明确了各现有方法的隐含假设，为设计更鲁棒的RLHF方法提供了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) is bottlenecked by reward hacking, where the policy exploits errors in a proxy reward model (RM) and produces high RM scores without genuine quality gains. A natural mitigation is pessimism: lowering rewards in regions where the RM is uncertain. However, standard scalar RMs provide no principled notion of uncertainty. We argue that the right object is a distributional reward model $p(r\mid x,y)$. Under either a Bayesian inference or a KL-distributionally robust optimization (KL-DRO) lens, the KL-regularized RLHF objective admits a closed-form effective reward $\tilde r(x,y) = \pmβ\log\mathbb{E}_p[e^{\pm r/β}]$. The pessimistic branch unifies the prior heuristics for RM ensemble aggregation: mean aggregation, worst-case optimization (WCO), and uncertainty-weighted optimization (UWO) all emerge as limits or truncations of this single expression. This also clarifies the implicit assumptions of each existing rule.

</details>


### [432] [Using Seismic Statistical Features and VQ-VAE to Improve Spatiotemporal Seismicity Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10069)
*Wei Quan,Denise Gorse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在先前基于XGBoost和60个地震统计特征（SSFs）的研究基础上，进行了两项关键扩展：（1）从全区域预测转为局部化预测（半径24 km），性能依然优秀；（2）引入基于VQ-VAE自编码器从二维地震图提取的新特征，该特征不仅提升了预测性能，还几乎完全替代了传统b值的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有研究证明60个地震统计特征（SSFs）优于428个通用时间序列特征的基础上，进一步探索局部化预测的可行性，以及引入基于二维地震图的深度学习特征来增强预测能力。

Method: 使用日本地震目录数据，采用XGBoost模型进行局部化预测（半径24 km区域）；训练VQ-VAE模型从二维地震图中提取特征，并通过SHAP分析评估特征重要性。

Result: 局部化预测的测试AUC值与先前全区域研究相当；VQ-VAE衍生特征在SHAP分析中排名最高，能够提升预测性能，且几乎完全替代了传统b值的功能。

Conclusion: 将一维目录数据的地震统计特征与二维地震图的VQ-VAE特征相结合，能够实现有效的局部化地震预测，且深度学习特征有助于替代传统物理统计特征。

Abstract: In this paper we build upon a previous study in which we demonstrated, using XGBoost and earthquake catalogue data from Japan and Chile, that a set of 60 seismic statistical features (SSFs) had much greater predictive value than a set of 428 generic time series features from the tsfresh package. We here extend this previous work in two key ways, focusing on data from Japan as a large dataset is necessary in order to allow for the training of a deep learning (autoencoder) model. First, we move from whole-region prediction (considering, for each candidate event, the likelihood of an event M $\geq$ 5.0 anywhere in the region in the next 15 days) to localised predictions in which both the region of feature computation and the region of prediction are restricted to a circle of radius 24 km around the candidate event, and we show that performance remains excellent, similar to our previous whole-region study for the same area. Second, we here couple this proven set of SSFs, based on one-dimensional (catalogue) data, with a novel feature based on two-dimensional seismic maps, obtained by training a VQ-VAE model to reproduce such maps as output and identifying a measure of its error in doing so with a localised build-up of crustal stress. We show that while localised prediction based on SSFs can be effective alone, with test AUC values as high as those obtained in the case of Japan in our previous whole-region study, the inclusion of the new natively-spatial VQ-VAE-derived feature, top-ranked by SHAP analysis, can enhance performance and additionally appears to near-wholly replace the traditionally-computed $b$-value in terms of feature usage.

</details>


### [433] [PhysMetrics.Weather: An Evaluation Framework for Physical Consistency in ML Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10642)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Timo Maier,Axel Lauer,Veronika Eyring,Pierre Gentine,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhysMetrics.Weather评估框架，通过守恒、频谱和动力学三类指标量化MLWP模型的物理真实性，以弥补纯数据驱动模型缺乏物理约束的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLWP模型主要依赖数据驱动，且仅用像素级误差指标（如RMSE）评估，无法保证预测结果符合已知物理规律，缺乏对模型物理真实性的量化评估手段。

Method: 构建PhysMetrics.Weather评估框架，包含三类物理指标：守恒指标（检查物理量守恒性）、频谱指标（分析空间尺度特征）、动力学指标（评估动力过程一致性）。

Result: 该框架能够量化MLWP模型的物理真实性，为物理信息架构的开发提供指导，并帮助判断模型是否适用于实际业务。

Conclusion: PhysMetrics.Weather填补了MLWP模型物理评估的空白，通过多维物理指标推动更可靠、物理一致的天气预报模型发展。

Abstract: Machine learning weather prediction (MLWP) models have achieved impressive forecasting performance at a small fraction of the computational costs required for traditional physics-based methods. However, they are primarily (1) data-driven and (2) evaluated using pixel-wide error metrics (e.g., RMSE), so there are no guarantees that their forecasts are consistent with known physical laws. We introduce PhysMetrics$.$Weather, an evaluation framework that assesses the physical realism of MLWP models across three types of metrics: conservation, spectral, and dynamical. By quantifying physical realism, this tool guides the development of physics-informed architectures and helps evaluate whether MLWP models are reliable for operational use. Our framework is available on Github at https://github.com/Emmakast/PhysMetrics.Weather.

</details>


### [434] [One Step Closer to Ground Truth: A Multi-Scale Residual-Aware Representation Learning Pipeline for Predicting Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10678)
*Amrijit Biswas,Mustafa Kamal,Robin Krambroeckers,M. M. Lutfe Elahi,Sifat Momen,Nabeel Mohammed,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段、模型无关的框架，将时间序列预测与残差学习显式解耦：基础Transformer首先生成初始预测，然后一个专门的元校正器动态建模跨变量的结构化误差模式，迭代修正残差偏差，在八个基准数据集上实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段Transformer预测模型存在系统性残差偏差，来源于结构差异、未建模随机成分或多尺度时序表示不足，且传统方法将残差视为不可约噪声，无法自适应校正结构化误差模式。

Method: 提出两阶段、模型无关框架：第一阶段基础Transformer生成初始预测；第二阶段元校正器（meta-corrector）动态建模跨多元通道的结构化误差模式，保持变量间依赖关系，迭代修正基础Transformer的残差偏差。该框架通过假设空间扩展（hypothesis space expansion）形式化，克服单阶段架构的近似限制。

Result: 在八个流行基准数据集上采用既定协议评估，实现了最先进性能，MSE和MAE指标显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架能够缓解系统性偏差，增强对复杂时序动态的鲁棒性，提升了基于Transformer的预测模型的实用适用性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have emerged as leading paradigms in time-series forecasting in recent years, employing self-attention mechanisms to capture long-range dependencies. Despite their success, these single-stage forecasting architectures exhibit persistent systematic residual biases arising from structural discrepancies, unmodeled stochastic components, or inadequate multi-scale temporal representations. This limitation persists when residuals are treated as irreducible noise, precluding adaptive correction of structured error patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce a two-stage, model-agnostic framework that explicitly decouples forecasting and residual learning into distinct stages of representation learning. A base transformer first generates the initial predictions. Subsequently, a dedicated meta-corrector dynamically models structured error patterns across multivariate channels, preserves cross-variable dependencies, and iteratively refines the residual bias of the base transformer. By formalizing this pipeline as a hypothesis space expansion, our framework addresses approximation limitations inherent in single-stage architectures, removes reliance on restrictive assumptions, and enables end-to-end learning of complex error dynamics. Evaluated on eight popular benchmark datasets using established protocols, our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, with significant improvements in standard metrics (MSE, MAE). The results demonstrate the framework's ability to mitigate systematic biases and enhance robustness to complex temporal dynamics, advancing the practical applicability of transformer-based forecasting models.

</details>


### [435] [When to Align, When to Predict: A Phase Diagram for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11190)
*Ilay Kamai,Hugues Van Assel,Aviv Regev,Hagai B. Perets,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的线性框架，系统分析了跨模态对齐（CA）和跨模态预测（CP）两种多模态表示学习范式的成功与失败条件。通过信号加噪声模型，推导出两种目标的分离比，揭示了它们互补的失败模式，并构建了一个四区域相图（Both、CA only、CP only、Neither）。实验在合成数据、立体视觉、图像-文本对和天体物理数据上验证了理论预测，包括跨模态训练有害的“Neither”区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对跨模态对齐（CA）和跨模态预测（CP）何时有效、何时失败的系统性理解，导致在科学领域（如生物医学、天体物理学）中，从业者无法诊断为何标准多模态方法不如最佳单模态。

Method: 开发了一个统一的线性框架，采用尖峰信号加噪声模型（spiked signal-plus-noise model），引入结构化的跨模态 nuisance 相关性，推导出两种目标的分离比，揭示其互补失败模式；并设计了数据驱动流程，利用少量带标签子样本将真实数据集定位到相图中。

Result: 成功将多模态问题划分为四个区域（Both、CA only、CP only、Neither），在合成数据、立体视觉基准、图像-文本对和真实天体物理数据上验证了理论预测，包括跨模态训练有害的“Neither”区域。

Conclusion: 本文框架允许从业者在训练前诊断多模态问题并选择正确目标，避免无效或有害的跨模态训练，对科学领域（如生物医学、天体物理学）具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment (CA) and cross-modal prediction (CP) are the dominant paradigms for multimodal representation learning, yet there is no systematic understanding of when each succeeds, when each fails, and when cross-modal training helps at all -- a gap that leaves practitioners, especially in scientific domains like biomedicine or astrophysics, with heterogeneous instruments and multiple levels of organization and measurement, unable to diagnose why standard methods underperform the best single modality. We develop a unified linear framework that addresses both questions.
